### Revision 50 B0 Source Gate / PP-OCR detector-support amendment

Historical record only. Every Revision 50 custody, corpus, artifact, authorization, resume, execution, and completion directive below is non-normative for Revision 51 except the five inherited production-schema keys named by the R51 machine contract.

This amendment supersedes Revision 49 only for G004/B0. Revision 49 B0 `31f58b85cfa723c6e9bb0910e5402059d9db7184`, its corpus, artifact, logs, diagnostics, and failed authorization remain immutable non-authorizing evidence.

1. The PP wrapper retains every raw `RotatedRect` before quantization as the eight source-scaled `f32::to_bits` coordinates returned in exact `RotatedRect::corners()` order. It also retains canonical `Vec<Vec<RotatedRect>>` line membership and the positionally corresponding `Vec<Option<TextLine>>`, including every `None`. Occurrence indices disambiguate duplicate rectangles; the validator proves the flattened canonical occurrence multiset equals raw detector output before rasterization and recognition length equals canonical-line length. Missing recognition, unassigned/shared support, non-finite or non-positive geometry, rotated geometry, and ambiguous topology fail closed.
   The only new production API is one `#[doc(hidden)]` workspace Rust observation type/method in `koharu-ml` carrying those detections, canonical lines, optional recognition, and the existing word-box projection to `koharu-app`. Existing `PpOcrV5::word_boxes` keeps its signature and behavior by delegating to that observation projection. No Scene, RPC, OpenAPI, external ABI, dependency, or second OCR pipeline changes.
2. VL-only promotion requires exactly one finite positive axis-aligned raw detector, present recognition, PP with no Han or protected/ambiguous Latin, and VL with Han and no protected Latin. It emits exactly that detector bbox; no union, expansion, threshold change, or VL-derived geometry is allowed.
3. PP-anchored Han requires unique detector ownership as selected Han or preserved source. Han and protected/ambiguous Latin in one detector, unassigned/shared support, or VL protected Latin rejects. PP/VL line count, scalar count, character identity, and vector order are diagnostics only.
4. Every selected detector emits one zero-rotation Scene target whose actual `eligible_text_lines` and `line_support_mask` raster is pixel-identical to detector support under the existing shared raster implementation. The validator checks emitted/downstream support, not a reported intermediate bbox. Scene/support APIs, dependencies, models, thresholds, B1, layout, font, render, UI, and color remain unchanged.
5. The sole target mask is `DimensionAndMaskValidatedTarget.agreed_mask`. The validator recomputes target-mask subset coverage, zero protected support, zero unsupported-rotation selection, unique source-ROI ownership, zero unmatched selected nodes, CPU/Metal agreement, and fail-closed fields from actual downstream support. Oracle data never enters production.
6. Diagnostics use one logical canonical `diagnostic-index.json` governed by `.omx/plans/hanonly-r50-b0-evidence-contract.json`. Its top level spans both phases and every candidate; `phase` and `candidate_id` live in each record, whose key is exactly `phase/candidate_id/device/entry_id`. Every update first preserves an immutable generation at the contract's deterministic relative path, then publishes current `diagnostic-index.json`; generation increments by one and binds the prior generation path, exact byte length, and SHA-256. The validator walks the complete generation chain and permits exactly one cell change per generation: add one new `captured_unclassified` cell while incrementing `expected_cell_count`, or terminalize one existing captured cell without changing the count. Before seal the top-level holdout hash is null. The first holdout-cell capture atomically binds the one sealed holdout hash while adding that cell; later generations require exact hash equality and any rebind rejects. Holdout cell additions are legal only after the calibration generation and selected candidate are frozen. Terminal rewrites reject. Records are strictly ascending by key and reject unknown fields, duplicate keys, missing expected cells, path/hash/byte-length drift, B0/device/candidate/manifest/fixture conflicts, or terminal rewrites. The validator descriptor-walks every referenced mode-0600 diagnostic, device-evidence, and log file and recomputes canonical bytes, exact lengths, and SHA-256. Generation capture and terminal publication are file-synced, atomically renamed, and parent-directory-synced. Any durability failure, broken generation link, orphan capture, temporary file, non-terminal state, or conflicting update blocks authorization.
7. R49 `h01-h04` and supplemental `x02-x05` are disclosed regression/challenge only. R50 calibration independently freezes approved `c01-c04`, including c04, under `calibration_manifest_sha256`. Only after rules, schema, implementation, clean B0 SHA, full calibration, and selected candidate freeze may an independent sealed operator prepare never-seen, never-run, non-derived `r50-h01` through `r50-h04` under a separate immutable `holdout_manifest_sha256`; implementation personnel do not inspect them before all eight cells terminate. The existing Rust evidence decoder first runs a no-model same-descriptor source/clean/mask decode preflight and emits a test-executable/feature/hash-bound attestation in the dedicated mode-0700 holdout-seal transaction directory. Then Python-standard-library `seal-holdout` validates that attestation and publishes the manifest/ledger through the closed recoverable two-file transaction before pre-holdout anti-fixture. B0 authorization reruns the same no-model decoder in memory and requires exact canonical attestation reproduction. It cannot alter the calibration manifest, selected candidate, frozen recall contract, calibration artifact fields, masks after runtime output, or any production byte.
   The disclosed R49 h04 `product-id` is pure Latin and was an oracle error: it must not remain an automatic target in any R50 challenge oracle and its ROI remains protected. R49 corpus bytes are not modified.
8. Ordered candidates remain `S25L4`, `S25L5`, `S25L6`, and `S25L7`. Calibration runs all 32 CPU/actual-Metal cells and selects the first all-pass candidate. Holdout runs only that frozen candidate once, without retuning or corpus changes.
9. The artifact uses integer `plan_revision: 50` and exact contract `detector-support-raster-preimage-v1` from `.omx/plans/hanonly-r50-b0-evidence-contract.json`. Every raw detector, including missing-recognition and unassigned/shared/rejected detectors, produces one record; `raw_detector_count` plus unique contiguous indices `0..count-1` proves completeness. Each closed preimage binds the raw detector and recognition class, canonical ownership assignment, emitted Scene and eligible-line quads, detector and actual `line_support_mask` metadata/hashes plus equality, `agreed_mask` metadata/hash/subset, protected/unsupported/unmatched counts, ownership/selection verdicts, and rejection reason. ### Revision 46 logic-correction amendment

This amendment is normative and changes no frozen canonical JSON preimage, byte length, SHA-256, public request/schema field, dependency, renderer count, or sixteen-test staged-RED inventory.

1. The direct page repair route uses the existing `MaskRole::Segment` URL role and accepts only a successfully resolved descriptor whose `produces` is exactly `[Artifact::Inpainted]`. The accepted Segment repair path keeps its existing direct mask-plus-inpainter behavior; rejecting unknown, non-Segment, Renderer, Typography, or multi/other-artifact dispatch is an intentional validation change.
2. Per-node source ink is derived from the aggregate CTD mask by subtracting forbidden support, extracting deterministic 8-connected components, and computing eligible owners from anchor/contact support. Exactly one owner assigns the complete component; zero owners drop it; more than one owner fails the page before inference. No component is split by input order or a fixture-derived threshold.
3. A trustworthy nonempty bubble may enlarge the layout domain only when it contains the complete source anchor. Missing, empty, or non-containing bubble evidence uses the original source anchor as the entire domain; it never falls back to a near-whole-page mask.
4. GREEN-B owns the shared staged `Op::Batch`/History commit boundary before either replace-import route claims atomicity. GREEN-C reuses that helper for marker/style operations and does not introduce it.
5. HanOnly ordering is conditional in `pipeline::run`; static inpainter descriptors remain unchanged, so AllText topology is byte-for-byte unchanged.
6. Renderer+Inpainter is one page transaction from the pre-permit coherent `B_prepare` snapshot: acquire History then Scene, capture authoritative `expected_epoch` and read-only staging inputs, release Scene then History, and finish staging before permit acquisition. Inpainted Scene changes, sprite bytes, normalized frozen `sprite_transform`, and `rendered_direction` are staged together, and Renderer is their sole writer. Only after inpainter, Renderer, final cancellation, one permit acquisition, poison-only under-gate recheck, Blob durability, Blob-local lock release, exactly one permit-held post-Blob History acquisition, exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison, and Scene revalidation may one History Batch enter the GREEN-B durable-commit protocol. Every recoverable failure before its canonical-log rename commit point discards staging and leaves `B_prepare` reachable state intact; a stale epoch after verified Blob promotion may leave only exact verified `unreachable_cas`, never poison or success. `Node.transform` and source/OCR geometry never become placement scratch.
7. Peak-live render admission counts every simultaneously live retained logical sprite, supersampled Pixmap, unavoidable copy, downsample output, masks, staged Scene/blob metadata, and transaction buffer. Infallible full-surface copies are eliminated or converted to checked/fallible paths. The shared budget is checked before allocation against the supported page envelope.
8. Every ink-bearing shaped cluster/run, including ligature and combining-mark groups at the shaping boundary, contributes at least one alpha pixel to the final logical sprite. Legal controls and whitespace are classified separately; whole-sprite nonemptiness alone is insufficient.
9. Successful real-image targets use two blind independently prepared source-ink masks. They must agree exactly before runtime evidence is opened. Machine residue checks use their agreed union and the independently prepared Clean reference to derive `M_delta={p in clean_reference_edit_roi | Source[p] != Clean[p]}` before runtime output is opened; `M_delta` must be nonempty and contain the agreed mask. The same frozen distance/source-closer/8-connected criteria run over both masks, and the existing pinned Source Gate OCR model/config may only add a rejection for a positive-area candidate intersecting `M_delta`. Human review may add a rejection but cannot waive any machine failure.
10. `hanonly-test-evidence` may add observation only. Final acceptance runs the same public-output equivalence contract once without that feature and once with it, then requires identical public Scene/blob/Rendered bytes, status, warnings, transforms, directions, and public error tokens.
11. The static production-policy scanner is defense in depth. The required merge job additionally runs the existing ordinary adversarial test with a CI-runtime cryptographic seed; dimensions, geometry, node order, text length, color, transform, and supported in-memory format vary after build, the seed is printed for reproduction, and no new dependency is added.
12. GREEN-B History commit uses cloned Scene/op staging plus whole-log copy-on-write. Before permit acquisition, acquire History then Scene for one coherent snapshot, capture authoritative `expected_epoch` and the read-only Scene/History inputs required to stage the complete Batch, then release Scene followed by History. With no staging History/Scene guard held and before permit acquisition, apply the complete Batch only to one staged clone, compute the checked next epoch and staged next state/inverses/undo/redo, and serialize the complete frame. Then acquire the persistence permit, recheck poison only, promote required Blob objects, release every Blob-local/cache/file lock, acquire History exactly once within the permit-held post-Blob boundary, perform exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison, acquire Scene, and revalidate required state. Only after that comparison and Scene revalidation, while holding the post-Blob History then Scene guards, read the current canonical old generation into immutable bytes and parse it with existing compatible startup semantics: replay complete decodable frames and ignore only a malformed or truncated trailing frame. Record exact raw old-generation bytes and `old_valid_log_bytes`; build the new generation solely as `old_valid_log_bytes + new_frame`; write and `sync_all` one same-directory task-owned `create_new` temp; then strictly replay that immutable temp to exact EOF from a separate fresh clone. Exactly the new `epoch+1` frame applies once and must equal the already staged Scene and next epoch. The private platform adapter in item 16 then returns `Committed`, `Unchanged`, or `Indeterminate` from exact canonical bytes plus required sync/reopen evidence. Only `Committed` durably replaces the canonical generation and publishes the staged real Scene, canonical writer, epoch, undo, and redo; `Unchanged` preserves exact `B_prepare`; `Indeterminate` invokes item 18's persistence-poison lifecycle without publishing. After outcome handling, release Scene then History; publish synchronous response/Dirty/autosave/job success only for `Committed`; then release the permit. Restart reads the canonical path once into immutable bytes, uses strict-first replay on a disposable fresh clone, and falls back to compatible replay on a second fresh clone only for a malformed/truncated legacy tail. Replay never concatenates generations, reuses a partially mutated probe clone, guesses, rolls back/truncates, or applies the new frame twice.
13. `B_prepare` blob equality means `reachable_blob_state(B)`: a canonical sorted map of exact references, lengths, and opened bytes/content hashes reachable from current Scene, committed/replayable History op roots, and current undo/redo op trees. Physical CAS objects outside those roots are `unreachable_cas`, excluded from equality and reported only by a separate orphan/integrity audit. A verified immutable orphan may remain a GC candidate but cannot be read by Scene/replay/undo/redo; task-owned temporary files must be removed.
14. The required metamorphic job resolves `han_only_source_color_work_budget_adversarial_stress` through `--list` to exactly one module-qualified full libtest name, executes only that name with `--exact --nocapture`, and proves exactly one test started, one passed, and zero failed. Missing, renamed, deleted, duplicate, root-unqualified, or zero-test output fails the job; the ordinary short ID remains unchanged.
15. The integration/source-policy lane exclusively owns every production and colocated test hunk in `crates/koharu-core/src/op.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/history.rs`, the durable-promotion and bound-control hunks in `crates/koharu-app/src/blobs.rs`, `PersistenceControl`, permit/accessors, field encapsulation, persistence poison, and direct-caller migration in `crates/koharu-app/src/session.rs`, poison-aware close in `crates/koharu-app/src/app.rs`, autosave poison stop in `crates/koharu-app/src/autosave.rs`, shared backend admission/direct guard/gated page transaction/fatal propagation in `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs`, and backend preflight/job behavior in `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pipelines.rs`. Its exact live direct-field caller closure is `crates/koharu-app/src/session.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/app.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/ai.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/bin/pipeline.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/binary.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/mcp/mod.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/psd_export.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/projects.rs`, `tests/integration-tests/tests/binary.rs`, `tests/integration-tests/tests/pipelines.rs`, and `tests/integration-tests/tests/scene.rs`; every migration hunk and the same-module structural audit of `ProjectSession::open_untrusted` belong only to integration/source-policy. Read-only callers move to existing or narrow read-only ProjectSession methods; write-capable Blob callers move to the accepted bound/gated path without changing public BlobStore `put_*` signatures. Shared Batch staging, whole-log History copy-on-write, apply/undo/redo/snapshot/compact/autosave/pipeline gate/race/fault tests, Windows prerequisite coverage, and import/GREEN-C reuse have the same exclusive owner. Planner/provenance owns no hunk, review sign-off, or checkpoint in those surfaces.
16. History durable replacement is one private, dependency-free platform adapter with observable outcomes `Committed`, `Unchanged`, or `Indeterminate`; API return alone never publishes state. Both platforms flush and relinquish the old append writer before replacing, `sync_all` the verified same-directory temp, invoke pinned Rust 1.97 `std::fs::rename`, reopen the canonical file, verify its exact bytes, and retain one usable append writer. Unix `Committed` additionally requires parent-directory `sync_all`. Windows `Committed` instead requires related old/temp/canonical handles released before overwrite rename, canonical reopen/file `sync_all`, exact-new bytes, no task temp, and a usable append writer; it makes no parent-directory-fsync claim. `Committed` alone publishes staged Scene, epoch, undo/redo, and writer. `Unchanged` requires exact raw-old bytes, a synced reopened old writer, and no temp and preserves `B_prepare`. Missing, unreadable, unexpected, partial, mixed, unsyncable, or ownership-uncertain state is `Indeterminate` and invokes item 18. Restart retains strict-first fresh-clone replay plus legacy-tail fallback on a second fresh clone and accepts only one canonical generation. No stronger hardware/power-loss guarantee is claimed beyond successful host sync contracts.
17. Existing `crates/koharu-app/src/blobs.rs` owns one private `durable_put_exact` helper, with no new production module or dependency. At `ProjectSession::open_inner`, the session's `BlobStore` is bound to that session's exact item 18 `PersistenceControl`. Public `BlobStore::{put_bytes,put_webp,put_raw}` signatures remain unchanged and automatically acquire the bound control; crate-private permit-aware variants require a non-cloneable permit whose control identity equals the store's bound control, preventing recursive acquisition when a ProjectSession transaction already holds the permit. A standalone `BlobStore` may own an independent local control but can never be installed as a ProjectSession store. After admission, `durable_put_exact` creates one same-directory task-owned `create_new` temp, writes/flushes all expected bytes, calls temp `sync_all`, and verifies exact length, bytes, and content hash before promotion. Unix releases the temp handle, renames to the canonical hash path, reopens/verifies exact canonical bytes, then opens and `sync_all`s the parent directory. Windows releases every related temp/canonical handle, performs overwrite rename through pinned Rust 1.97 `std::fs::rename`, reopens the canonical file, calls canonical-file `sync_all`, and verifies exact canonical length, bytes, and hash; it makes no parent-directory-fsync claim. An existing exact object is verified and reused; a wrong object is replaced and reverified. Single puts hold their permit through verified canonical and cache publication. Multi-object Renderer+Inpainter uses permit-aware variants under one outer session permit. Failure removes a certainly owned temp, returns before History commit, and may leave only an exact verified `unreachable_cas` object. Blob/CAS failure never poisons because the later History commit has not made the object reachable.
18. `ProjectSession` owns one private `Arc<PersistenceControl>` containing fast `AtomicBool persistence_poisoned` plus one non-reentrant synchronous mutex that is the sole persistence linearization gate; every new/reopened session initializes an unpoisoned control. The exact fatal text remains `project persistence is indeterminate; close and reopen the project`. The optional Acquire load is only a fast rejection. Correctness requires acquiring the gate, reloading poison under it, and constructing a non-cloneable permit tied to that exact control; the permit remains held through every canonical write, in-memory publication, and synchronous response/Dirty/autosave/job success publication. The one normative order is: acquire History then Scene before the permit, capture coherent `expected_epoch` and read-only staging inputs, release Scene then History, and finish lock-free staging; acquire the persistence permit; poison-only under-gate recheck; Blob canonical promotion with no History/Scene lock; release Blob cache/file-local locks; acquire History exactly once within the permit-held publication boundary and perform exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison; acquire Scene and revalidate required state; History canonical replacement plus Scene/epoch/undo/redo publication; release Scene then History; synchronous response/Dirty/autosave/job success publication; release permit. No staging History/Scene guard may cross permit acquisition. Within the permit-held publication boundary, no pre-Blob History acquisition or epoch read/validation is permitted. No atomic epoch mirror, additional persistence gate, additional permit-held History phase, or compatibility shim is permitted. A stale epoch found after Blob promotion publishes no Scene/History/success state, never poisons, and may leave only exact verified `unreachable_cas` that cannot be reached from Scene, replayable History, undo, or redo. No path acquires the gate while holding History, Scene, Blob cache, App autosave, Registry, job-map, or event-bus locks, and no `.await` occurs while a permit is held. Read-only Scene snapshot, snapshot-with-epoch, epoch/project summary, diagnosis, and export remain gate-free and expose no write handle. `ProjectSession.scene`, `history`, and `blobs` become private; narrow read-only and gated methods replace direct access, while a structural policy and the ordinary lifecycle test prove same-module `ProjectSession::open_untrusted` cannot bypass the control. Existing request/schema, Scene data-model, `EngineCtx`, `Engine::run`, Renderer, and BlobStore `put_*` signatures do not change, but removing those three public ProjectSession fields is an explicit downstream-breaking Rust helper/API change with no compatibility shim. Apply, apply-if-epoch, undo, redo, snapshot/compact, autosave, page import/mask repair, pipeline CAS/History commit, and success publication all use the same control. The History adapter never reacquires it: its caller holds the permit. `Indeterminate` stores poison with Release while still holding the permit, suppresses staged memory and every success publication, then releases; every waiter rechecks and fails before canonical I/O. Deterministic barriers/channels prove both total orders: poison wins while a writer waits before gate acquisition, or an already-permitted canonical publication and success complete while the poisoner remains blocked and poison linearizes only after release. No sleep or elapsed-time oracle is allowed. Pipeline poison is `Failed`, never a warning or `CompletedWithErrors`; autosave logs once and exits. `App::close_project` atomically removes the session, closes/joins autosave, skips `FlushNow` and final compact, drops the lock, and succeeds without writing. Only later open constructs an unpoisoned session after strict-first canonical recovery; no in-process reset exists.
19. Existing `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs` defines one `#[doc(hidden)] pub` pure function `backend_admission(source_text_policy, cpu, target_os) -> Option<&'static str>`. Because `pipeline` is public, this is an explicit additive downstream-public Rust helper, not a private or workspace-only API; it changes no existing signature, request/schema field, Scene, Engine, `EngineCtx`, or Renderer contract. `None` admits AllText at either CPU flag, HanOnly `cpu=true` everywhere, and HanOnly `cpu=false` only on macOS. `Some` is exactly `han_only.unsupported_backend: runtime backend is not accepted by Revision 46; retry with explicit CPU`. `start_pipeline` keeps first side-effect-free region rejection. `spawn_pipeline_job` then calls the helper before request-step `Registry::find`: admitted requests retain synchronous unknown-ID HTTP 400, while unsupported requests skip Registry preflight but still create one ordinary job. Direct `pipeline::run` independently calls the same helper after its region guard and before `infos_for_spec`, order/page/Scene/run-state/model/engine/blob/History access; only it emits the one warning and returns `warning_count=1`, so RPC produces `CompletedWithErrors` exactly once. Requested Metal on macOS remains subject to actual-Metal T6 proof. CUDA, Vulkan, and every other non-CPU path on Windows/Linux/other remain outside Revision 46.

## Outcome and success criteria

Fix the shared runtime boundaries that currently make HanOnly technically safe but visually fail:

1. retain complete target ink evidence through Segment-to-inpaint expansion while subtracting protected/non-target regions;
2. treat PP-OCR geometry as an anchor and derive a deterministic dynamic layout region from the current page, bubble, writing mode, neighboring source geometry, and target ownership;
3. replace fixed absolute production crop/size deductions with geometry-derived backend policy that scales across source dimensions: the backend exclusively computes `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` from the per-target resolved layout in crate-private HanOnly records; HanOnly automatic UI with no manual size displays the existing Auto/empty-numeric state and never estimates an automatic pixel value; manual sizing and AllText remain unchanged, and UI/backend parity is required only for the PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist;
4. accept Planner line/style/font-size suggestions only as constrained, observable hints, keep final font size under local layout/raster authority, and preserve an exact local fallback;
5. treat rotated Han geometry as an explicit unsupported result in this bounded correction, never as a silently successful translation;
6. retain every listed HanOnly/AllText/manual/group/protected-content/safety/atomicity invariant;
7. make image intake bounded, byte-accounted, format-explicit, orientation-canonical, and Scene-atomic before any replace operation, with the same decoder contract used by import and later blob loads;
8. make one portable static production-policy audit mandatory for every normal merge to `main`, with machine-verified Ruleset plus distinct candidate-head and required-check-commit evidence, while leaving model/Metal/360-cell work in trusted final acceptance;
9. make automatic HanOnly color and stroke fail closed under `source-color-contract-v2` plus deterministic `source-color-work-budget-v1`: render only from a machine-validated, work-admitted source-pixel fill/stroke contract with one deterministic target stroke width, never from prediction, a default, a contrasting-color guess, timeout, or partial candidate, and preserve the complete source ROI with an explicit error when the source appearance is ambiguous, too expensive to classify, or cannot be represented by the current solid-fill/optional-solid-stroke renderer;
10. prove that every successful sprite is actually composited into the final Rendered RGBA; bind every trusted runtime cell plus `R/A/C` to one trusted-target production closure; require trusted macOS and PR Ubuntu to share identical whole-file `common` and target-independent generator-lock hashes; independently reproduce each target record twice; and bind both target records, the exact PR candidate head, and the exact GitHub test-merge check commit in `G`.
11. prove before any erase or inpainter run/apply that every successful target rasterizes the complete expected translation into one final frozen sprite: bind the pre-Planner translation, accepted frozen input, and pre-inpaint raster entry by SHA-256 inside the guarded evidence root; prove insertion-only line-break reversibility; cover every final input byte by one shaped-cluster interval or deterministic layout-control record; treat glyph ID `0` for any non-control cluster as missing `.notdef`; reject every nonzero glyph ID outside `u16`; require every shaped glyph to traverse the fill pass; reject empty alpha; independently check the complete retained logical RGBA set and each actual-scale transient raster surface; keep at most one target scratch surface live; measure every builder failure against `B_prepare`, captured after upstream producers and immediately before the builder, without rolling back those producer commits; and make Renderer persist/composite those exact immutable bytes once with zero reshaping or rerasterization.
12. preserve public request/type/serialization field shapes while truth-syncing the newly rejected region behavior and stable HTTP 400 across source comments, generated OpenAPI artifacts, and all four localized HTTP API references; keep raw text and every text-derived digest/length/count only in guarded local evidence, while exported `R/A/C/G`, CI corpus, workflow annotation, and closure summaries expose at most random content-independent target-correlation IDs plus non-length verdicts.
13. make the cross-platform durability and backend guarantees truthful and enforceable: classify History replacement by exact canonical bytes plus platform-required sync/reopen evidence, poison every indeterminate session, admit only explicit CPU everywhere and requested Metal on macOS, and make one real Windows History contract test an unconditional prerequisite of the sole required `HanOnly Production Policy` context.

The implementation is complete only after deterministic tests, bounded/atomic malformed and over-limit import tests, strict source-color extraction/render oracles, checked classifier-work accounting and adversarial fail-closed stress tests, explicit unsupported-color and unsupported-backend preservation tests, an independent-source full-page smoke matrix, the approved test-only regression input, explicit-CPU and actual-Metal runs, repeatability, restart stability, Unix and real-Windows History durability tests, human texture review, the exact prerequisite-plus-required CI contract, and an active verified repository Ruleset all pass.

## Root-cause chain

1. **Recognition geometry became erase geometry.** `finalize_segment_mask` clips refined CTD output to tight PP-OCR line support, then `prepare_inpaint_mask` clips both before and after dynamic expansion. Ink outside the tight box is irretrievably discarded before inpainting.
2. **Recognition geometry became layout geometry.** HanOnly inputs are blanket-locked, `build_render_inputs` discards a target when its nonempty translated-line count differs from the eligible OCR-line count, single-line inputs are locked again, and post-render validation rejects every alpha pixel outside the tight line union. The fitter can only skip the target or choose a smaller font, even when safe neighboring background exists.
3. **Planner authority is both too broad and too weak.** Stored Planner style is treated as an explicit manual override, so it can replace reliable predicted light-fill/dark-stroke style; meanwhile HanOnly Planner lines and font size are entirely ignored. A single persisted boolean cannot safely represent mixed Planner reflow and later manual style. The correct boundary is one transient HanOnly Planner hint plus locally authoritative font/raster fit.
4. **One tight bbox carries four incompatible responsibilities.** PP-OCR remains correct for identity/order/anchor decisions, but it is not a complete ink support and is not the available layout area. The fix must derive private per-run erase and layout geometry without changing recognition geometry.
5. **Fixture-calibrated absolute policies survived into production.** Source Gate selects absolute C2 two-pixel crop growth (`source_language_gate.rs:195,808-835`), while backend and UI source-relative sizing duplicate a blanket `-5px` deduction outside Japanese/Korean (`pipeline/engines/renderer.rs:119-127`; `RenderControlsPanel.tsx:310-325`). These values do not scale with the source image or text geometry; any replacement ratio must be predeclared, calibrated, frozen, and independently held out.
6. **Unsupported geometry and no-op inpaint can look successful.** Nonzero rotation is rejected by crop and renderer safety (`source_language_gate.rs:849-870`; `pipeline/engines/renderer.rs:328-331,379-382`), while an eligible mask that becomes empty can return an unchanged image. The pipeline must distinguish safe no-target behavior, unsafe no-mask failure, and explicitly unsupported geometry.
7. **Current evidence is not source-independent.** The five checked source-gate crops share one `source_raw_blake3` in `fixture-manifest.json:2-55`; they are five regions from one source, not five production image classes.
8. **Replace import mutates before complete validation.** Both `create_pages` and `create_pages_from_paths` apply a removal Batch before all candidate files have been read, decoded, format-checked, and bounded. A later malformed or unreadable file can therefore destroy the prior page set even though the requested replacement fails.
9. **Automatic text preprocessing still has absolute discontinuities.** Backend automatic sizing clamps `max_dim/90` to `12..28`, renderer policy retains `GLOBAL_CAP_PX=72`, UI duplicates the cap, and PP-OCR word-box preprocessing switches on source-height `160` and source-dimension `1024`. Those branches can change layout or OCR behavior when equivalent content crosses a resize boundary.
10. **Decode identity and resource ownership are implicit.** UI selection exposes PNG/JPEG/WebP while backend `image::load_from_memory` accepts the compiled decoder set, EXIF orientation is not canonically applied, transparent model input is not normalized, multipart/path decoding is parallel and unbounded by aggregate decoded bytes, and BlobStore's LRU is count-based at 64 images. Production behavior can therefore vary by container metadata or exhaust memory before HanOnly logic runs.
11. **Automatic color is neither source-authoritative nor fail closed.** `resolve_text_color` accepts `fontPrediction.text_color`, but `resolve_stroke_style` ignores `fontPrediction.stroke_color` and synthesizes `contrasting_stroke_color(text_color)`; missing evidence can still fall back to default black. The model directly regresses fill/stroke RGB values, but predictions alone cannot strictly prove the source appearance, and the current renderer represents only one solid fill plus one optional solid stroke. The fix must derive a validated private color contract from canonical Source pixels before erasure and refuse unsupported or ambiguous source styles instead of rendering a plausible wrong color.
12. **The strict source-color classifier was deterministic but not production-bounded.** Exhaustive fill and ordered fill/stroke candidates each replay pixel reconstruction/path classification, and stroke candidates search `1..=U`; a high-entropy or large target/page could therefore consume quadratic-or-worse work before eventually failing closed. Correctness must use a frozen checked work-unit reservation, never elapsed time, and complexity rejection must happen before candidate materialization, erase, inference, blob creation, renderer output, or Scene mutation.
13. **P0 target counting was circular.** The prior plan called filtered automatic-target count `T` a collection length available without traversal, but live production first has to inspect page nodes to derive that collection. Revision 46 must admit and charge that enumeration from O(1) page-node count `J` before reading any node.
14. **The direct repair route accepted arbitrary registered engines.** `PutMaskParams.pipeline` is an unconstrained string and `pages.rs` currently resolves and runs any matching descriptor, while the UI writes the repair mask through the existing `MaskRole::Segment` endpoint and current inpainters consume `Segment`. Region-bearing repair must therefore be restricted before body decode, blob persistence, or Scene/engine effects to URL role `MaskRole::Segment` and an exact sole `Inpainted` producer.
15. **Nonempty output did not prove translated-text completeness.** Source removal, one nonempty sprite, exact final replay, and omission inequality can all pass for an arbitrary or incomplete sprite. Successful acceptance also needs a byte-bound translation-to-renderer chain and complete layout-cluster/glyph fill-pass evidence.

## Current audit baseline

- `test.jpeg` and the current untracked `test-image/O1CN01LriAra2AloJPVFqEZ_!!2216907268244.webp` are two container/name representations of the same approved `790x1023` regression image. Regression identity is determined by decoded pixel content, not filename, extension, or raw container hash. Those values, crop coordinates, and derived node data are evidence only and must never become production branches or constants.
- The fresh audit found no production branch keyed by that basename, a known source hash, the complete `790x1023` pair, fixed fixture coordinates, crop names, or fixed `NodeId` values. Existing exact literals are confined to `#[cfg(test)]` code and committed test fixtures.
- The production-genericity defects are shared policies rather than a direct fixture lookup: tight OCR geometry is reused for erase/layout/sprite containment, Source Gate uses absolute C2 growth, backend and UI retain absolute font caps/deductions, PP-OCR branches on absolute source dimensions, rendering requires target/OCR line-count equality, and nonzero rotation is unsupported.
- The current UI-only chain `eligibleSourceLayout -> automaticSourceSize -> groupedAutomaticSourceSizes` consumes OCR/source boxes plus prediction/detection/fallback and applies its own `-5px`, `12..28`, `72px`, and source-box cap/deduction policy. It cannot access the crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout records, so exact backend/UI `G/Csrc` or automatic-pixel parity is neither available nor a valid target. The correction retires this HanOnly automatic estimator chain and preserves the existing Auto/empty-numeric UI state.
- `create_pages` and `create_pages_from_paths` currently clear pages before all inputs decode, use broad content decoding independently of the UI allowlist, and decode collections in parallel. `BlobStore::load_image` decodes again through a separate helper and retains up to 64 decoded images without byte accounting.
- The compiled `image` decoder has a default allocation ceiling but no default width/height ceiling; that per-decoder limit does not bound multipart bytes, simultaneous decodes, cached decoded bytes, or full-page mask allocations. The fix must use checked byte arithmetic and one aggregate ownership contract rather than relying on decoder defaults alone.
- The planned checker, test, digest corpus, workflow job `HanOnly Production Policy`, and active required-check Ruleset are not implemented yet. Local source inspection is evidence for this revision only; it is not an enforceable merge guard.
- `FontPrediction` already carries `text_color`, `stroke_color`, `font_size_px`, and `stroke_width_px`, and the ML detector regresses both color triples. Current app rendering uses the predicted fill but discards the predicted stroke color in favor of an automatically contrasting stroke. Existing renderer passes already separate stroke then fill and accept exact RGBA values, so strict validation can reuse the current renderer without a second production renderer or runtime dependency.
- `PutMaskParams.pipeline` currently accepts an arbitrary string; `pages.rs` persists the mask blob, snapshots/applies the mask, resolves any registered `EngineInfo`, loads it, and calls `Engine::run` with a region. `EngineInfo` has no role field, so the narrow production guard must check the route's existing `MaskRole::Segment` and require `engine_info.produces == &[Artifact::Inpainted]` before body decode/blob persistence, Scene access, engine load/run, or `app.apply`.
- `TinySkiaRenderer::render_pass` visits every layout line and `render_line` visits every shaped glyph, but the current path does not make glyph ID `0` a target-level missing-glyph failure and a failed `u16::try_from(glyph.glyph_id)` silently advances past a nonzero out-of-range glyph. Revision 46 rejects glyph-zero `.notdef` for non-control clusters, makes nonzero conversion failure fail closed, and binds successful runtime layout transcripts to the expected target bytes; no public renderer API changes.

## Preserved invariants

- HanOnly replaces Han only; complete Latin remains protected; isolated `S` behavior remains valid; pure English remains rejected.
- AllText, zero-target cleanup, repair regions, and invalid/missing target-language behavior do not change.
- English remains detected-first; other recognized languages retain their existing backend prediction/detection source-estimate order. The backend blanket `-5px` deduction and absolute `12..28`/`72px` automatic caps are intentionally removed. For HanOnly automatic sizing, backend-only `cross_extent` is resolved-region height for horizontal writing and resolved-region width for vertical writing, and `G=floor(min(page_width,page_height,cross_extent))` with checked finite positive inputs/conversion. `G` is only the geometry-derived search ceiling; the existing stroke/effect/padding-aware local raster-fit predicate performs every further reduction, and no `GLOBAL_CAP_PX` or renamed fixed pixel cap participates. UI receives none of `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` and produces no automatic pixel estimate.
- Manual font size and manual style remain highest priority and outside automatic group computation.
- An unmarked stored HanOnly style remains an explicit `manual_override`: it retains current behavior and is reported as such, but no source-color equality claim is made for an intentional user override. A marked Planner style never enters that tier. AllText color behavior remains unchanged.
- Every axis-aligned non-manual automatic HanOnly target accepted by Source Gate first receives one provisional source-color/width result. The pipeline may atomically publish the final private map `automatic_strict(SourceColorContract) | unsupported_source_color(SourceColorReason)` only after the complete no-side-effect layout/font/group/Planner-size/color/width preflight succeeds and before any erase or inpaint write. Rotation is classified first as a terminal geometry outcome and is outside this color-mode domain. There is no partial publication or automatic “best effort” color mode.
- HanOnly scheduling makes that chronology executable with existing artifacts only. `FontPredictions` and `TypographyStyles` each permit zero or one selected producer; more than one fails before engine load. `cloud-typography-planner` already needs `FontPredictions`; each selected inpainter additionally needs `FontPredictions` and `TypographyStyles`, so `build_order` conditionally orders only producers that exist. Missing optional producers create no edge, synthetic producer, or engine requirement.
- The HanOnly Renderer/Inpainted matrix is total for normal HanOnly `pipeline::run`, defined exactly by `spec.options.region.is_none()`. With no Renderer and no `Inpainted` producer, the spec is valid and requires zero builder calls, zero sprite reservation/raster/publication, and no frozen object for that page. With no Renderer and one inpainter, the spec remains valid when its other needs are valid and requires exactly one complete builder preflight/publication for that `PageId` immediately before that inpainter; its frozen sprite payload is released without persistence after successful inpaint because no Renderer consumes it. Selecting Renderer with zero `Inpainted` producers fails before engine load. Selecting Renderer with exactly one inpainter requires exactly one complete builder preflight/publication for that `PageId` immediately before it and requires inpainter and Renderer to consume the same page object inside one `B_prepare`-scoped transaction. More than one `Inpainted` producer always fails before engine load. HanOnly-only conditional order is `font < typography < builder-layout/raster/freeze < inpainter < renderer-persist/composite` when all are present; omit only absent optional producers and omit the final Renderer term when Renderer is absent, while static descriptors and AllText order remain unchanged. `start_pipeline` rejects `req.region.is_some()` immediately after extraction and before session lookup, options/spec construction, job/cancel registration, event publication, or task spawn, returning one HTTP 400 directing callers to the existing atomic page repair route. As the first executable validation, `pipeline::run` independently rejects `spec.options.region.is_some()` before `infos_for_spec`, order construction, page/Scene access, run-Arc creation, engine load, warning, builder, publication, or mutation. Public `StartPipelineRequest.region` and `PipelineRunOptions.region` field/schema shapes and `options_from_request` conversion remain unchanged. The direct Segment repair route in `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs` is the sole allowed region-bearing engine ingress only after a pre-side-effect guard proves `role == MaskRole::Segment`, `Registry::find(params.pipeline)` succeeds, and `engine_info.produces == &[Artifact::Inpainted]`. Unknown IDs, Renderer/Typography/other producers, and non-Segment roles return one stable HTTP 400 before body decode/blob persistence, Scene snapshot/read/apply, engine cache/load/run, builder/publication/accessor, or mutation. The accepted route retains the existing direct `EngineCtx` path, performs zero builder calls and zero frozen-state publication, and bypasses the task-local accessor before any lookup. Missing task-local state is never a repair fallback. `PutMaskParams.pipeline` type/serialization shape remains unchanged, but the route comments, OpenAPI behavior/response descriptions, generated API artifacts, and localized HTTP API references must document the stable 400 and direct callers to `PUT /pages/{id}/masks/{role}?pipeline=...`.
- The sole owner of complete HanOnly render construction is crate-private `pipeline/engines/renderer.rs::prepare_and_freeze_hanonly_render_inputs(...)`. After every selected upstream font/Typography producer has successfully committed its permitted changes and immediately before invoking the builder, `pipeline/mod.rs` captures `B_prepare`: observable Scene bytes/epoch, History epoch/canonical-log bytes, `reachable_blob_state(B_prepare)`, and persisted sprite inventory. `reachable_blob_state` is the canonical sorted exact-reference/length/byte-hash map reachable from current Scene, committed/replayable History op roots, and current undo/redo op trees; physical unreachable CAS is excluded and separately audited. From that exact pre-inpaint snapshot, the builder resolves final layout/font/effects, validates expected/frozen translation identity, builds canonical cluster/control coverage, rejects glyph ID `0` for non-control clusters and nonzero glyph IDs outside `u16`, executes the shared production fill raster path exactly once per successful target, requires nonempty alpha, and freezes immutable sprite bytes, transcript, placement, and resolved metadata into one `PageId` object. For retained payload, it computes each `logical_sprite_bytes_i=checked(logical_width_i*logical_height_i*4)`, checked-converts every logical dimension and byte count to `usize`, proves the logical surface lies within that target's disjoint owner rectangle, checked-sums the complete canonical target set, and reserves it only when the total is at most `checked(page_width*page_height*4)`. Separately, for each target at the actual existing 2-4x `raster_scale_i`, it checked-computes `raster_width_i=logical_width_i*raster_scale_i`, `raster_height_i=logical_height_i*raster_scale_i`, and `raster_surface_bytes_i=raster_width_i*raster_height_i*4`, checked-converts those values to `usize`, and constructs the transient surface through the existing fallible surface constructor. Targets rasterize serially; each supersampled/copy/downsample scratch allocation is released before the next target begins, so transient surfaces are never summed as retained bytes and at most one target scratch surface is live. No target buffer or partial object is published until every target and both retained/transient checks pass. The existing `pipeline/mod.rs` defines crate-private `PipelineRunState` plus one `tokio::task_local!` key, creates exactly one `Arc<PipelineRunState>` per `pipeline::run`, and exposes only a crate-private non-panicking closure accessor based on `try_with`. Frozen objects are keyed by `PageId`; a page reaching one selected inpainter receives exactly one atomic complete publication under its own key immediately before inpainting, while a page with no inpainter or any builder failure publishes nothing and releases every unpublished buffer/reservation/scratch surface. Each related Typography, selected inpainter, selected Renderer, and feature-gated AOT-observation future is awaited through a scope binding the same run Arc and current `PageId`; the accessor succeeds only for that page and errors before/after scope, for a wrong page, for a completed prior page, and after run completion. The sole builder is the deliberate private exception: `pipeline/mod.rs` passes it the same Arc and current `PageId` directly. Builder, inpainter, and Renderer observe `Arc::ptr_eq` and the same current-page frozen-object identity. If the inpainter fails after publication, Renderer is skipped and the sprite payload is released with zero sprite/blob persistence; after successful Renderer persistence/composition the payload is released immediately. Small immutable terminal metadata may remain until run-state drop, but pixel buffers never accumulate across pages. Concurrent runs use distinct Arcs; nested same-task runs shadow then restore the outer Arc; `tokio::spawn` does not inherit the task-local; no carrier-dependent work or mutable handle outlives its awaited scoped future; and the Arc plus every remaining page record drops at `pipeline::run` completion. The exact public `EngineCtx` fields, crate-root re-export, and by-value `Engine::run(&self, EngineCtx<'_>)` signature remain unchanged, and `PipelineRunState` is never added to `EngineCtx`, `PipelineRunOptions`, `RenderBlockInput`, `PageRenderOptions`, Scene, OpenAPI, or another public carrier. After `Inpainted`, a selected Renderer calls a crate-private HanOnly entry in the existing `crates/koharu-app/src/renderer.rs` that persists/composites the already-frozen current-page sprite exactly once; it performs zero layout, shaping, glyph validation, raster, fit, grouping, Planner-size, rounding, or width work. AllText remains on the unchanged public render path.
- Each successful target's frozen record binds `expected_translation_utf8_sha256`, accepted `frozen_renderer_input_utf8_sha256`, `renderer_entry_utf8_sha256` for the builder's pre-inpaint raster entry, byte/scalar counts, the exact insertion-only Planner-break transcript, canonical layout-cluster/control coverage digest/counts, shaped glyph count, representable-glyph verdict, `missing_glyph_count`, successful fill-pass completion, nonempty-alpha verdict, logical sprite dimensions/bytes, actual raster scale/dimensions/surface bytes, scratch-release verdict, and frozen sprite identity. Without Planner breaks, all three byte digests match; with accepted breaks, removing only the audited inserted `\n` bytes reconstructs the exact original translation digest. Every final UTF-8 byte is covered by a shaped-cluster interval or one closed legal no-ink layout-control record. A translation-identity mismatch, final-layout failure after local fallback, cluster/control-coverage failure, glyph-validity failure, fill-traversal failure, or nonempty-alpha validation failure is a deterministic hard pre-write `geometry_or_font` failure. Only checked logical/actual-raster arithmetic or `usize` conversion failure, retained owner-rectangle/page-cap reservation failure, or fallible raster-surface-construction failure is a recoverable `geometry_or_font` failure. Both classes leave final observable Scene/History/reachable-blob/sprite state equal to `B_prepare`, produce zero builder publication, erase, inpainter run/apply, or downstream Inpainted/Rendered/blob/sprite persistence relative to that baseline, and neither roll back nor alter upstream producer commits already present in `B_prepare`. Process-level allocator OOM/abort is outside recoverable `geometry_or_font` semantics and carries no process-local preservation claim. The low-level glyph-conversion error remains defense in depth in the shared raster path. Detailed digests/counts and the opaque-ID mapping exist only in mode-0600 files beneath the descriptor-guarded external evidence root and never contain raw source or translated text. Exported `R/A/C/G`, CI corpus, workflow annotation, and closure summaries reject raw text, stable NodeId, source/OCR/translated line counts, text-derived digests, text/byte/scalar/cluster/glyph lengths or counts, and the guarded mapping; they may carry only a random content-independent target-correlation ID plus non-length verdicts and unrelated closure/governance fields.
- `SourceColorContract` is crate-private and run-local. Its sole source coordinate is `RenderBlockInput.source_transform` in page space; the eligible translated-line `transform` remains layout-only and cannot affect source sampling, source-ink ROI, `F_s`, or source width. Convert `source_transform` to a page-clipped integer bbox with `left/top=floor` and inclusive `right/bottom=ceil-1`. Define `source_geometry_estimate` as that unexpanded bbox height for horizontal writing and width for vertical writing; then compute checked `F_s=floor(source_geometry_estimate+0.5)` before `G`, prediction, or Planner adjustment and require `F_s>=1` for every automatic contract. Let `f_t_raw` be the finite positive final backend size after the exact per-target fit, Planner-size outcome, and group-min resolution; define `F_t=checked_u64(floor(f_t_raw+0.5))` in integer page pixels before raster/device scale. Freeze and pass that same `F_t` unchanged to Renderer. The contract contains exact source-derived fill RGBA, optional source-derived stroke RGBA, `F_s`, uniquely proved single-sided source stroke radius `W_s`, optional reduced `stroke_ratio`, frozen `F_t`, deterministic target radius `W_t`, complete partition and preflight evidence, version `source-color-contract-v2`, and work-budget version `source-color-work-budget-v1`; it is never persisted to Scene/OpenAPI or exposed as a default-build public API.
- Strictly supported source appearance is one opaque solid fill plus zero or one opaque solid stroke. Source fill/stroke candidate samples must be nonempty, alpha `255`, and satisfy frozen product constants `COLOR_UNIFORMITY_P95_MAX_CHANNEL=4`, `COLOR_MIN_CLUSTER_DISTANCE_L1=24`, and `COLOR_AA_BLEND_RESIDUAL_MAX_CHANNEL=1`. For each representative, P95 is the checked nearest-rank percentile over max-channel deviations of every `Q` sample in that representative's complete 5-bit bin, not the already-filtered direct-core subset; direct membership is the same-bin subset with deviation `<=4`. These values are frozen before any image result, are not selected or tuned from regression/calibration/holdout images, and may change only in a new reviewed plan revision.
- Gradients, multiple fill colors, patterns/textures, nonuniform or nonopaque text alpha, shadows, glows, unsupported effect colors, missing interior samples, ambiguous fill/stroke partition, insufficient foreground/background separation, any source sample outside the frozen contract, or `ClassifierWorkBudgetExceeded` are `unsupported_source_color`. A work-limited target uses stable warning `han_only.unsupported_source_color:classifier_work_budget_exceeded`; no partial/current-best candidate, prediction, Planner value, default, timeout, elapsed threshold, or retry may recover it. Every unsupported target is removed from erase ownership before inpainting, retains byte-identical Source/Inpainted/Rendered pixels throughout its edit ROI, emits no erase/block/sprite pixels, emits exactly one stable warning, and surfaces RPC `CompletedWithErrors`.
- For `automatic_strict`, source pixels, source geometry, and source topology are authoritative and sufficient. Let `Ω` be the target-owned integer page-pixel domain after subtracting protected/non-target ownership, and let `Q` be the nonempty intersection of `Ω`, the integer `source_transform` bbox, and the complete per-node source-ink support. Let `B=(Dilate8(Q,1)\Q)∩Ω`; all `Q/B` source pixels require alpha `255`. Build a fixed 5-bit RGB histogram on `Q` with key `(r>>3,g>>3,b>>3)`, order bins by `(-count,key_r,key_g,key_b)`, and use per-channel lower medians as representatives. P1 stops there. Only after E admission may it derive direct cores, one immutable candidate-independent `bg(q)` table, and candidate partitions. Exhaustively enumerate every one-core fill candidate and every ordered distinct fill/stroke core pair whose fill/background and stroke/background distances to every shared `bg(q)`, plus fill/stroke representative distance, are all `L1>=24`; enumeration order stabilizes diagnostics only and never resolves ambiguity.
- Revision 46 retains `source-color-work-budget-v1` and freezes `SOURCE_COLOR_MAX_CORES=64`, `SOURCE_COLOR_MAX_CANDIDATES=4096`, `SOURCE_COLOR_TARGET_WORK_UNITS=16_777_216`, and `SOURCE_COLOR_PAGE_WORK_UNITS=134_217_728`. `color_constant_set_sha256` hashes only the unchanged closed `hanonly-color-constant-set-preimage-v1`; `source_color_contract_sha256` hashes only the revised closed `hanonly-source-color-contract-preimage-v1` with `source-color-work-unit-v3`. Both use the exact no-whitespace/no-newline canonical UTF-8 JSON and lowercase SHA-256 rules normatively listed in the test specification; neither preimage contains an image, filename, manifest role, model output, elapsed time, translation digest, or runtime value. The external visual manifest is not either preimage. No image, filename, machine speed, model output, or elapsed runtime may affect classification.
- Accounting is checked `u64` and stage-gated before traversal/allocation. Let `J=page.nodes.len()` be the O(1) page-node count, `T` the automatic target count derived only by the admitted node enumeration, `A=page_width*page_height`, canonical target key `(anchor.top,anchor.left,anchor.bottom,anchor.right,NodeId)`, and `X_i` the clipped `source_transform` bbox area. P-1 first reads only `J`. If `J>SOURCE_COLOR_PAGE_WORK_UNITS`, `p0_metadata_unbounded/node_enumeration` hard-fails with requested/reserved/consumed `J/0/0`, no node traversal, target record, warning, or mutation. Otherwise reserve `J`, traverse every page node exactly once, derive the automatic-target collection and `T`, then checked-compute `Wterm=J+T`. Overflow is `p0_arithmetic_overflow/target_terminalization` with `null/J/J`; page-limit excess is `p0_metadata_unbounded/target_terminalization` with `Wterm/J/J`; both emit no target record. After atomically extending to `Wterm`, traverse the `T` targets exactly once to allocate terminal slots. Define total `choose2_checked(T)=0` for `T<2`; otherwise divide the even operand before checked multiplication: even `T` uses `(T/2)*(T-1)`, odd `T` uses `T*((T-1)/2)`. Then checked-compute `Wpair=choose2_checked(T)` and `Wmeta=checked(Wterm+Wpair)`. Pair or addition overflow, or page-limit excess, consumes/reserves only `Wterm`, emits one existing budget-unsupported record/warning per target, and performs no pair comparison. On admission, atomically extend to `Wmeta` and execute exactly `Wpair` unordered canonical-key comparisons. Admitted P0 requested/reserved/consumed is exactly `Wmeta`; `J`, `T`, and phase `node_enumeration|target_terminalization|canonical_ranking` are guarded diagnostics.
- P1 computes without pixel access `P_i=4*A+19*X_i+81_920`, the exact calculator value and conservative requested/reserved upper bound for admission. The four `A` terms reserve at most one per-target ownership comparison, `Ω` materialization, `Q/B` membership-storage operation, and compaction/validation operation per page pixel; the `19*X_i` terms reserve `Q` formation, eight fixed-order `B` neighbor probes, `Q` alpha/5-bit-histogram work, at most `8X_i` `B` alpha checks, and one further bounded `Q` pass that populates the admitted occupied bins' three 256-slot channel histograms; `81_920=32_768+768*64` reserves at most one full bin scan and at most 64 three-channel lower-median slot scans. Overflow or `P_i` above target limit rejects that target before pixel preflight. Checked-sum `Ppage_requested=Wmeta+ΣP_i` over remaining targets; overflow or page-limit excess rejects all remaining targets before pixel access. Only then reserve exactly `P_i` for each admitted target and scan row-major.
- P1 ownership uses minimum Manhattan distance to the anchor with canonical-key ties. It builds `Q` row-major and `B=(Dilate8(Q,1)\Q)∩Ω` with fixed neighbor order `NW,N,NE,W,E,SW,S,SE`, membership-bit dedupe, and row-major compaction. Empty/nonopaque `Q/B` uses the existing semantic unsupported reason. The fixed 32,768-bin histogram is scanned numerically. If occupied-bin count `H>64`, terminate `core_count` before per-channel histogram allocation or median work; otherwise populate only the at-most-64 admitted per-channel histograms in the separately charged `X_i` pass and derive each representative through actual visited 256-slot scans. `classifier_preflight_work_units_consumed=C_i` is the checked sum of only declared operations that actually execute; each real fixed operation or loop iteration increments its counter once. Unused `B` capacity, skipped iterations, unvisited histogram slots, work skipped after `H>64`, padding/no-op work, and synthetic counter increments consume zero, so `0<=C_i<=P_i` and equality is not required. Padding or synthetic inflation is `accounting_invariant_failure`. Page preflight consumption is exact admitted P0 consumption plus `ΣC_i`, while page requested/reserved remains `Wmeta+ΣP_i`. P1 does not compute direct-core membership, P95, core order, `bg(q)`, candidates, alpha witnesses, labels, or topology. It exposes only `N=|Q|`, `L=|B|`, `M=area(Ω_bbox)`, `H`, `K=H^2`, scalar-only `U=min(F_s-1,floor((min(width(Ω_bbox),height(Ω_bbox))-1)/2))`, bin counts, and representatives.
- `source_color_contract_sha256` binds the exact page aggregation, not only per-target formulas. Its canonical `accounting` key order is `evaluation,p0,p1,page,precedence,work_unit_version`; `p0.terminal` freezes requested/reserved/consumed values for `p0_arithmetic_overflow`, `p0_metadata_unbounded`, and `p0_page_limit`; and `accounting.page` freezes checked evaluation, preflight, and total requested/reserved/consumed sums. The test specification's closed JSON is normative. `color_constant_set_sha256` remains byte-for-byte unchanged.
- Stage E applies exact precedence `core_count`, then `candidate_count`, evaluation arithmetic overflow, target total limit, and page evaluation limit. `H=65,K=4225` is `core_count`; `candidate_count` is subordinate and unreachable under the current paired limits but remains schema-valid. Before candidate/witness allocation compute:

  ```text
  Wbg     = N*L
  Wcore   = H*N + 256*H + H
  Worder  = H*(H-1)/2
  Wfill   = 1_028*N + 3
  Wstroke = 3_071*N + 12*M + 7
  E_i     = Wbg + Wcore + Worder + H*Wfill + H*(H-1)*Wstroke
  R_i     = P_i + E_i
  ```

  `N*L` builds exactly one immutable table `bg(q)=argmin_{b∈B}(Chebyshev(q,b),b_y,b_x)` shared read-only by every candidate; no candidate-specific witness traversal exists. `H*N+256H+H` derives same-bin deviations/direct-membership bits, complete same-bin nearest-rank P95 histograms/results, and nonempty-core status; `H*(H-1)/2` derives total core order. `Wfill` charges one `N` fill/background separation pass, `254*N*4` fixed unrolled integer alpha-channel/residual updates, `11N` one-label BFS work, and three record checks. `Wstroke` charges `2N` fill/stroke-background separation, `3*254*N*4` three-pair alpha work, `21N` two-label BFS/tie validation, `12M` direct-Chebyshev topology over `Ω_bbox`, and seven record/interval checks. Individually rejected targets leave Stage E. The page checks `Epage_requested=ΣE_i` and `Rpage_requested=Ppage_reserved+Epage_requested`; only a passing page atomically reserves all E allocations/traversals. Targets/candidates run in canonical order, and page consumption is the checked sum of deterministic counters, never completion order.
- For integer alpha `a∈1..254`, every RGB channel is `blend=(a*foreground+(255-a)*background+127)/255` with checked integer arithmetic and floor division; residual is RGB `L∞` and must be `<=1`. Fill-only pair order is `(fill,bg(q))->{fill}`. Stroke pair order is `(fill,bg(q))->{fill}`, `(stroke,bg(q))->{stroke}`, `(fill,stroke)->{fill,stroke}`. The first item is the alpha-weighted foreground; no reverse `(stroke,fill)` exists. Any equal minimum residual attained by more than one pair order is `AmbiguousSemanticColorWitness`; pair order never resolves a cross-pair semantic tie. Within one winning pair, the smallest alpha is diagnostic witness. A unique fill/stroke pair permits both labels and therefore enters the two-label path proof.
- Candidate labels use fixed-order multi-source 8-neighbor BFS inside `Q`. Distances initialize once; queue removals and all eight edges are charged; shortest-path multiplicity saturates at `0|1|2`; equal-distance contributions do not requeue. A non-core pixel is valid only when exactly one compatible label has minimum distance and that label has exactly one shortest path. Cross-label ties, same-label multipath, unreachable cores, incompatible witnesses, or incomplete classification invalidate the candidate. Exactly one complete candidate yields `AutomaticStrict`; zero or more than one, including output-identical but semantically different candidates, is fail-closed `UnsupportedSourceColor`.
- Stroke width performs no `1..=U` scan. One multi-source 8-neighbor BFS over the full rectangular `Ω_bbox` computes direct Chebyshev distance `d(p,F)`. Let `lo=max(1,max d(s,F))` for `s∈S`; let `hi=min(U,min d(p,F)-1)` over `p∈Ω\(F∪S)`, or `U` when that outside set is empty. Independently verify `p∈S iff d(p,F)<=lo` for every `p∈Ω\F`; accept only `lo==hi` and set `W_s=lo`. `lo>hi` or `lo<hi` is `UnprovableStrokeWidth`. `classifier_width_probe_count` counts stroke candidates reaching this one interval proof, never enumerated widths.
- Complexity warning bytes are exactly `han_only.unsupported_source_color:classifier_work_budget_exceeded`; product-warning dedupe keys `(NodeId,ClassifierWorkBudgetExceeded)`, so the same bytes emit once per rejected target without appended node/limit detail. Internal overflow after admission, `consumed>reserved`, calculator/executor disagreement, or an uncharged traversal is `accounting_invariant_failure`: one hard RPC page failure, no unsupported warning, and no erase/inference/blob/renderer/Scene mutation.
- For each valid provisional candidate partition, reconstruction and label semantics remain unchanged. Before erase, the backend freezes the final resolved rectangle, font identity, writing mode, effects/padding, per-target fit, Planner-size outcome, group-min result, and `F_t`. No-stroke requires `W_s=W_t=0` and no ratio. Stroke requires `F_s>1`, the unique interval-proved `W_s`, and checked `W_t=floor((2*W_s*F_t+F_s)/(2*F_s))>=1`. The exact geometry/font/color/`F_t/W_t` combination must pass raster-fit, probe, ownership, checked retained logical-sprite reservation, independently checked actual-scale raster construction, complete translation/cluster/glyph/fill traversal, serial scratch release, and nonempty-alpha validation through the shared raster path before the complete page object is atomically published and before erase/inpaint. A translation-identity mismatch, final-layout failure after local fallback, cluster/control-coverage failure, glyph-validity failure, fill-traversal failure, or nonempty-alpha validation failure is a deterministic hard pre-write `geometry_or_font` failure. Only checked logical/actual-raster arithmetic or `usize` conversion failure, retained owner-rectangle/page-cap reservation failure, or fallible raster-surface-construction failure is a recoverable `geometry_or_font` failure. Both classes leave final Scene/History/reachable-blob/sprite state equal to `B_prepare`, retain upstream producer commits, and produce no publication, inpainter run/apply, erase, or downstream persistence; process allocator OOM/abort is outside the contract. A source-color or width representability failure is the existing unsupported-preserve-ROI result. Renderer receives immutable sprite bytes plus frozen `F_t` and `width_px=W_t` metadata unchanged and may only persist/composite once; it may not reshape, rerasterize, rerun fit/grouping/Planner-size selection/rounding/clamping/width derivation, or revalidate by a second pixel path. Prediction, Planner, default black/width, and `contrasting_stroke_color` cannot select any partition, ratio, or renderer value.
- Machine no-residue, geometry, style, width, and composite verdicts apply only to `automatic_strict` and `manual_override` targets. `unsupported_rotation` and `unsupported_source_color` instead require byte-identical Source/Inpainted/Rendered edit ROIs, zero erase/block/sprite pixels, and an explicit `CompletedWithErrors` warning. Human review decides only successful-mode inpaint texture plausibility and cannot replace or waive any machine verdict.
- `production-closure-v1` is a conservative source/build/enforcement-input closure, not a cfg semantic reducer. Its `common` section hashes canonical path plus whole normalized bytes for every tracked workspace `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, root/UI `package.json`, `bun.lock`, root `.oxfmtrc.json`, `Dockerfile`, `cliff.toml`, `.cargo/config.toml`, `rust-toolchain.toml`, all tracked regular files beneath `scripts/` except generated `scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json`, every tracked `.github/workflows/*.{yml,yaml}`, `.github/CODEOWNERS`, UI root build/config files `.oxlintrc.json`, `components.json`, `instrumentation-client.ts`, `next-env.d.ts`, `next.config.ts`, `openapi.json`, `orval.config.ts`, `postcss.config.mjs`, `tsconfig.json`, and `vitest.config.ts`, `crates/koharu/tauri.conf.json`, every tracked regular Rust file beneath each workspace production package's `src/` plus declared `build.rs` and statically resolved repository-local include outside `src/`, and every tracked non-test regular production UI file beneath `ui/app`, `ui/components`, `ui/hooks`, and `ui/lib`, including generated API files. Text normalization is only CRLF-to-LF; mixed Rust and checker files are hashed whole, so embedded test/evidence changes intentionally invalidate acceptance. Standalone Cargo test/example/bench targets, crate `tests/`, UI tests outside the named enforcement scripts, external evidence, untracked/ignored files, and paths outside those roots do not enter. This safety over-approximation may reject harmless test-only drift but cannot drop a field-, statement-, block-, platform-, feature-gated, checker, workflow, or ownership byte.
- The closure also carries target-independent `generator_lock_inputs_sha256` over Rust `1.97.0`/rustc commit, Cargo/bindgen lock identities, exact `LLAMA_CPP_TAG=b8935`, and exact archive `SHA-256=a1732727571c2ad6f94bd9e650de2bef299ca170501d0deb429d00acc8854896`. Target triple, host triple, Cargo/rustc versions, bindgen version, libclang identity, and logical generated filenames/content hashes belong only to `generated_targets[target]`. `koharu-llm/build.rs` must verify both downloaded and cached archives before extraction, reject an unverified preexisting source tree, and use a digest-qualified source directory. The trusted macOS T6 target and Ubuntu PR target may differ; they must share identical schema, `production_common_sha256`, and `generator_lock_inputs_sha256`, while only same-target repeats require identical generated bytes. Every final cell and `R/A/C` binds the trusted target entry; the PR check validates its own Ubuntu entry and emits exactly one native GitHub Actions notice annotation titled `HANONLY_PRODUCTION_CLOSURE_V1` whose message is the canonical closure JSON; T7 validates the check-run annotations endpoint, recomputes the PR entry, and writes both target entries into `G` on the exact `C` head without a commit-hash edge inside `C`.
- Each automatic node first computes an independent safe maximum; a horizontal group uses the minimum of those maxima.
- Page, protected Latin, other source, target overlap, raster geometry, node bijection, and atomic pre-write validation stay at least as strict.
- The production import contract is byte-sniffed PNG, JPEG, or WebP only; extension/MIME disagreement does not override valid bytes, and every other compiled decoder format is rejected before blob or Scene mutation. Canonical page geometry is oriented decoded RGBA8 after applying decoder-reported EXIF orientation. Source/render alpha is preserved; model RGB conversion composites transparent pixels over opaque white in one shared private helper. ICC/profile equivalence is not promised beyond the decoder's RGBA8 result.
- Every import path validates all files before one Scene Batch. Invalid, unsupported, corrupt, unreadable, over-limit, or mixed valid/invalid input leaves Scene, History, undo/redo, and page order unchanged. Raw upload/path bytes, decoded RGBA bytes, in-flight decode work, and decoded-cache bytes use checked arithmetic and one documented budget derived from the upstream decoder allocation contract and repository-supported page envelope, never from fixture dimensions; over-budget input fails explicitly.
- Rotated targets remain out of scope for rendering in this correction, but they must be restored safely, produce no rendered block/sprite, emit one warning per node, and surface RPC `CompletedWithErrors`.
- No production/runtime dependency, default-build public abstraction, compatibility shim, Scene/OpenAPI field, second renderer, learned visual scoring model, or sample-specific production constant is added. Two exact direct dev-only `koharu-app` edges, `rustix =1.1.4` with `fs` and `sha2 =0.10.9`, may be added solely to the ignored Rust evidence harness. One additional `koharu-llm` build-only `sha2 =0.10.9` edge may verify the pinned llama.cpp archive; all three packages already exist in `Cargo.lock`, and no new package/version/source/checksum is allowed. The build-input hunk may add `rust-toolchain.toml` pinned to `1.97.0`, add `LLAMA_CPP_ARCHIVE_SHA256` beside the existing tag in `.cargo/config.toml`, and make `build.rs` fail before extraction on digest drift. A default-off `hanonly-test-evidence` Cargo feature may expose narrow observational APIs solely to the harness; it is absent from all default/release feature sets and changes no inference behavior. A deterministic source-color contract/oracle, a test-only clean-reference residual metric, and a repository-wide policy audit are permitted. Existing generated schema shapes may remain permissive for full Scene/patch data, but external HTTP/MCP apply must be runtime-restrictive: forward-authoritative `typographyPlanVerified` presence is rejected before typed deserialization, and any optional generated-doc update must add no new/remove field.
- The required GitHub job is static and portable: it contains no model, Metal, external-image path, or `hanonly-test-evidence` workflow literal. It consumes a committed test-only digest corpus derived from accepted evidence; the trusted final matrix remains the authority for real-image and device acceptance.
- “Non-bypassable” covers every normal merge path enforced by the active repository Ruleset. The repository settings owner can still alter that Ruleset; organization-owned immutable workflow governance is a separate, materially larger follow-up rather than a hidden promise in this plan.

## Phase discipline

D0 through D3 are discovery and test-scaffolding phases only. They may add ignored-harness/report hooks, capture baseline evidence, add compiling tests that fail on current behavior, and run temporary local diagnostic mutations that are immediately reversed against the D0 dirty-file ledger. They retain no production behavior change. RED-A and RED-B must then fail against that recorded baseline before GREEN-A or GREEN-B/B0 may retain hunks.

Before G004 retains B0 production hunks, D2/D3 commit the exact seven T2 IDs marked only `#[ignore = "hanonly-pre-b1-red"]` and exact nine T3 IDs marked only `#[ignore = "hanonly-pre-greenc-red"]` from the test specification. The G004 pre-hunk gate first compiles the complete workspace. For each short ID it runs `--list --ignored`, requires exactly one module-qualified name with that final segment, then executes only that full name through `--exact --ignored` and proves exactly one test started and failed. A short substring is discovery-only and never the RED execution. Zero/duplicate matches, a passing test, setup/list/compile failure, multiple executed tests, a broad module/crate skip, an unknown ID carrying either marker, or one failure standing in for another is forbidden. G004 then removes only the two B0-owned T2 markers, proves those two tests fail unignored, implements Source Gate / PP-OCR recall-forward until those two pass, and leaves the remaining five T2 plus nine T3 markers intact. The checker then proves the Revision 50 `B0_SHA` marker/ID inventory before the default `bun cargo test --workspace --tests`, so the normal suite remains green while only those named future-phase tests are temporarily omitted.

B0-sensitive harness, parser, validator, feature, model/config/library fingerprint, candidate inputs, and the complete eight-path B0 interpreter are finalized and committed at a globally clean pre-B1 `B0_SHA`; only its detached `B0_WORKTREE` may run calibration-freeze and holdout. `B0_FROZEN_INTERPRETER_PATHS` is exactly `.omx/plans/hanonly-r50-b0-evidence-contract.json`, `scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts`, `scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.test.ts`, `scripts/hanonly_evidence_ledger.py`, `scripts/hanonly_evidence_ledger_test.py`, `package.json`, `ui/package.json`, and `bun.lock`. The B0 artifact frozen projection records the contract path, exact byte length, and SHA-256. The checker and ledger may import no additional repository-local helper; adding one requires adding it to this closed path set in a new reviewed plan revision.

A passing immutable B0 artifact authorizes GREEN-B/B1, then GREEN-C.
