B1's first test-only hunk removes only the remaining five `hanonly-pre-b1-red` attributes, proves those exact tests remain RED under default execution, and then implements them to GREEN. GREEN-C follows the same sequence for only the nine `hanonly-pre-greenc-red` attributes. After GREEN-C, remaining local governance and closure inputs outside the frozen interpreter, including workflow, CODEOWNERS, and non-frozen build/config/toolchain inputs, are finalized before the clean `IMPL_SHA`; none of the eight frozen interpreter paths or checker-contained closure rules may change from `B0_SHA`. Final acceptance first proves both endpoint commits, path existence, regular-blob type, mode `100644`, identical blob OID, and zero endpoint-tree diff for all eight paths; it then rejects either stage marker anywhere in tracked Rust source, requires every named ID exactly once without any `#[ignore]`, and runs the full default workspace suite before validating the B0 artifact without recalibration or reselection and running T5/T6/A. Only excluded `C`, external evidence, remote Ruleset state, and external `G` may change after T6. All production implementation instructions remain exclusively in the GREEN phases.

## RALPLAN-DR

### Principles

1. Keep recognition geometry immutable; derive erase, layout, color, and stroke-width contracts as private run-local backend data.
2. Fix each root cause once at the existing shared boundary. Add no production/runtime dependency, second renderer, Scene/OpenAPI field, runtime evidence service, compatibility shim, or visual-scoring model. The sole dependency exceptions are the three already-locked dev/build edges plus test-only ledger/closure tooling.
3. Successful targets require guarded machine proof before inpaint of complete target-text shaping/glyph/fill/nonempty-alpha rasterization plus checked sprite reservation, then machine proof of residue removal and exact frozen-sprite composition; unsupported targets require byte-identical preservation and explicit failure, while exported evidence remains a sanitized non-length-verdict projection.
4. Production behavior and acceptance scale from runtime geometry and independent sources. The backend alone computes automatic HanOnly pixel sizes; the UI displays Auto without guessing them, while PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlists remain aligned.
5. B0 authorization, frozen interpreter bytes, final T6 closure, candidate-head/test-merge identities, and the acyclic `R -> A -> C -> G` evidence chain remain causally ordered and non-retrospective.

### Decision drivers

1. Preserve causal, production-equivalent evidence from pre-B1 B0 through final `R/A/C/G`, the candidate head, and the required-check test-merge commit without self-reference.
2. Make pre-inpaint complete target-text rasterization and sprite reservation, source removal, target geometry, automatic style/width, frozen-sprite lifetime, and final composition deterministic and machine-verifiable across independent production inputs.
3. Deliver the smallest shared-boundary correction while preserving dirty-worktree state, manual sizing, AllText, format parity, and the explicit dependency/schema/renderer exceptions.

### Viable options

#### Option A: one private runtime geometry handoff in the existing pipeline (recommended)

- Keep PP-OCR public schema, source-coordinate mapping, and post-detection record semantics stable; B0 may change crop-local preprocessing and produce a new frozen validated detection set.
- Preserve expanded target components through one shared final erase-mask boundary.
- Resolve one non-overlapping, axis-aligned, obstacle-free owner box once per target and carry it in a crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout record; each qualifying one-inpainter page in GREEN-C embeds that record in its immutable `PageId`-keyed frozen object. Keep public `RenderBlockInput`, `PageRenderOptions`, and existing Source/recognition transforms untouched.
- Replace absolute C2 crop growth, backend `-5px`, backend `12..28`/`72px` automatic caps, and source-page OCR thresholds with B0-frozen crop-local Source Gate / PP-OCR detection preprocessing plus geometry-derived backend font ceilings; let the existing fitter/rasterizer decide any required target-size reduction.
- Keep the resolved-layout record and `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` crate-private and backend-only. Retire `eligibleSourceLayout`, `automaticSourceSize`, and `groupedAutomaticSourceSizes` from the HanOnly automatic UI path together with its `-5px`, `12..28`, `72px`, and source-box cap/deduction logic. When no manual font size exists, preserve the current Auto label and empty numeric value; manual sizing, AllText, public render inputs, and the PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist contract remain unchanged.
- Reuse BlobStore as the one bounded PNG/JPEG/WebP oriented-RGBA8 decoder/cache boundary, decode each import request serially under checked byte reservations, and apply existing removals plus all additions only once after complete validation.
- Carry the complete HanOnly Planner suggestion, including proposed font size, in the crate-private per-run `Arc<PipelineRunState>` scoped by `pipeline/mod.rs`; key frozen page results by `PageId`, define total `G/S0/Smax/G0/G1` semantics, record accepted/rejected/ignored-by-policy outcomes, and never write HanOnly Planner lines/style/verification to Scene or any public carrier.
- Carry one closed private HanOnly color mode in that same task-scoped state. Manual override remains explicit; automatic solid fill/stroke uses source-derived exact RGBA only after deterministic pre-inpaint validation; every ambiguous or unrepresentable source style is excluded from erase/render and reported unsupported.
- Enforce conditional existing-artifact order only for present producers in normal `pipeline::run`, defined as `spec.options.region.is_none()`. Font/Typography each permit zero or one producer. Renderer requires exactly one inpainter; without Renderer, zero or one inpainter is valid. One inpainter requires `prepare_and_freeze_hanonly_render_inputs(...)` exactly once for the current `PageId` immediately before it; that call completes final layout/shaping/glyph/fill raster, checked whole-page sprite reservation, nonempty-alpha validation, and atomic publication before any inpainter run/apply. Zero inpainters require zero builder/reservation/raster/publication for that page. Renderer consumes the exact frozen sprite after inpaint and performs zero shaping/raster work. Multiple ordering-critical producers and Renderer-without-inpainter reject before engine load. Reject region-bearing HTTP pipeline requests before job creation and region-bearing direct `pipeline::run` calls before scheduling/run-state creation, while preserving the public field/schema shape. Keep `pages.rs` as the sole allowed region-bearing repair path on its current direct inpainter execution with zero builder/publication and no task-local accessor call.
- Classify rotation before Source Gate and exclude it from color-mode completeness. For a supported source stroke, derive one unique integer `W_s`, scale it to final target size with the frozen checked round-half-up formula, and fail closed as `UnprovableStrokeWidth` when uniqueness cannot be proved.
- Reuse persisted Source/Inpainted-base/brush/sprite blobs, independently recompute protected support, and replay the production order `base -> Source protected restore -> brush -> ordered sprites` in a test-only independent compositor; require exact decoded final RGBA equality and require omitting each successful target sprite from a fresh replay to change at least one final pixel in that target's safe region.
- Reuse the existing production inventory through one conservative whole-file closure with a target-independent common/generator-lock partition and per-target generated records. Emit the trusted-target fields in every final cell and `R/A/C`; make the closure writer, PR CI, `A`, and T7 execute the same helper twice from clean linked worktrees with fresh target directories. `C` contains no commit SHA, so its post-T6 commit cannot create a reverse or self-reference edge; `G` alone binds trusted and PR target records to the authenticated head.
- Require every resolved rectangle to contain the source anchor with its center inside that anchor, and require the final sprite center to remain inside the same anchor.
- Predeclare the Revision 49 inherited crop candidates `S25L4`, `S25L5`, `S25L6`, and `S25L7` plus the frozen Source Gate / PP-OCR recall contract; select on four non-regression calibration sources including mandatory `c04`, freeze, then validate on four fresh disjoint holdout sources on CPU and Metal.
- Route nonzero rotation through the existing unsupported-geometry path, emit one `han_only.unsupported_rotation` warning per node, restore source pixels, produce no rendered block/sprite, and surface RPC `CompletedWithErrors`. Full rotated rendering is a follow-up.
- Reuse the existing `test.yml` workflow for one Windows prerequisite job and one uniquely named required policy job. The required `HanOnly Production Policy` job uses mandatory `needs`, job-level `if: ${{ always() }}`, and a first-step success assertion so a failed, cancelled, or skipped Windows prerequisite produces a failing required context rather than a skipped one. Protect both jobs, the checker, tests, and digest corpus through CODEOWNERS plus an active empty-bypass Ruleset rather than creating a second workflow or a new governance service.
- Freeze the exact eight-path B0 interpreter at both endpoint trees. Keep all sixteen future-phase behavior tests in `B0_SHA` under exact ID-specific ignore reasons, prove each one fails separately, remove each stage marker before its corresponding production hunk, and reject every marker at final.
- Pros: one policy per responsibility, backend-only automatic pixel authority, UI Auto/empty-state honesty, UI/backend format parity, strict no-guessed-color behavior, atomic replacement, bounded decoded cache, no schema change, source-independent tests.
- Cons: requires coordinated edits in Source Gate, PP-OCR preprocessing, BlobStore/RPC import, shared support/renderer pipeline files, and the existing UI preview helper; the eight frozen interpreter paths cannot evolve after B0 without restarting the plan revision, import decode becomes serial, rotated text remains explicitly unsupported, and richer visual styles can be preserved-with-error rather than translated.

#### Option B: keep absolute policies and add backend-local retries

- Let each inpaint backend grow masks independently, retain C2/`-5px`/font/OCR thresholds and current broad/count-cached import behavior, keep predicted/default/contrasting automatic colors, and shrink/retry sprites after validation failures.
- Pros: superficially smaller edits per function; little shared type work.
- Cons: three erase policies drift, retries rasterize repeatedly, source-size behavior remains absolute, the UI continues guessing pixels from source boxes without private runtime geometry, and the tight geometry remains duplicated. It is operationally viable but fails the source-independence driver.

#### Option B2: support fill-only automatic and fail every automatic stroke closed

- Treat any automatic stroke, shadow, or glow as `unsupported_source_color`, preserve its complete source ROI, and defer all stroke topology/width work to a separate revision.
- Pros: the smallest and safest production surface; removes stroke topology, scaling, width-unit, and probe complexity while retaining an absolute no-guessed-style guarantee.
- Cons: it evades this revision's strict product target that at least one independent topology-unique supported-stroke holdout must render successfully. This is the strongest simpler alternative, but it is rejected because shrinking the successful domain would not satisfy the requested source-style fidelity; shadow and glow still remain fail-closed under the selected option.

#### Option C: persist recognition/erase/layout boxes in Scene

- Pros: explicit provenance and UI visibility.
- Cons: violates the no-Scene/OpenAPI-field constraint, creates migration/generated-code work, and persists transient model evidence. Rejected by requirement, not merely preference.

#### Option D: implement full polygon-aware rotated erase and rendering now

- Pros: broader geometry coverage and no unsupported rotated cases.
- Cons: changes crop, ownership, fit, raster, compositing, overlap, and sprite-transform contracts at once; substantially increases blast radius before the axis-aligned production failures are fixed. Rejected for this bounded correction. Rotation is made explicit and measurable now, then handled by a separate approved plan.

## Implementation sequence

### D0: establish layered reproduction

**Root cause / hypothesis.** The incorrect Rendered image is produced from an already damaged Inpainted layer, while layout, Planner, and automatic fill/stroke color resolution add separate visual failures.

**Files/functions.** Add test-only, Python-standard-library-only `scripts/hanonly_evidence_ledger.py` plus `scripts/hanonly_evidence_ledger_test.py`; extend the existing ignored runtime matrix in `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs`; add exact test-only direct `rustix 1.1.4`/`sha2 0.10.9` dev edges in `crates/koharu-app/Cargo.toml` and only their `koharu-app` dependency-list entries in `Cargo.lock`; use existing engine test diagnostics in `ctd_segment.rs`, `support.rs`, `renderer.rs`, and `typography.rs`; observe import/decode/cache behavior in `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/blobs.rs`, and `ui/lib/io/openFiles.ts`. Add no new lock package record, production dependency edge, production field, or production import of the ledger tool.

**RED evidence.** On the approved test-only regression input, capture Source, raw Segment, final erase mask, Inpainted, Rendered, recognition anchors, erase support, layout regions, crop-padding decision, source-size estimate, applied size deduction, candidate/independent/group sizes, sprite alpha bounds, predicted/resolved fill and stroke RGBA, stroke width, color-resolution branch, rotation support status, and Planner field outcomes. Prove outline pixels lie outside the final erase mask, layout regions equal tight anchors, production crop uses absolute C2, non-Japanese/Korean target sizing applies a fixed `-5px`, automatic source sizing crosses the current `12..28`/`72px` clamps, PP-OCR preprocessing changes at the current source-dimension thresholds, automatic stroke ignores predicted stroke color for a contrasting color and missing color evidence can reach default black, replace import clears existing pages before a later decode failure, and decoded caching is bounded by entry count rather than bytes.

**Implementation (diagnostic only).** The first D0 scaffolding hunk implements and tests one Python-standard-library CLI with `create` and `rehydrate` modes backed by the same `os.open(..., dir_fd=...)`, `os.mkdir(..., dir_fd=...)`, and `os.rename(..., src_dir_fd=..., dst_dir_fd=...)` held-directory descriptor walker, `O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW`, same-descriptor type/SHA-256, mode/owner, closed-schema, containment, and NUL-output functions. Startup fails before mutation unless the target platform exposes all required flags and `dir_fd` operations. `python3 -m unittest scripts/hanonly_evidence_ledger_test.py` passes before any evidence directory or production hunk exists. Before its first `mkdir`, `create` proves canonical `pwd -P`, requires the caller-supplied owned mode-0700 canonical evidence base to be absolute and outside the original, implementation, B0, acceptance, and PR linked worktrees, validates the direct run-id child shape, and validates approved input/visual-manifest regular-file hashes from descriptors reached one component at a time. It reads the input descriptor once, hashes the immutable bytes, and obtains decoded dimensions with one bounded magic-selected header parser over those exact bytes. The parser supports the approved regression containers only: JPEG SOF dimensions and WebP `VP8`, `VP8L`, or `VP8X` dimensions; truncation, malformed lengths, unsupported format/chunk, duplicate or contradictory dimension records, and trailing records that alter the selected dimensions fail. Either representation must prove `790x1023`, and the ignored Rust harness must bind it to the approved decoded-pixel hash before model use; raw JPEG/WebP hashes may differ. `sips` and `/dev/fd` child traversal are not used. It also descriptor-walks and same-descriptor hashes the fixed tracked `crates/koharu-app/tests/fixtures/source-gate-deterministic-recall/fixture-manifest.json` and rejects any argument-vector, `shell=False` `git status --porcelain=v1 -- <fixed-path>` output before directory creation. Only the verified hashes enter the Revision 49 ledger schema inherited from Revision 46; media type and dimensions are revalidated from the same bytes during every create/rehydrate and harness run rather than added to the ledger schema.

After the read-only checks, `create` keeps repository/base descriptors open, creates each evidence-base/run/temp child relative to its held parent descriptor, and persists a deterministic canonical ledger through temp-file, rename, final-file fsync, run-directory fsync, and base-directory fsync. Existing exact run roots are accepted only by the closed idempotent recovery states absent/empty/exact deterministic temp/exact final; unknown entries, symlinks, wrong metadata, or byte mismatch fail without overwrite. Every injected write/rename/fsync failure emits zero bytes, and a same-argument retry must converge to one durable final ledger and no temp. Immediately before NUL output, both `create` and `rehydrate` fresh-walk from a held filesystem-root descriptor and require `(st_dev, st_ino, type)` plus owner/mode equality between every emitted/fixed path and its still-held repository/input/visual-manifest/fixture/base/run/ledger descriptor. A namespace replacement therefore cannot turn a successful old-inode write into a wrong exported path.

The mode-0600 JSON ledger has exactly the closed keys `version`, `visual_input`, `visual_input_sha256`, `visual_manifest`, `visual_manifest_sha256`, `source_gate_fixture_manifest_sha256`, and `evidence_root`; neither mode sources or evaluates data. The ignored Rust harness uses exact dev-only `rustix::fs::{openat,mkdirat,renameat,statat,fsync}` descriptor primitives and `sha2::{Digest,Sha256}`. It descriptor-walks every visual manifest, Source, Clean, and mask path, reads each opened descriptor exactly once, hashes those bytes, parses/decodes from the same immutable in-memory bytes, and creates cell outputs only after all validation; it never reopens a validated path. `--test-dependency-inventory` proves two exact app dev edges, one exact llm build-only `sha2` edge, two app lock-list additions, one llm lock-list addition, and no new package record; promotion, drift, misplaced edge, or extra edge fails. Add test-only/report-only hooks to existing diagnostics. Outside every worktree, persist the original dirty worktree's NUL status, tracked/staged binary diffs, untracked path/blob hashes, `HEAD`, exact `typography.rs` patch, and blob hash before implementation. Independently hash every planned Cargo manifest and `Cargo.lock`, and store one exact mode-0600 `pre-edit-Cargo.lock` byte snapshot plus its SHA-256/owner/mode/`(dev,ino,type)` metadata. The dependency inventory revalidates and parses that snapshot and permits only the three named dependency-list additions; every package/version/source/checksum record and every other dependency list must remain equivalent. Write reports/artifacts only beneath the canonical external evidence root. Do not attach an automatic trap to the root; only nested scratch may be removed. The verifier owns explicit cleanup after rollback evidence and human acceptance are checkpointed.

Before RED-A, freeze one test-visible `image-input-contract-v1` from current `image 0.25.10` decoder capabilities and repository product constraints: byte-sniffed PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist; decoder-reported orientation followed by RGBA8 canonicalization; white-background alpha composition only at model RGB conversion; checked `width*height*4`; one `512 MiB` decoded-byte ceiling matching the pinned decoder's current default `max_alloc`; one shared decoded-memory reservation budget for in-flight images plus cache; one aggregate encoded-import-byte ceiling; serial decode inside each import request; and a page-mask workspace preflight derived mechanically from page pixels and the maximum simultaneously live one-byte masks. The two byte ceilings may equal the pinned decoder default but are named safety invariants, not fixture-derived values. Record UI/backend allowlist parity and every retained limit source. If dependency drift changes the decoder default or the supported-page envelope cannot fit the budget, stop for a new reviewed contract instead of silently retuning from the regression image.

**GREEN evidence.** D0 itself is observational. Its report schema and frozen `image-input-contract-v1` become the before/after evidence used by every GREEN batch. The approved image is labeled `known_regression`, never `calibration_source`; no limit, format branch, font cap, or OCR scale is selected from it.

**Stop condition.** Stop if Source/Inpainted/Rendered provenance, backend identity, or predicted/resolved fill/stroke branch cannot be proven, the Python or Rust descriptor primitive/dependency inventory is unavailable, a path identity changes before output, any ledger fault cannot recover idempotently with zero failed-attempt output, or the image-input budget/format/orientation contract cannot be frozen independently of test dimensions; do not infer a rendering or color fix from final PNG appearance alone.

**Validation commands.** `bun cargo test -p koharu-app source_gate_real_crop_source_gate_runtime_matrix --lib -- --ignored --nocapture --test-threads=1` plus the new ignored HanOnly visual matrix filter described in the test spec.

**Rollback.** Remove only test-only diagnostics/report hooks, the exact two `koharu-app` dev edges, and their two lock dependency-list entries; no production behavior, production dependency, new package record, or data migration is involved.

**Risks.** Performance: report generation is ignored-test-only. Mis-erasure/protected/compatibility: none. Visual gap: D0 records evidence but does not judge inpaint texture quality.

### D1: prove the complete-ink clipping point

**Root cause / hypothesis.** Existing CTD refinement and inpaint expansion contain enough dynamic ink growth, but app-layer intersections at `ctd_segment.rs:88-90` and `support.rs:614-618` discard it.

**Files/functions.** `ctd_segment.rs::finalize_segment_mask`; `support.rs::{line_support_mask,prepare_inpaint_mask,intersect_gray_masks}`; `mask.rs::expand_mask_for_inpainting` and `expand_mask_to_bubble_region_for_inpainting` as evidence only unless RED proves insufficiency.

**RED evidence.** Parameterized component masks whose stroke/shadow extends outside tight OCR support must fail current coverage while protected Latin remains adjacent.

**Diagnostic procedure.** Add compiling table-driven fixtures that expose complete target components, tight line support, adjacent protected Latin, and each backend's current final mask. Confirm both named intersections are the first irreversible coverage-loss points. A temporary local mutation may remove one clip at a time only to distinguish causation, but it must be reversed and its before/after mask hashes retained before D1 closes. Do not retain production mask changes in D1.

**GREEN evidence.** Every target component/outline fixture is covered, canonical target/forbidden supports match across callers, every backend-specific result has zero protected/non-target overlap, and 0.5x/1x/2x/4x normalized support scales with geometry.

**Stop condition.** If removing tight clips exposes unrelated CTD components that cannot be separated using current target components, protected lines, and local geometry, stop before broadening support. Do not use PP-DocLayout/bubble/card fill as fallback.

**Validation commands.** `bun cargo test -p koharu-app ctd_segment -- --nocapture`; `bun cargo test -p koharu-app final_inpaint_mask -- --nocapture`; `bun cargo test -p koharu-ml inpainting::mask -- --nocapture`.

**Rollback.** Restore only D1 mask-selection changes; retained RED fixtures remain useful regression evidence.

**Risks.** Performance: at most one additional page-mask subtraction, target O(page pixels). Mis-erasure: component leakage; mitigate with target-component contact and final forbidden-mask subtraction. Protected Latin: require exact zero overlap. Compatibility: AllText and explicit repair-region branches remain untouched. Visual gap: coverage does not guarantee inpaint texture quality, which remains runtime/human acceptance.

### D2: prove tight recognition boxes and absolute policies cause undersized layout

**Root cause / hypothesis.** The current local fitter is correct for the box and cap it receives; blanket/single-line locking, source-bbox alpha validation, absolute C2 crop growth, backend blanket `-5px` plus `12..28`/`72px` automatic caps, UI source-box estimation with duplicated fixed policy, and PP-OCR's source-dimension-triggered 2x preprocessing give it geometry/policy that changes at arbitrary production resize boundaries. Separately, import and later blob loads do not share one bounded canonical decode contract.

**Files/functions.** `source_language_gate.rs::{safe_crop_bounds,crop_policy_parameters,compute_safe_crop_bounds,source_gate_candidates,test diagnostics}`; `koharu-llm/src/paddleocr_vl.rs::{context_params,test execution evidence}`; test-only existing staging-API binding/accessor work in `koharu-llm/{build.rs,src/safe/model.rs}`; `koharu-ml/src/pp_ocr_v5.rs::{word_box_inference_scale,word_boxes,test model evidence}`; `pipeline/engines/renderer.rs::{run_renderer_page,build_render_inputs,validate_and_composite_han_render_output}`; `renderer.rs::{render_page,resolve_layout_boxes,prepare_source_relative_automatic,fit_font_size_with_predicate,grouped_fitted_source_relative_font_sizes}`; `ui/components/panels/RenderControlsPanel.tsx::{eligibleSourceLayout,automaticSourceSize,groupedAutomaticSourceSizes}`; `ui/lib/io/openFiles.ts`; `crates/koharu-app/src/blobs.rs::{load_image,decode_blob,image cache}`; `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs::{create_pages,create_pages_from_paths}`.

**RED evidence.** Parameterized tests show a long translation selects a smaller font inside the tight anchor although a larger raster fits a collision-free surrounding region; equivalent geometry at `0.5x/1x/2x/4x` receives non-proportional context from C2, `-5px`, `12..28`, and `72px`; PP-OCR changes preprocessing across current `159/160/161` candidate-height and `1023/1024/1025` source-dimension neighbors. The UI estimator chain guesses an automatic pixel value from source/OCR boxes plus prediction/detection/fallback even though it cannot observe crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout records. PNG/JPEG/WebP format-equivalent fixtures, JPEG orientations `1/3/6/8`, opaque/alpha variants, and filename-extension mismatches expose decode-contract drift. A replace request with a valid first file and corrupt/unsupported/over-limit later file proves current Scene mutation; a cache/import stress fixture proves count-only and parallel decode memory is not bounded by decoded bytes. Reversing input order must expose no allowed successful-result variation.

**Diagnostic procedure.** Add baseline tests and report fields that compare the current locked source box with surrounding page/bubble/obstacle geometry and prove a larger actual-font raster could fit without collision. Before running any calibration output, freeze the current Revision 50 candidate IDs/formulas `S25L4`, `S25L5`, `S25L6`, and `S25L7`, every Source Gate crop rule, PP-OCR crop-local upscale rule, coverage threshold, acceptance rule, residual/pre-render coverage threshold, raw-evidence/parser schema, `image-input-contract-v1`, `source-color-contract-v2`, `source-color-work-budget-v1`, exact color/width/work constants and accounting table, production-closure schema/common/generator-lock contracts, policy-audit rules, required-check rules, and same-descriptor SHA-256 of the fixed tracked Source Gate fixture manifest. The manifest assigns exactly four non-regression full-page hashes to `calibration`, including mandatory `c04`, and four different non-regression hashes to `holdout`; the approved regression image, whether supplied as `test.jpeg` or its decoded-equivalent WebP, is `regression` and never selects a ratio, input limit, font ratio, OCR scale, coverage threshold, color/width/work threshold, or color/width/work algorithm. Record as RED that the current PaddleOCR-VL CPU context does not explicitly disable `offload_kqv`/`op_offload` and that requested flags plus available-device enumeration cannot prove actual execution. Add compiling fail-closed tests for post-load model-device/offloaded-layer/model-buffer evidence, MTMD backend, separate per-inference context/compute buffers, context flags, raw-log parsing, process/instance binding, and raw runtime node observations, including negative CPU fixtures with empty loaded-device evidence, missing maps, empty maps, and zero CPU bytes. Freeze the exact default-off `hanonly-test-evidence` feature bridge before B0: app feature propagation, dependency feature declarations, feature-only public FFI observational items, build-script binding gate, featureless dependency-test checks, release/build feature-inventory checks, generated-Rust capture/audit checks, the exact three-edge dependency inventory, exact command `bun scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts --b0-source-gate-anti-fixture`, and the dedicated two-phase B0 command/environment contract. Do not run candidate selection in D2 against unproven device evidence. GREEN-B subgate B0 performs the explicit CPU-context, feature-gated evidence enablement, and Revision 50 detector-support / Source Gate repair only; then it commits clean `B0_SHA`. Its detached worktree runs the required anti-fixture check, dedicated Source Gate-only calibration/freeze process, re-runs the required anti-fixture check before holdout, then runs a fresh holdout process, writes/fsyncs the Revision 50 frozen artifact including `b0_sha`, `source_gate_fixture_manifest_sha256`, required-check evidence, frozen recall-parameter evidence, B0 source-removal preflight / pre-render coverage evidence, and mandatory `c04` pass evidence, and passes holdout before B1 may retain any target layout/font/render/UI/input behavior. Full final Rendered erase/render/source-ink/residual/alpha/locality/color/width/work/composite/warning oracles run after GREEN-A/B1/C in T5/T6. Freeze integer half-open conversion, source-anchor locality, ownership tie-break, exhaustive-oracle expectations, backend-only source-size precedence, UI Auto/empty-state behavior, crop-local model preprocessing contract, import atomicity, decode/cache byte accounting, format-allowlist parity, and the one-box fit/validation contract as test vectors only. Do not add a field to public `RenderBlockInput` or `PageRenderOptions`, change public `render_page`, change target layout/font/render production behavior, remove locks, or retain decoder/renderer/UI production hunks in D2.

**GREEN evidence.** The crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout record, local fit, grouping, and post-render validation exclusively compute and consume `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1`; public `PageRenderOptions` is unchanged and is not repurposed as a carrier. For horizontal writing set `cross_extent=resolved_region.height`; for vertical writing set `cross_extent=resolved_region.width`; then compute `G=floor(min(page_width,page_height,cross_extent))` with checked finite positive inputs/conversion. `G` is only a geometry-derived integer search ceiling and contains no `GLOBAL_CAP_PX` or renamed fixed pixel cap. Let `Csrc=min(source_estimate,G)`. The no-Planner baseline `S0=largest_fit(Csrc)` and `Smax=largest_fit(G)` use the exact same resolved region, font, writing mode, stroke/effects/padding, and raster predicate, so `S0<=Smax` is asserted; no fit at the minimum positive renderer-valid size fails before mutation. For a group, `G0=min(S0_i)` is the no-Planner baseline and `G1=min(selected_i)` is final; each selected size must be at least its `S0_i`, proving `G1>=G0`. Every alpha pixel stays inside its owner region and outside all forbidden/target occupancy; the resolved-rectangle center and final alpha-bbox center remain inside the source anchor. Source crop padding scales proportionally before raster rounding, is frozen from four calibration sources, and passes four disjoint holdout sources on validator-derived CPU and actual Metal without retuning or executable/model drift. B0 freezes the PP-OCR crop-local integer upscale rule needed for Source Gate detection recall: it reaches a named model-input minimum candidate height, is bounded by checked model-input pixels and a named model maximum scale, ignores page width/height thresholds, maps selected boxes back to source coordinates, and must preserve normalized boxes/recall in adjacent-threshold plus `0.5x/1x/2x/4x` tests. B1 consumes that frozen recall contract but does not redefine OCR scale, recall thresholds, or acceptance rules. No automatic pixel value is exported to or reconstructed by the UI. `RenderControlsPanel.tsx` retires the HanOnly estimator chain and fixed deductions/caps; when no manual font size exists, it preserves the existing Auto label and empty numeric value. Manual sizing, public `Renderer::render_page(...)`, public `RenderBlockInput`, public `PageRenderOptions`, and AllText remain unchanged; UI/backend parity applies only to the PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist. Shared intake accepts only byte-sniffed PNG/JPEG/WebP, applies orientation and RGBA8 canonicalization once, uses serial request decode plus checked reservations and byte-budgeted LRU eviction, and stages all removals followed by additions in one `Op::Batch` only after every file validates. ### RED-A: real-image source-residue regression

**Hypothesis.** The approved image fails because complete target ink is outside the final erase mask, not because export uses the wrong layer.

**Files/functions.** D0 ignored harness and D1 mask path; no committed image or scene fixture.

**RED/GREEN.** RED records current uncovered outline pixels. GREEN requires top two and bottom three Han regions fully removed, protected Latin ROI hashes unchanged, and no damaged Inpainted/Rendered write.

**Implementation.** Run the T0 baseline diagnostic mode on the current available/default device and inspect every layer. Do not require the not-yet-implemented strict requested/actual AOT device evidence during RED-A.

**Stop condition.** If the final erase mask covers the full source ink but Inpainted still visibly retains it, stop D1 changes and isolate the selected inpaint backend/model instead of broadening the mask.

**Commands.** T0 ignored baseline diagnostic command only. The strict T6 CPU/Metal command block is reserved for post-GREEN final acceptance.

**Rollback/risks.** Roll back D1 only if coverage or protected invariants regress. Visual texture remains human-reviewed.

### RED-B: specification-independent layout regression

**Hypothesis.** A geometry-derived region and raster predicate scale across images; any fixed padding/font/ratio branch will fail the scale/aspect/order matrix.

**Files/functions.** Renderer tests and the private runtime region resolver from D2.

**RED/GREEN.** RED covers 0.5x/1x/2x/4x, aspect ratios, independent source hashes, writing modes, lengths, node distributions, bubble states, ordering, crop context, backend automatic-size behavior, UI guessed-pixel behavior, UI/backend format parity, locality, and rotated geometry. GREEN meets every T2 invariant without sample-specific constants or absolute non-quantization deductions; backend alone computes automatic pixels and UI shows Auto/empty. Rotated geometry is GREEN only when the source ROI is unchanged, no rendered block/sprite exists, one warning is emitted per node, `warning_count > 0`, and RPC reports `CompletedWithErrors`.

**Implementation.** Use generated masks/transforms and actual font raster ink, not screenshot golden pixels.

**Stop condition.** Reject any implementation whose normalized output changes with input order or depends on `790x1023`, the known source hash, five nodes, crop filenames/bounds, fixed padding, blanket `-5px`, fixed character/line counts, or specific NodeIds.

**Commands.** T2 filters plus ten-repeat loop and parallel test threads.

**Rollback/risks.** No crop/layout production rollback exists until GREEN-B/B1; tests remain. Main risk is over-constraining noisy bubble masks, covered by missing/empty-bubble cases.

### GREEN-A: minimal erase-support root fix

**Root fix.** Replace consecutive tight line-support intersections with one final dynamic target-component support, followed by forbidden-mask subtraction.

**Implementation.** Remove only the redundant tight app-layer clips proven by D1/RED-A. Starting from the aggregate CTD `GrayImage`, subtract canonical forbidden support first, then extract 8-connected nonzero components in stable raster order. For each complete component, compute eligible owners only from intersection/contact with the canonical per-node anchor/support set. Exactly one eligible NodeId receives the entire component; zero-owner components are dropped; a component with multiple eligible owners marks the page unsafe and fails before inference, rather than splitting by distance, input order, or a fixture-derived threshold. Build canonical per-node target supports plus one canonical forbidden support from the assigned components, page bounds, protected Latin, and non-target source geometry; derive the aggregate target support only as the union of the selected NodeId supports. This per-node representation is the later source-color checkpoint's authoritative ink evidence and allows unsupported-color nodes to be removed before aggregate/backend expansion without subtracting pixels owned by another target. Replace the current binary `Option`-style inpaint-mask handoff with the smallest private tri-state result that distinguishes prepared mask, no eligible Han targets, and unsafe empty/ambiguous ownership after forbidden subtraction. Only the no-eligible state may keep the existing zero-target/no-inference behavior; every unsafe state fails before inference, blob creation, or Scene ops. Let AOT/Lama retain glyph-only expansion and Flux retain region-fill expansion, then subtract the same forbidden support from each backend-specific result. Do not enlarge recognition boxes or fill whole bubbles/cards.

**Exact files.** Required: `ctd_segment.rs`, `support.rs`, `lama.rs`, `aot.rs`, `flux2_klein.rs`. Conditional only on D1 proof: `koharu-ml/src/inpainting/mask.rs`.

**Evidence.** T1, RED-A, complete nonempty per-node source-ink supports with deterministic 8-connected exactly-one ownership, zero-owner dropping, multi-owner page failure, exact union equality, canonical target/forbidden-support equality across engines, backend-specific final-mask subset checks, tri-state dispatcher tests, and atomic no-safe-mask failure. Adjacent and shared-component two-node fixtures run in reversed node order. Zero eligible targets remain a no-op/no-inference path; nonzero targets with no safe unambiguous component fail before inference or persistence.

**Stop/rollback/risks.** Stop on protected overlap or divergence in canonical support. Backend final masks may differ only by their already-established glyph-fill versus region-fill semantics. Revert this isolated batch. Performance must remain one bounded mask pass; no full bubble/card erase.

### GREEN-B: minimal dynamic layout-region root fix

The inherited Revision 46 color-contract requirement still applies to the current Revision 50 B0 `crop-policy-selection.json`: its frozen projection carries lowercase-hex `source_color_contract_sha256` and `color_constant_set_sha256` immediately after `image_input_contract_sha256`. The hashes are computed only from the exact closed canonical-JSON preimages `hanonly-source-color-contract-preimage-v1` and `hanonly-color-constant-set-preimage-v1` in the test specification. Calibration-freeze recomputes both before model load; holdout and final `IMPL_SHA` independently recompute and compare them. Missing/null/type/length/case/hex drift, one-nibble change, swapped hashes, phase mismatch, final mismatch, alternate preimage bytes, or the old Revision 45 contract hashes fail before model load, output access, B1 authorization, or artifact mutation.

**Root fix.** Execute GREEN-B as two ordered subgates. B0 proves the Source Gate calibration can distinguish explicit CPU from actual Metal using post-load/post-inference evidence from the same `PaddleOcrVl` instance and may repair Source Gate / PP-OCR recall only far enough to make source-language detection, source-text coverage, crop-local OCR recall, crop-local preprocessing, inverse mapping, coverage acceptance, and source-removal preflight production-generic. B0 then freezes the smallest all-pass source-coverage candidate before holdout. Only after B0 calibration, fsynced freeze, and disjoint holdout pass may B1 remove forced source-box locking, derive exactly one deterministic axis-aligned obstacle-free runtime rectangle per axis-aligned target, store it in a crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout record/map, and pass that map directly to a crate-private entry in the existing `renderer.rs` so fit and post-raster validation consume the same rectangle. Public `RenderBlockInput`, public `PageRenderOptions`, and public `Renderer::render_page(...)` remain unchanged. GREEN-C later embeds the same records in its page-complete frozen object. B1 consumes only the B0-accepted Source Gate / PP-OCR boxes, anchors, and coordinate mapping; B1 target layout/font/render behavior must not read candidate ID, candidate geometry terms, crop padding, test role, or corpus role. B1 removes backend absolute deductions/caps, retires the HanOnly UI automatic estimator chain while preserving Auto/empty state, makes PNG/JPEG/WebP intake canonical and byte-bounded, and routes nonzero rotation to explicit unsupported geometry through the existing warning result boundary. The page-level owner mask is only an intermediate allocation aid; it is never a second fit/raster contract.

**B0 implementation and gate.** Add `hanonly-test-evidence = []` to `koharu-llm` and `koharu-ml`; add `hanonly-test-evidence = ["koharu-llm/hanonly-test-evidence", "koharu-ml/hanonly-test-evidence"]` to `koharu-app`. It is absent from every crate's `default` feature list and from release/build feature surfaces. Every cross-crate observational struct/accessor/log hook, including all llama-ext-dependent items, uses feature-only `#[cfg(feature = "hanonly-test-evidence")]`; it is `pub` only under that gate, carries no setter or behavior switch, and is absent from default and ordinary dependency-unit-test artifacts. Dependency-local helpers that do not reference the bridge may remain `#[cfg(test)]`. The app ignored harness uses `#[cfg(all(test, feature = "hanonly-test-evidence"))]`. `koharu-llm/build.rs` checks `CARGO_FEATURE_HANONLY_TEST_EVIDENCE` before including/binding staged `llama-ext.h`, exactly matching every FFI consumer; default `koharu-llm`/`koharu-ml` unit tests run without the feature. Make `PaddleOcrVl` retain the existing CPU request and pass it into context construction. CPU construction explicitly uses `n_gpu_layers=0`, `mtmd_use_gpu=false`, `offload_kqv=false`, and `op_offload=false`; GPU-requested construction explicitly uses the existing default GPU-layer policy with `mtmd_use_gpu=true`, `offload_kqv=true`, and `op_offload=true`. In the dedicated feature-enabled ignored `--test-threads=1` harness only, serialize process-global llama/ggml/mtmd log capture and report shaped loaded-model device records, offloaded/offloadable layers, model-buffer bytes by backend, MTMD/CLIP backend, per-inference context flags, and separate context/compute-buffer byte maps from the same instance ID. PP word-box inference remains explicitly recorded as rten CPU. No component may self-report an authoritative `actual_device` or pass result; the validator derives both from raw facts and fails on missing, empty, zero-byte, duplicate, mixed, unknown, unparsed, or contradictory evidence. CPU derivation requires a nonempty all-CPU loaded-model device set and strictly positive CPU bytes in each model, context, and compute map; negative tests reject every vacuous form. B0 may change Source Gate crop-local recall, PP-OCR model-input scaling, inverse mapping, and source-coverage acceptance only for source-removal preflight. B0 changes no target layout rectangle, target font policy, target renderer behavior, UI behavior, color migration, rotation behavior, or default public schema/API.

**B0 execution contract.** Finalize every B0-sensitive harness/parser/validator/feature/model/config/library fingerprint and candidate input, including all eight `B0_FROZEN_INTERPRETER_PATHS`, then commit a globally clean `B0_SHA` that contains GREEN-A and B0 plus the Revision 50 detector-support / Source Gate repair, but no B1 target layout/font/render/UI/color behavior. Those eight complete endpoint blobs are bound by `B0_SHA` and cannot change through final `IMPL_SHA`. Create a detached clean `B0_WORKTREE` at that exact SHA. Add one dedicated ignored filter, `han_only_source_gate_crop_selection_matrix`, separate from the post-GREEN T5/T6 full-pipeline filters. It accepts only `HANONLY_SOURCE_GATE_SELECTION_PHASE=calibration-freeze|holdout`, exact `HANONLY_B0_SHA`, an absolute `HANONLY_SOURCE_GATE_SELECTION_ARTIFACT` below the canonical evidence root, an absolute `HANONLY_SOURCE_GATE_SELECTION_REPORT_DIR` below that root, and an absolute phase-matching `HANONLY_SOURCE_GATE_REQUIRED_CHECK_ATTESTATION` below that root; relative/root/escaping paths, unknown phase, local `HEAD != B0_SHA`, any B1 target-rendering hunk, missing D0 manifest ledger, dirty fixed fixture manifest, same-descriptor fixture-hash drift, missing/wrong-phase/wrong-B0/wrong-endpoint/wrong-result required-check attestation, or dependency inventory drift fail before model load. The required checker mode may be added or extended in the frozen checker path. The artifact path must not exist for `calibration-freeze` and must be the fsynced regular file produced by that phase for `holdout`.

**B0 module boundary.** Keep this small. The static checker owns production-policy scanning only; the artifact validator owns only artifact schema, required-check evidence, frozen recall contract, hashes, and authorization decisions; the shared B0 runner is stateless and reused by the two thin entry phases `calibration-freeze` and `holdout`. CI corpus, workflow, CODEOWNERS, and Ruleset checks stay in their existing final checker modes. Any new repository-local helper for checker, validator, ledger, or B0 runner must be added to `B0_FROZEN_INTERPRETER_PATHS` and the owner table in the same reviewed revision.

Before either phase may create an artifact, the ordered pre-B0 gate must pass: full evidence-ledger rehydration; complete workspace compilation; unique `--list --ignored` resolution of each staged short ID to exactly one module-qualified libtest name; execution of only that full name through `--exact --ignored`; proof that exactly one test started and failed for the remaining staged IDs; checker validation of the exact B0 marker/ID inventory; default workspace tests/check; exact three-edge dependency/lock inventory; featureless dependency tests; policy-audit tests plus release-feature inventory; exact default/evidence Cargo JSON capture for generated Rust; `bun scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts --b0-source-gate-anti-fixture` with `HANONLY_B0_REQUIRED_CHECK_PHASE=pre-calibration` and an attestation output path; then the feature-enabled app reachability test. The B0 runner must descriptor-open that pre-calibration attestation before calibration model load. A short substring is discovery-only and cannot execute staged RED. Run two sequential fresh test processes with the exact same `--features metal,hanonly-test-evidence` and filter.

`calibration-freeze` runs all four candidates across four calibration entries, including mandatory `c04`, and both validator-derived devices, selects the smallest all-pass candidate, writes the complete pre-holdout artifact with `plan_revision:50`, `b0_sha`, empty/null holdout fields, frozen fixture-manifest hash, required-check attestation evidence from the pre-calibration anti-fixture command, frozen recall-parameter evidence, per-cell PP/VL coverage counts, explicit `rejected_after_vl:false`, explicit `pp_vl_incomplete_coverage:false`, B0 source-removal preflight / pre-render coverage evidence, and the two lowercase SHA-256 values recomputed from the exact closed color-contract preimages, fsyncs file and parent directory, and exits. `holdout` reopens and validates that frozen artifact before model load, reruns the anti-fixture command and consumes its attestation before model load, hashes its own executable/feature/model/library/parser/fixture-manifest/image-input-contract/source-color-contract/work-budget/recall-contract set, independently reconstructs both canonical preimages and digests, requires exact equality with calibration, runs only the selected candidate across four disjoint holdout entries and both devices, appends only allowed holdout fields plus the pre-holdout required-check attestation evidence, fsyncs file and parent, and revalidates the frozen projection. The validator then reopens the completed artifact through the guarded descriptor path, requires exact `B0_SHA`, passed holdout, `retuned_after_freeze:false`, both required-check attestations successful and hash-bound, frozen recall-parameter equality, B0 source-removal preflight / pre-render coverage pass for every calibration and holdout cell, no `rejected_after_vl` or `pp_vl_incomplete_coverage` in any selected calibration or holdout cell including mandatory `c04`, and exactly one selected Revision 50 candidate in the frozen recall contract, and returns its whole-file SHA-256 for the durable execution ledger. The artifact is never rewritten after that digest is frozen. Final `IMPL_SHA` acceptance first passes the eight-path endpoint-tree byte gate and final RED-state gate, then requires the same artifact bytes/digest, both exact preimage-derived hashes, exact frozen recall contract, and accepted source-coordinate boxes/anchors but never invokes the selection filter.

Any B0-sensitive or frozen-interpreter change, dependency/feature leak, non-exact staged-RED command, ambiguous full test name, generated-Rust binding/audit drift, fixture or input/color/work-contract/preimage drift, required-check failure, calibration/device/freeze/holdout failure, or missing `c04` pass evidence invalidates the artifact, rolls back every B0 feature/API/build/context/harness/recall hunk, emits no B1 authorization, forbids same-revision retuning, and ends Revision 50.

**B1 implementation.** Convert finite Source/obstacle transforms to clamped integer half-open boxes with `floor(min)` and `ceil(max)` and reject invalid anchors. Build allowed pixels from page bounds minus protected Latin/non-target Source support. A nonempty connected bubble component may enlarge one target's domain only when it contains the complete anchor; missing, empty, or non-containing bubble evidence makes the original source anchor itself the complete domain, with no base-page or invented-padding fallback. When a trustworthy bubble exists, assign its allowed pixels by minimum Manhattan distance with `(top, left, bottom, right, NodeId)` ties. For each resulting owner mask, or the anchor mask in the no-bubble branch, use row-histogram/monotonic-stack maximal-rectangle search, retain only rectangles containing the full anchor whose rectangle center lies inside the anchor, and select by descending `(area, primary-axis extent, secondary-axis extent)`, then ascending center displacement and lexicographic coordinates; compare generated small masks with an exhaustive oracle using the same locality filter. Convert each winner directly into the crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout map, discard owner masks, and fail before writes if no rectangle exists. Keep existing `source_transform` and `transform` unchanged, remove blanket/single-line locks only after the private map exists, stop requiring translated-line count to equal OCR-line count for a validated same-node runtime hint, preserve manual locks, and pass the same private record to fitting and post-render alpha validation through the crate-private HanOnly renderer entry. Define the final sprite center as the center of its integer half-open nontransparent alpha bounding box; both coordinates must satisfy the source anchor's half-open bounds. Source Gate computes padding, OCR scaling, inverse mapping, and coverage acceptance only in B0; B1 may consume the accepted boxes/anchors but may not read candidate ID, candidate geometry terms, crop padding, corpus role, OCR scale thresholds, or Source Gate acceptance rules when deriving target layout/font/render behavior. For HanOnly automatic sizing, the private resolved-layout map plus backend fit, grouping, and post-raster validation exclusively own and consume geometry-derived `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1`, with no absolute pixel clamp, cap, floor, or blanket deduction; actual-font fit remains the reduction authority. Public `PageRenderOptions`, `RenderBlockInput`, and `render_page` are unchanged and carry none of this data. The UI deletes or stops calling `eligibleSourceLayout`, `automaticSourceSize`, and `groupedAutomaticSourceSizes` in the HanOnly automatic path and preserves the existing Auto label with an empty numeric value. Manual sizing and AllText remain unchanged; UI/backend parity is required only for the PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlists. B1 consumes the B0-frozen `word_box_inference_scale` / recall contract only to interpret accepted source-coordinate detections and must not redefine OCR scale, recall thresholds, inverse mapping, coverage acceptance, or Source Gate acceptance rules.

Add one production image decoder boundary in `koharu-app` and make both RPC import routes plus `BlobStore::load_image` use it. It content-sniffs and rejects anything except PNG/JPEG/WebP, creates an `ImageReader` decoder with the frozen `Limits`, reserves checked oriented RGBA bytes before allocation, reads decoder orientation, decodes, applies orientation, and returns canonical RGBA8 plus detected allowed format and oriented dimensions. Internal raw-RGBA blobs retain their exact wrapper path but undergo checked header-length and `width*height*4` validation under the same budget. A single app helper beside that decoder produces white-matte RGB for every app engine/model call, including Source Gate/PP-OCR and the three inpaint wrappers; source/render blobs preserve canonical RGBA. Replace the 64-entry LRU policy with eviction by tracked decoded RGBA bytes, reserving in-flight plus cached bytes under one `BlobStore` budget and releasing reservations on every success/error path. Import requests enforce aggregate encoded bytes, decode serially in natural order, and retain only decoded metadata/blob refs until all candidates pass.

GREEN-B also introduces the one shared staged `Op::Batch` helper and whole-log copy-on-write History commit boundary required by replacement atomicity. Before the persistence permit, acquire History then Scene once to capture coherent `expected_epoch`, Scene bytes/epoch, History canonical-log/undo/redo inputs, and the other read-only staging inputs that define `B_prepare`; release Scene then History. Finish every asynchronous/body/decode/engine operation, clone Scene and the complete Batch tree, apply every child through the real recursive path against staged clones, populate staged inverses, compute the proposed next epoch/undo/redo, and serialize the complete `LogFrame` plus length prefix without retaining either staging guard. Then acquire item 18's one session permit as the outermost publication boundary and recheck poison only. Promote required Blob objects without History or Scene locks and release Blob cache/file-local locks. Next acquire History exactly once within the permit-held publication boundary and perform exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison; then acquire Scene and revalidate required Scene state. A stale epoch at this point returns the optimistic-concurrency/stale outcome with poison false and no Scene/History/success publication; exact verified promoted objects may remain only as `unreachable_cas`. With the guards held, read the canonical old generation into immutable bytes, parse it with existing compatible startup semantics, record exact raw bytes, and define `old_valid_log_bytes` as the accepted prefix ending after the last complete decodable frame; reject internal corruption and omit from the new generation only an ignored malformed/truncated trailing frame. Build solely `old_valid_log_bytes + new_frame` in one same-directory task-owned `create_new` temp, write/flush/`sync_all`, and strictly replay it to exact EOF from a separate fresh clone; exactly the new next-epoch frame applies once and equals staged Scene/next epoch. Then call item 16's adapter without reacquiring the gate. Only `Committed` publishes real Scene, canonical writer, epoch, and undo/redo while the inner locks remain held. `Unchanged` preserves every `B_prepare` observation. `Indeterminate` stores item 18's poison before releasing the permit and returns its exact fatal reason without staged publication. Release Scene then History, synchronously publish any caller response/Dirty/autosave/job success while still holding the permit, then release the permit; no `.await` or Scene SSE broadcast occurs in this boundary. Do not use inverse apply or truncate rollback. On restart, read canonical bytes once and strictly replay on a disposable fresh clone; only malformed/truncated legacy-tail failure may discard it and use compatible replay on a second fresh clone. Reject every other failure. Never concatenate generations, inspect task temps, reuse probe state, guess, or double apply. Successful sync calls establish the strongest host ordering; no absolute power-loss guarantee is claimed.

Both import routes build one final Batch with removals first and additions second and execute it through this helper. Their prior public-field/direct-Blob calls migrate to ProjectSession gated methods; one outer permit covers the complete import publication rather than allowing a separate Blob gate and later History gate. Every Blob/CAS write uses item 17's permit-aware `durable_put_exact`: common same-directory temp create/write/flush/`sync_all` plus exact temp verification; Unix rename, exact canonical verification, and parent-directory `sync_all`; Windows related-handle release, overwrite rename, canonical reopen/file `sync_all`, and exact canonical byte/hash verification with no directory-fsync claim. For equality, `reachable_blob_state(B)` is the canonical sorted map of exact references, lengths, and opened bytes/content hashes reachable from current Scene, committed/replayable History op roots, and current undo/redo op trees. Physical objects outside those roots are `unreachable_cas`: they are excluded from `B_prepare` equality and separately reported with sorted IDs, sizes, content verification, and before/after delta. A verified immutable object completed before a failed History commit may remain only as an unreachable GC candidate and may not be read by Scene/replay/undo/redo; task-owned temps are removed, while corruption, symlink, or wrong owner/type is a separate CAS-integrity failure. Blob failure returns before History, releases the permit without success publication, and never poisons; History `Unchanged` preserves observable state; only History `Indeterminate` stores poison under the still-held permit. GREEN-C reuses these boundaries and may not replace them.

The same shared memory reservation service accounts peak live bytes across decoded/cache RGBA, canonical Source/Inpainted pages, page masks, cloned/staged Scene and blob metadata, retained logical sprites, the one supersampled Pixmap, any unavoidable source copy, downsample output, and transaction buffers. Every width/height/stride/byte sum and `usize` conversion is checked before allocation. Full-surface `to_vec`/copy sites are eliminated where ownership can move; any remaining copy and destination are reserved together and created fallibly. Inputs inside the frozen supported page envelope must either fit this complete budget or return typed `geometry_or_font` resource failure before erase/inference/Scene mutation; allocator OOM is not an accepted boundary result for admitted inputs.

For unsupported geometry, replace the undifferentiated private rejection with `UnsupportedTextGeometryReason::{Rotation,ExistingUnsupported}`. Classification checks finite nonzero node/text rotation first and records `Rotation`; every other current `eligible_text_lines` failure preserves current behavior under `ExistingUnsupported`. In `pipeline/mod.rs`, remove the page-start collection that runs before `EngineCtx` exists. Retain page-local `unsupported_seen` for tracing and add separate `rotation_warned: HashSet<NodeId>` for product warnings. Immediately after each `EngineCtx` is constructed and before it is moved into `engine.run`, collect the current scene; trace all newly unsupported nodes, and for each `Rotation` whose NodeId newly enters `rotation_warned`, call `ctx.warn(format!("han_only.unsupported_rotation: node={}", node_id))`. This pre-run placement observes rotated candidates before Source Gate can remove them. After applied ops, collect again and route any newly created rotation message through that step's same `engine_warnings` sink because `ctx` has been consumed; the shared `rotation_warned` set guarantees exactly once. `ExistingUnsupported` remains tracing-only. Restore source pixels, emit no rendered block/sprite, and let the existing sink increment `warning_count` and RPC map it to `CompletedWithErrors`; add no public result field or counter. A mixed same-page test with one rotated and one non-rotation unsupported node proves only the rotated node emits the exact prefix once.

**Exact files.** B0 model owner: `crates/koharu-llm/Cargo.toml`, `crates/koharu-llm/src/paddleocr_vl.rs`, `crates/koharu-llm/build.rs`, `crates/koharu-llm/src/safe/model.rs`, `crates/koharu-ml/Cargo.toml`, and the evidence plus production crop-local preprocessing/inverse-mapping hunks in `crates/koharu-ml/src/pp_ocr_v5.rs`. B1 model owner has no authority to redefine PP-OCR scale, recall thresholds, inverse mapping, or Source Gate acceptance; it may only consume the B0-frozen contract.

**Evidence.** B0: exact metadata/lock inventory proves the two app harness packages are direct dev-only edges, llm `sha2` is build-only, the lock changes only three dependency lists, and no new lock package exists; every named future B1/GREEN-C test compiles and fails separately under its exact stage marker while the default workspace suite remains green; featureless `koharu-llm` and `koharu-ml` unit tests prove no cfg/build mismatch; default workspace check plus release-feature inventory prove feature absence; Cargo JSON logs bind production-reachable generated Rust to the exact default/evidence builds and the full audit finds no corpus literal; feature-enabled app check proves the cross-crate bridge compiles; an app-level reachability test reads one real dependency snapshot; the clean pre-B1 `B0_SHA/B0_WORKTREE` and dedicated two-process Source Gate filter produce exact Revision 50 `crop-policy-selection.json` bound to `B0_SHA`, complete calibration/holdout cells, same-instance post-load/post-inference facts, non-vacuous validator-derived CPU/Metal, empty/missing/zero evidence rejection, frozen-projection equality, immutable descriptor-derived artifact digest, unchanged eight-path interpreter blobs, unchanged feature/executable/model/library/fixture-manifest/parser/image-input-contract/color-preimage fingerprints, two unignored passing B0-owned tests, remaining staged-RED inventory, and both required anti-fixture check executions. B1: T2, RED-B, that exact frozen artifact/digest, frozen recall contract, accepted source-coordinate boxes/anchors, backend-only `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` tests, UI HanOnly Auto/empty-numeric tests, retired-estimator/fixed-policy absence checks, unchanged manual-size/AllText tests, UI/backend PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist parity, source-threshold-neighbor OCR tests that consume but do not redefine the B0-frozen recall contract, anchor-only missing/empty/non-containing-bubble and page-edge locality tests, PNG/JPEG/WebP plus orientation/alpha/extension-mismatch canonicalization tests, corrupt/unsupported/over-limit/mixed replace atomicity tests for both routes, nested late-failing Batch/History tests, blob temp/verify/fsync/rename fault tests, complete peak-live byte-budget/cache-eviction/error-release tests including retained+Pixmap+copy+downsample+staging coexistence, explicit rotation warning/`CompletedWithErrors` test, existing safety regressions, ten repeats, and restart stability.

**Stop/rollback/risks.** Stop before B1 if B0 cannot prove same-instance CPU/Metal from actual post-load/post-inference facts or if calibration, freeze, required-check, or holdout fails; roll back B0 and end the revision. After B1 starts, stop if missing/empty/non-containing bubble evidence expands beyond the source anchor, a safe region cannot be represented privately, rectangle/sprite locality fails, B1 reads or redefines the B0-frozen recall contract, any fixture-derived size/limit enters production, UI emits any estimated automatic HanOnly pixel or copies/exposes private layout policy, a format/orientation/reload path bypasses the shared decoder, peak-live memory accounting omits a simultaneously live buffer or can overflow/leak/double-reserve, an admitted supported input can reach allocator OOM instead of pre-allocation rejection, a failed staged Batch/replace mutates Scene, History, undo/redo, broadcast, or reachable blob state, rotation produces a rendered block/sprite or fails to produce exactly one warning per node plus `CompletedWithErrors`, or AllText/group/manual behavior changes. Revert the B1 layout/input/Batch/History/backend/UI/warning batch together so preview/runtime/result policy cannot drift; B0 recall-forward rollback remains separate and owns OCR scale, crop-local preprocessing, inverse mapping, and Source Gate acceptance. Only a verified unreachable CAS object explicitly enumerated by rollback evidence may remain, and no page state may change. Retain B0 only when its independent evidence gate still passes. GREEN-C depends on B1; if a B1 defect is found after C lands, revert C first and then B1. If the defect invalidates B0 selection evidence, also revert B0. Reject repeated raster retry loops or input-order-dependent allocation.

### GREEN-C: constrained Planner and visual-style root fix

**Root fix.** Carry the complete HanOnly Planner lines/style/font-size suggestion in a fresh private pipeline-run hint, accept insertion-only line breaks only when removing them reconstructs the captured originally single-line translation, and update renderer handling accordingly. Persist no HanOnly Planner op, marker, or color contract, so protected-source classification remains on the original Scene translation. Record per-field acceptance and keep local font authority/fallback. For horizontal writing set `cross_extent=resolved_region.height`; for vertical writing use `resolved_region.width`; compute checked `G=floor(min(page_width,page_height,cross_extent))`, with no `GLOBAL_CAP_PX` or renamed fixed cap. Define `Csrc=min(source_estimate,G)`, `S0=largest_fit(Csrc)`, and `Smax=largest_fit(G)` in the exact same resolved region/font/writing/stroke/effects/padding/raster predicate; assert `S0<=Smax`, and let Planner select only a normalized integer in `S0..=Smax`. Define group baseline/final sizes as `G0=min(S0_i)` and `G1=min(selected_i)` and assert `G1>=G0`. For HanOnly, an unmarked stored style is manual and remains highest priority; a stored style with `typography_plan_verified=true` is ignored entirely as a render-style input and never enters the manual tier. For every remaining automatic node, derive `source-color-contract-v2` under `source-color-work-budget-v1` from canonical Source pixels and complete source-ink support before inpaint, independent of font-prediction availability, and require renderer fill/stroke bytes to equal the source contract exactly. Planner/default colors, `contrasting_stroke_color`, partial candidates, and elapsed-time recovery are not fallback options. If a contract cannot be proved, represented, or admitted under the frozen work budget, classify the node as `unsupported_source_color` before erase and preserve it. AllText keeps its existing persisted Planner behavior. Enforce raw marker-presence rejection before HTTP/MCP deserialization and the closed persisted-marker table on the actual core apply path, Renderer, inverse, and untrusted-import boundaries.

**Implementation.** In `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pipelines.rs`, `start_pipeline` rejects `req.region.is_some()` immediately after extraction and before session lookup, options/spec construction, `spawn_pipeline_job`, job/cancel registration, event publication, or task spawn. It returns one HTTP 400 directing repair callers to the existing atomic page-mask repair route; `StartPipelineRequest.region`, serialization field shape, and `options_from_request` mapping remain unchanged. Update the route comments and response documentation, regenerate the normal OpenAPI/Orval artifacts, and truth-sync `docs/{en-US,ja-JP,zh-CN,pt-BR}/reference/http-api.md` so none claims region-bearing pipeline execution is usable. As the first executable statement in existing `pipeline::run`, reject `spec.options.region.is_some()` before `infos_for_spec`, `build_order`, page/Scene access, run-state creation, engine load, warning, builder, publication, or mutation; this protects direct non-RPC callers while preserving `PipelineRunOptions.region`. RPC requests stop at the route guard and therefore never encounter the run guard, so one request produces one error and no duplicate lifecycle event.

In `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs`, when `params.pipeline` is present, perform the sole repair-engine guard before body-empty/decode checks, blob persistence, session/Scene read or snapshot, engine cache/load/run, and `app.apply`: require `role == MaskRole::Segment`, resolve `Registry::find(engine_id)`, and require `engine_info.produces == &[Artifact::Inpainted]`. Unknown IDs, non-Segment roles, and Renderer/Typography/other/multi-artifact descriptors return one stable HTTP 400 with zero side effects. Do not hardcode the current inpainter IDs. Preserve `PutMaskParams.pipeline` type/serialization shape, the successful mask-plus-engine Batch, and the existing repair options; truth-sync the stale route comment and OpenAPI 400 behavior documentation. Only a descriptor passing this guard may reach the direct `region.is_some()` inpainter bypass.

For accepted `spec.options.region.is_none()` runs, add one crate-private per-run `PipelineRunState`, one crate-private `tokio::task_local!` key, and one crate-private non-panicking `try_with` closure accessor in `pipeline/mod.rs`. `pipeline::run` creates exactly one `Arc<PipelineRunState>` per invocation; the state contains mutex-protected `(PageId, NodeId) -> HanOnlyTypographyHint`, a `PageId`-keyed store of immutable page-complete frozen HanOnly sprite objects, and a separate optional AOT device label. The exact public `EngineCtx` fields/re-export and by-value `Engine::run(&self, EngineCtx<'_>)` signature remain unchanged, and no public options/input/state type receives this Arc. The typography hint stores the original translation, proposed lines, proposed style, proposed font size, and per-field outcome. `HanOnlyColorMode` is the closed enum `ManualOverride | AutomaticStrict(SourceColorContract) | UnsupportedSourceColor(SourceColorReason)`. Validate typography hints only for originally single-line text, one Source-safe region, insertion-only `\n` bytes, finite bounded font-size input, and locally safe raster placement.

Make crate-private `pipeline/engines/renderer.rs::prepare_and_freeze_hanonly_render_inputs(...)` the only builder and only production HanOnly layout/raster invocation. After all selected upstream producers have committed and immediately before this call, capture `B_prepare` as observable Scene bytes/epoch; History epoch, exact canonical-log bytes, and undo/redo state; exact `reachable_blob_state(B_prepare)`; and persisted sprite inventory. `reachable_blob_state(B)` is the canonical sorted map of exact blob references, lengths, and opened bytes/content hashes reachable from current Scene, committed/replayable History op roots, and current undo/redo op trees. Physical objects outside those roots are `unreachable_cas`; they are excluded from every `B_prepare` equality verdict and appear only in the separate orphan/integrity audit. The builder receives the same Arc and current `PageId` directly, rechecks the captured original from that pre-inpaint Scene snapshot, computes `S0/Smax` with the same predicate, applies the exact Planner outcome table, resolves `G0/G1`, derives `f_t_raw/F_t`, combines that result with the provisional source partition to derive `W_t`, and constructs the final translation through the shared crate-private HanOnly raster path. Before any retained target sprite allocation or raster call, canonicalize the complete target order; for every successful target checked-compute `logical_sprite_bytes_i=checked(logical_width_i*logical_height_i*4)`, checked-convert logical dimensions and byte count to `usize`, prove the surface is contained by its disjoint owner rectangle, checked-sum every `logical_sprite_bytes_i`, and require the total to be at most `checked(page_width*page_height*4)`. Reserve the entire retained logical target set before allocating any frozen sprite. Then serially and exactly once per successful target: checked-compute the actual existing `raster_scale_i` in `2..=4`, `raster_width_i=checked(logical_width_i*raster_scale_i)`, `raster_height_i=checked(logical_height_i*raster_scale_i)`, and `raster_surface_bytes_i=checked(raster_width_i*raster_height_i*4)`; checked-convert every raster dimension/byte count to `usize`; construct the transient surface through the existing fallible surface constructor; hash the expected/frozen/raster-entry bytes; build and validate the insertion-only break plus cluster/control transcript; reject glyph zero and nonzero out-of-range glyphs; execute the fill raster pass; require complete fill traversal and nonempty alpha; downsample/freeze the logical sprite; and release that target's supersampled/copy/downsample scratch before the next target starts. Never sum transient surfaces as retained logical bytes or keep more than one target scratch surface live. Freeze immutable sprite bytes, placement, transcript, source mode, `F_t/W_t`, actual raster facts, scratch-release verdict, and final probe/ownership/safety result. Publish exactly one complete page object only after every target and both retained/transient checks succeed. A translation-identity mismatch, final-layout failure after local fallback, cluster/control-coverage failure, glyph-validity failure, fill-traversal failure, or nonempty-alpha validation failure is a deterministic hard pre-write `geometry_or_font` failure. Only checked logical/actual-raster arithmetic or `usize` conversion failure, retained owner-rectangle/page-cap reservation failure, or fallible raster-surface-construction failure is a recoverable `geometry_or_font` failure. Both classes drop all unpublished buffers/reservations/scratch before inpainter run/apply or erase; final observable state equals `B_prepare`, neither class adds builder publication or downstream persistence, and neither rolls back nor alters earlier upstream commits in that baseline. Process-level allocator OOM/abort is not a recoverable geometry/font verdict. The low-level out-of-range glyph-ID error in `crates/koharu-renderer/src/renderer.rs` remains defense in depth in this same shared pre-inpaint raster path.

All three inpaint engines first bypass task-local lookup when `ctx.options.region.is_some()`; because both pipeline entry guards and the pages descriptor/role guard reject every other source of that value, only the accepted direct repair route can reach this branch, preserving zero-builder/zero-publication behavior. Otherwise they consume the current page's supported/unsupported NodeIds through the scoped accessor. After `Inpainted`, a selected Renderer invokes the crate-private HanOnly entry in the existing `renderer.rs` and only persists/composites each already-frozen sprite exactly once in canonical production order. It verifies frozen-object/sprite identity but performs zero translation rebuild, layout, shaping, cluster/glyph validation, raster, fit, grouping, Planner-size selection, rounding, clamping, or width derivation. On Renderer success the pixel payload is released immediately after persistence/composition; on inpainter failure Renderer is skipped and the payload is released with zero sprite/blob persistence; on a valid no-Renderer branch the payload is released after successful inpaint. Terminal non-pixel metadata may remain for diagnostics until run-state drop, but no page's pixel buffers may accumulate into the next page. The closed legal no-ink control table is the only input-byte coverage that may produce no glyph. A focused static/unit contract proves the shared fill `render_pass` traverses every layout line/glyph, while the Renderer-entry contract proves zero layout/raster calls. The existing public `Renderer::render_page(...)`, `RenderBlockInput`, `PageRenderOptions`, and `TinySkiaRenderer::render(...)` signatures remain compatible and are not repurposed. Remove the private HanOnly path's exact translated-line-count gate only for this validated same-node preflight; ordinary unvalidated mismatches on the public path retain safe source restoration.

Before reading or hashing any manifest target text, the test-only evidence harness generates one run-scoped `target_correlation_id` per validated manifest target from 128 bits of operating-system cryptographic randomness and encodes it as exactly 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters. IDs are unique within the acceptance run, stable for that target across all 40 cells, nondeterministic across reruns, and generated independently of text, NodeId, path, position, length, digest, or image content. Persist the target-to-ID mapping only in the guarded mode-0600 evidence root; no production pipeline record, Scene field, or renderer input stores the ID. Detailed local T0/T5/T6 rows may use that mapping and retain text-derived digests/counts, but the exporter performs a closed-schema projection: `R` and `A` may expose only correlation-ID-keyed booleans for translation identity, cluster/control coverage, glyph coverage, fill traversal, composite replay, and omission; `C`, the workflow annotation, closure summaries, and `G` contain no target text proof fields or mapping.
