A path/field/value scanner fails generation if any exported artifact contains raw text, stable NodeId, source/OCR/translated line counts, text-derived digest, text/byte/scalar/cluster/glyph length or count, or the guarded mapping.

After rotation has been terminally classified and Source Gate has accepted an axis-aligned target, and after GREEN-A Segment support has produced complete per-node source-ink evidence, but before any inpaint backend can commit an erase mask, run the exact exhaustive source partition and source-width algorithm frozen in `Preserved invariants` and repeated normatively in the test specification. Explicit unmarked manual style records `ManualOverride` and bypasses source-color equality validation. The provisional automatic source partition uses only canonical Source RGBA8, page-space `source_transform`, target ownership, protected/non-target masks, and complete per-node source-ink support. It must enumerate every 5-bit histogram core candidate and every complete fill-only or fill/stroke partition, classify every `Q` pixel exactly once through the frozen background/AA/path/topology rules, and accept only one complete valid partition. `transform`, font prediction, Planner data, target text, filenames, device, manifest role, and regression/calibration/holdout values are forbidden inputs to that source partition. Shadow and glow are terminal unsupported effects. `F_s>=1` is mandatory before any provisional result; stroke additionally requires `F_s>1` and one topology-unique `W_s`. The same pre-inpaint checkpoint then resolves the independent final layout/font/group/Planner-size result, defines `F_t` in integer page pixels, computes no-stroke `W_s=W_t=0` or checked stroke `W_t`, validates the combined frozen result, and only then publishes `AutomaticStrict`. Missing/extra classification, multiple valid partitions, invalid samples, missing/invalid `F_t`, invalid `W_t`, or any arithmetic/unit failure records a closed `SourceColorReason` unless the failure is the existing hard layout/font pre-write failure. Prediction-to-contract distance may be recorded only after the mode is immutable.

P-1, P0, P1, and E each reserve before the work they authorize. Read only `J=page.nodes.len()` before admission; reserve `J` before the one node enumeration that derives `T`; extend to `Wterm=J+T` before target-terminal slot creation; and extend to `Wmeta=checked(Wterm+choose2_checked(T))` before canonical pair comparisons. Per-target diagnostics buffer until page termination and record exact `classifier_terminal_state`; target `classifier_preflight_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed`, `classifier_evaluation_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed`, `classifier_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed`, and `classifier_target_work_units_limit`; plus page `classifier_page_node_count`, nullable `classifier_automatic_target_count`, `classifier_p0_phase`, `classifier_page_preflight_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed`, `classifier_page_evaluation_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed`, and `classifier_page_work_units_requested|reserved|consumed|limit`. `H/K/U` are null until derived, action counts are zero before execution, requested is null only when an earlier stop or overflow prevents derivation, and unadmitted reserved/consumed values are zero. Pre-terminal failures reserve/consume `0` or `J`; canonical-ranking failures reserve/consume `Wterm`; admitted P0 is exactly `Wmeta`; no snapshot is double-counted. `T=0/1/2`, pair multiplication boundary/overflow, and the separate final-addition overflow are mandatory. For admitted P1, requested/reserved is the exact conservative `P_i`, while consumed is the independently counted real declared operation total `C_i<=P_i`; unused upper-bound slack is not consumed, and padding/no-op/synthetic increments hard-fail. Final page values are canonical totals repeated identically in every emitted automatic-target record, including canonical-ranking rejects; enumeration/terminalization failures emit no target record. Limit kind is one of `none|core_count|candidate_count|target_work_units|page_work_units|arithmetic_overflow`; elapsed microseconds are diagnostic only and excluded from verdicts, retries, equality, fingerprints, hashes, selection, and tuning.

The same pre-inpaint checkpoint removes every `UnsupportedSourceColor` node from canonical erase ownership and final inpaint masks before inference. It retains byte-identical Source pixels in the node edit ROI and uses exact budget-warning bytes `han_only.unsupported_source_color:classifier_work_budget_exceeded` with a `(NodeId,reason)` product-warning dedupe key; other reasons retain their frozen message contracts. Renderer consumes the mode once: `ManualOverride` follows the existing explicit-style path; `AutomaticStrict` passes the exact frozen rectangle/font/`F_t`/RGBA/`W_t` to the existing stroke/fill renderer and never calls fit, grouping, Planner-size, rounding, clamp, Planner/default/contrasting color, or width resolution; `UnsupportedSourceColor` restores Source defensively and emits no block/sprite. `resolve_stroke_style` must stop silently discarding `fontPrediction.stroke_color` from diagnostics, but predicted fill/stroke/width never select final automatic values. The existing renderer is exercised in test-only fill-only and stroke-only probe configurations to validate exact color bytes and width; no new production render pass, API, or dependency is added.

Before building the order, HanOnly spec validation permits zero or one selected producer independently for `FontPredictions` and `TypographyStyles`, rejects more than one, and applies the total Renderer/Inpainted matrix: Renderer selected requires exactly one `Inpainted` producer; Renderer absent permits zero or one; more than one always rejects. Keep every registered engine's static `needs`/`produces` descriptor byte-for-byte unchanged, including `aot-inpainting`, `lama-manga`, and `flux2-klein`. Only for accepted normal HanOnly runs, `pipeline::run` adds private conditional order constraints from each selected Font/Typography producer to the sole builder, from the builder to the selected inpainter, and from that inpainter to Renderer. Missing optional producers omit their edge. AllText and the direct Segment repair route use the unchanged global DAG; no new `Artifact` variant, synthetic producer, or unconditional engine is introduced.

One pure item 19 backend table is evaluated at both ingresses. `start_pipeline` first performs existing region rejection; `spawn_pipeline_job` then evaluates admission before request-step Registry preflight. Unsupported requests skip Registry but still create one normal job and emit nothing at RPC precheck. Admitted requests retain existing synchronous unknown-ID HTTP 400 before job creation. Direct `pipeline::run` repeats the same decision after its first-executable region guard and before `infos_for_spec`, order/page/Scene/blob/History, run-state, model, or engine access. Only direct run emits exactly one stable warning and returns `warning_count=1`, producing `CompletedWithErrors` once. Explicit CPU is admitted everywhere; macOS non-CPU is admitted as requested Metal and still requires actual-Metal B0/T6 proof; Windows/Linux/other non-CPU fails closed. AllText is admitted unchanged. This is request/platform admission, not a pre-load actual-device probe.

In `pipeline/mod.rs`, after the first-line region guard and when the validated matrix contains one selected engine whose `produces` includes `Artifact::Inpainted`, call `prepare_and_freeze_hanonly_render_inputs(...)` exactly once for each qualifying page when policy is HanOnly, passing the per-run `Arc<PipelineRunState>` and current `PageId` directly and using the same Scene snapshot with no intervening engine invocation. The builder reads Source, Segment, eligible-node geometry, protected/ownership masks, manual-style provenance, and transient typography hints; computes the private resolved-layout records, provisional source partitions, final fit/group/Planner outcomes, `F_t/W_t`, complete translation/cluster/glyph/fill evidence, per-ink-bearing-shaped-cluster/run alpha contribution, complete peak-live reservation, immutable sprite bytes plus normalized frozen `sprite_transform` and `rendered_direction`, nonempty alpha, and final safety; then atomically publishes the complete immutable preflight object under that page key. A page with no selected inpainter or any preflight failure creates no entry. Partial maps/sprite sets are never visible.

For a one-inpainter row without Renderer, the selected inpainter may commit through the same ProjectSession-gated atomic engine path after successful publication. For a Renderer+Inpainter row, acquire History then Scene before the permit, capture coherent `B_prepare` plus authoritative `expected_epoch`, release Scene then History, clone/stage the page Scene, run/apply inpainter only against staging, feed that staged Inpainted state and the same frozen sprite to Renderer, and stage every sprite blob plus normalized transform, direction, final Rendered blob, and ops before acquiring the persistence permit. After all `.await` work and the final cancellation check, acquire one item 18 permit, recheck poison only, promote every sprite/final object through item 17's permit-aware `durable_put_exact` with no History/Scene lock held, release Blob-local locks, acquire History exactly once within the permit-held publication boundary, perform exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison, then acquire Scene and revalidate required state before committing one History Batch through item 16 without reacquiring the gate. Release Scene and History, synchronously publish the job success/Dirty result, and only then release the permit. Cancellation, engine/Renderer failure, Blob failure, Batch cloned-apply failure, or a stale epoch found after Blob promotion discards staging and preserves `B_prepare` reachable state; exact verified promoted objects may remain only as `unreachable_cas`, and stale epoch never poisons or publishes success. History `Unchanged` also preserves `B_prepare` and emits no success. History `Indeterminate` stores poison before permit release, suppresses every success, invokes item 18's fatal lifecycle, makes the job `Failed`, and is never recoverable equality. Renderer remains sole writer of sprite/normalized transform/direction and never writes `Node.transform` or source/OCR geometry.

For every related Typography, selected inpainter, selected Renderer, and feature-gated AOT-observation invocation, construct the existing `EngineCtx` unchanged and await a scope binding `run_state.clone()` plus the current `PageId` around `engine.run(ctx)`; engines read state only through the crate-private `try_with` accessor. The builder's direct Arc/`PageId` handoff is the sole deliberate non-accessor use. Builder, inpainter, and Renderer must observe the same Arc, current-page frozen-object identity, and frozen sprite identity. Access fails before entry, after scope, for another or completed prior page, and after run completion. Every terminal path releases pixel payload and transaction scratch before the next page. Small immutable terminal metadata may remain inaccessible to later-page scopes until run drop, but pixel buffers do not accumulate across pages. Concurrent `pipeline::run` invocations use distinct Arcs; nested/reentrant runs on one task install a distinct inner Arc and restore the outer after completion. Because `tokio::spawn` does not inherit this task-local, no carrier-dependent work may access it from a spawned child or retain Arc/state-derived mutable handles after the scoped future; all such work remains in and is awaited by that future. The Arc and all page records drop when `pipeline::run` returns. `prepare_inpaint_mask` receives the current page's frozen supported automatic/manual NodeId set and excludes unsupported-color nodes before backend-specific expansion; an empty supported set follows the existing no-inference Source-clone path. Only direct `pages.rs` repair can reach an inpainter with `region.is_some()`; that Segment branch bypasses this accessor and matrix before lookup, preserving its legal direct behavior with zero builder/publication. Region-bearing HTTP or direct pipeline calls have already failed without side effects. Missing Segment/source/layout/font evidence, ambiguous component ownership, translation/cluster/glyph/fill/cluster-alpha failure, peak-live reservation failure, an incomplete node bijection, an unknown typed reason, an invalid frozen result, duplicate same-page publication, wrong/stale-page access, cross-page contamination, surviving payload/scratch, repair accessor use, a matrix-count mismatch, a public engine/render-input change, scope leakage/cross-run state, or any Renderer layout/raster rebuild fails before committed erase or Scene effects.

Under exact `hanonly-test-evidence`, `koharu-ml` exposes only `#[cfg(feature = "hanonly-test-evidence")] #[doc(hidden)] pub fn device(&self) -> &candle_core::Device`; no accessor, trait, wrapper, or device-label method exists in its default build. Every app import, call, classifier helper, run-state write, and test consuming it uses the same exact feature gate. App classification is private observational `cpu|metal|cuda` only and cannot affect engine selection, inference, retries, or output. The feature-enabled existing app AOT instance records its device before inference and repeated page labels must agree; default workspace/release builds contain no reference. Add the shared raw `parse_external_op_json(Value)` boundary for HTTP/MCP. Reject either marker value at `/addPage/page/nodes/<id>/kind/text/typographyPlanVerified`, `/addNode/node/kind/text/typographyPlanVerified`, and `/updateNode/patch/data/text/typographyPlanVerified`, recurse through `/batch/ops/<index>`, and ignore only `/removePage/prev_page`, `/removeNode/prev_node`, and `/updateNode/prev`. Do not remove or add public schema fields for this marker; if a source comment is updated for OpenAPI clarity, regenerate only the normal generated artifacts and assert the behavior remains schema-permissive/runtime-restrictive.

Add one private marker guard to both actual `Op::apply` and `Op::validate`, and route both through the shared staged Batch helper already implemented and accepted in GREEN-B. `Op::validate` invokes that helper on clones and discards the staged result, so validate/apply semantics cannot drift. GREEN-C adds no second staging algorithm or History boundary. Mixed and nested late marker failures snapshot Scene, op tree, epoch, exact log bytes/hash/length, observable undo/redo outcomes, and blob inventory; all stay unchanged on error, while a successful Batch proves populated inverses and exact undo/redo. Non-Planner edits of marked state capture old style+marker, clear or replace style, and set false; trusted true requires same-op nonempty style; undo/redo restores exact state; Renderer omits style/marker; untrusted import clears marked style+marker while preserving translation. `support.rs` receives only the bounded source-color extractor and closed reasons after its GREEN-A/B1 owners have checkpointed their hunks; no concurrent shared-file edit is allowed.

**Exact files.** `crates/koharu-app/src/typography.rs`; `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/engine.rs` only for locking unchanged public `EngineCtx`/`Engine::run` and static descriptors; `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs` for region rejection, additive downstream-public backend admission, task-local/page state, gated staged transaction, poison propagation, and tests; `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/engines/typography.rs`; the bounded support hunk; the three inpainter files only for prior masks/repair/support/device handoffs; `crates/koharu-ml/src/aot_inpainting/mod.rs`; production plus colocated tests in `crates/koharu-core/src/op.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/history.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/blobs.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/session.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/app.rs`, and `crates/koharu-app/src/autosave.rs`; `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/history.rs`; `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pipelines.rs` for region/backend preflight, admitted unknown-ID 400, unsupported job lifecycle, behavior comments, and unchanged-field test; existing renderer/builder files; and `crates/koharu-renderer/src/renderer.rs`. The exact direct-field caller migration closure is `crates/koharu-app/src/session.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/app.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/ai.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/bin/pipeline.rs`, `crates/koharu-app/src/pipeline/mod.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/binary.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/mcp/mod.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/psd_export.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs`, `crates/koharu-rpc/src/routes/projects.rs`, `tests/integration-tests/tests/binary.rs`, `tests/integration-tests/tests/pipelines.rs`, and `tests/integration-tests/tests/scene.rs`. Every hunk in that closure, including the same-module `ProjectSession::open_untrusted` structural audit, is exclusively integration/source-policy owned. Existing public request/Scene data-model/Engine/`EngineCtx`/Renderer and BlobStore put signatures remain compatible. The doc-hidden backend admission function is explicitly additive downstream-public, and removal of public `ProjectSession.scene/history/blobs` is explicitly downstream-breaking with replacement read-only/gated methods. Generated behavior documentation is regenerated. No second renderer or public evidence API is planned.

**Evidence.** T3 covers all four normal-pipeline (`region.is_none()`) Renderer/Inpainted matrix rows; every valid zero/one Font/Typography permutation and HanOnly-only conditional order while asserting every static inpainter descriptor and AllText order are unchanged; rejection of Font/Typography/Inpainted counts above one; Renderer/no-inpainter rejection before engine load; no-Renderer/no-inpainter success with zero builder/publication per page; one-inpainter success with one immediately preceding `PageId` publication; same Arc/current-page frozen-object identity when Renderer exists; duplicate/partial/wrong-page publication rejection; and zero Renderer builder/recompute calls. Extend existing staged ID `hanonly_pre_greenc_red_t3_run_state_lifetime_contract` without renaming it to lock the exact public `EngineCtx` field set/re-export and by-value `Engine::run` shape; prove direct region-bearing `pipeline::run` rejection before registry/order/pages/run-Arc/engine/builder/publication/accessor/warning/blob/history/Scene work; fail `try_with` outside scope, after completion, and for wrong/prior pages; isolate sequential/concurrent runs; prove nested same-task shadow/restore; prove spawned children do not inherit the task-local; reject any Arc/state-derived mutable handle that outlives the scoped future; and run a two-page `Scope::WholeProject` table covering one inpainter without Renderer, one with Renderer, and page-one failure at every inpainter/Renderer/blob/commit boundary followed by page-two success and final run-state drop. Run that same existing contract once in the default feature set and once with `hanonly-test-evidence`, write public-only output reports, and require byte-identical Scene/blob/Rendered bytes, status, warnings, transforms, directions, and public error tokens; feature-only diagnostics are excluded from comparison. Extend existing `http_options_inherits_source_text_policy` without renaming it to preserve exact region conversion and prove a region-bearing HTTP request returns one 400 before session/job/cancel/event/task/Scene effects. Upgrade the existing `repair_brush_engine_ctx_keeps_single_engine_path` test, retaining its name, to execute the real Segment route under default `AppConfig`/HanOnly with a nonempty region and prove direct inpainter execution, mask-plus-engine atomicity, zero builder/publication, and no accessor invocation. T3 also covers exact transient-hint reconstruction/lifetime tests, zero-HanOnly-Scene-op checks, protected-source classification, raw HTTP/MCP presence tests, direct-apply marker guard tests, Renderer omission, persisted style clearing with inverse/undo/redo and untrusted import, rotation/color-mode domain tests, closed color-mode/reason tables, source-sample alpha/uniformity/separation/ambiguity boundary tests, checked `F_s` round-half-up neighbors/nonfinite/overflow, empty/clipped `Ω`, AA core-distance tie/unreachable/residual failures, unique thin/thick and `UnprovableStrokeWidth` zero/multiple/overflow topology fixtures, reduced-ratio and checked round-half-up `W_t` tests, predicted-stroke-loss regression, exact source-contract RGBA/width resolution, outline full-width and bitmap-radius unit probes, terminal shadow/glow behavior, pre-inpaint unsupported-color removal, warning dedupe/status mapping, AllText regressions, manual-style tests, approved-image plus independent holdout color reports, exact work-reservation formula/boundary/overflow tests, target/page complexity-preservation tests, and generated high-core/high-candidate/large-`U`/aggregate-page stress tests with reversed-order equality. Extend existing staged ID `hanonly_pre_greenc_red_t3_source_color_contract`; after B1 and before the first GREEN-C production hunk, prove ordinary non-ignored `han_only_source_color_work_budget_pipeline_contract`, `han_only_source_color_work_budget_completed_with_errors`, and `han_only_source_color_work_budget_adversarial_stress` RED, then make all four GREEN without timing thresholds, external images, or fixture-specific constants. The adversarial test accepts and prints a CI-runtime seed and generates image dimensions, geometry, node order, text length, colors, transforms, and PNG/JPEG/WebP-equivalent in-memory payloads after build. The frozen sixteen-ID staged matrix does not change.

Three additive ordinary platform/lifecycle contracts sit outside both frozen ID inventories. Existing `history::tests::windows_durable_replace_generation_contract` is extended in place to cover Windows History and Blob/CAS handle release, overwrite rename, canonical-file sync, exact bytes/hash, temp absence, append continuity, and History outcome classification. New `session::tests::persistence_poison_lifecycle_contract` covers apply/apply-if-epoch/undo/redo/snapshot/compact/autosave, public/direct Blob puts, RPC page import/mask repair, pipeline sprite/final-CAS promotion, History commit, synchronous response/Dirty/autosave/job publication, allowed read-only access, no-write close, lock release, strict close/reopen recovery, exact permit/control identity, and the exact 13-file direct-field caller closure. It structurally audits `ProjectSession::open_untrusted` because same-module privacy cannot prevent raw-field bypass. It proves one pre-permit coherent History-then-Scene staging snapshot captures authoritative `expected_epoch` and read-only staging inputs, releases Scene then History before permit acquisition, and permits no staging guard to cross the permit. It then proves Blob promotion and Blob-local release precede exactly one permit-held post-Blob History acquisition and exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison. Stale-after-promotion leaves only exact verified unreachable CAS, keeps poison false, and emits no Scene/History/success publication. Barrier/channel hooks prove two races without sleeping: poison wins after another writer's fast read but before its gate acquisition, and an already-permitted writer completes canonical plus success publication while a poisoner is blocked until permit release. A structural test rejects `.await` inside the permit boundary, gate acquisition while an inner lock is held, any permit-held pre-Blob History acquisition or epoch read/validation, any staging guard crossing permit acquisition, more or less than one permit-held post-Blob History acquisition, more or less than one permit-held epoch comparison, and any unlisted direct-field caller. New `routes::pipelines::tests::backend_admission_preflight_contract` covers the pure table, unsupported zero-Registry job path with one direct-run warning/`CompletedWithErrors`, direct-call guard, AllText invariance, and admitted unknown-ID synchronous HTTP 400. These do not change the sixteen staged IDs or three ordinary source-color work-budget IDs.

**Stop/rollback/risks.** Stop if HTTP `/pipelines` accepts a region or creates session/job/cancel/event/task/Scene effects before rejection, direct `pipeline::run` accepts a region or touches registry/order/pages/run-state/engine/warnings/blobs/history/Scene first, either public region field/schema/conversion changes, selected producer order can place font or Typography after inpaint, multiple ordering-critical producers are accepted, Renderer is accepted without exactly one inpainter, one qualifying page lacks exactly one immediately preceding frozen publication under its `PageId`, a no-inpainter page performs any builder/publication, the builder publishes partially or twice for one page, a wrong/completed page record is visible, a failed page contaminates the next, any page record survives run completion, direct direct repair invokes the accessor or performs builder/publication work, Renderer invokes the builder or recomputes frozen inputs, the `EngineCtx` field set/re-export or by-value `Engine::run` signature changes, `PipelineRunState` enters any public carrier, `try_with` succeeds outside an active current-page scope or after completion, builder/inpainter/Renderer see different Arcs or current-page frozen identities, sequential/concurrent/nested runs cross state, an inner run fails to restore the outer task-local, a spawned child inherits/accesses the carrier, any Arc/state-derived mutable handle escapes its awaited scoped future, HanOnly emits a Planner/color Scene op, a hint or color contract outlives one run, a Source-Gate-accepted axis-aligned automatic node lacks exactly one closed color mode before inpaint, rotation enters that completeness set, a contract constant is tuned from image results, source validation is vacuous, any classifier loop lacks a declared charge, candidate materialization precedes `H/K` checks, any width enumeration exists instead of the single reserved interval proof, any stage works before reservation, consumed work exceeds reservation, page admission/totals depend on input order, elapsed time affects correctness, a complexity rejection reaches erase/inference/blob/renderer/Scene work, renderer RGBA/width differs from the contract, `W_s` is not unique, width arithmetic overflows, shadow/glow renders, `contrasting_stroke_color`, Planner color, predicted/default width, or default black reaches `AutomaticStrict`, an unsupported-color node reaches backend expansion/inference, has nonzero erase/sprite pixels, or lacks exact ROI equality/one warning/`CompletedWithErrors`, raw HTTP/MCP validation is absent or accepts either marker value, direct apply bypasses the guard, Renderer reasserts style/trust, persisted style invalidation is not undo/redo exact, untrusted import promotes Planner style, provenance is irreducibly ambiguous, fallback is lossy, the B0 color/work hashes drift, or the exact AOT accessor/app observation escapes `hanonly-test-evidence`, affects behavior, or cannot label the loaded instance consistently. Revert only GREEN-C task-owned hunks, including both region guards, the task-local/current-page accessor/scopes, page-keyed private state, repair-aware bypass and supported-NodeId/device-label hunks in the three inpaint files, sole builder Arc/`PageId` handoff, and bounded source-color hunk in `support.rs`, while retaining unchanged public region/engine/render contracts, unchanged repair route, strengthened repair regression, earlier mask/reason hunks, and the pre-existing dirty `typography.rs` baseline. No new `Artifact` variant, default-build public abstraction, style system, renderer, learned scorer, or runtime dependency.

Also stop if any inpainter static `needs` changes, AllText order changes, a legal Segment repair changes successful behavior, Renderer writes sprite without its normalized frozen `sprite_transform` and `rendered_direction` in the same commit, `Node.transform` or source/OCR geometry is repurposed, any Renderer+Inpainter failure leaves Inpainted/Rendered/History/reachable-blob state different from `B_prepare`, an ink-bearing shaped cluster/run contributes zero final alpha, default and evidence-feature public outputs differ, or the CI-runtime-seeded metamorphic required test is absent, deterministic only from checked-in literals, or bypassable by path/event filters. GREEN-C rollback removes only conditional-order/run-state/page-transaction/Renderer/style/color hunks and reuses, rather than reverts, the already accepted GREEN-B Batch/History boundary.

### Final CPU/Metal, repeatability, and visual acceptance

Before any final output is viewed, acceptance also freezes `source-color-work-budget-v1`, all four work limits, the accounting-table hash, and `ClassifierWorkBudgetExceeded`; deterministic test-only work accounting is part of the color/width oracle and `color_constant_set_sha256`.

**Files/functions.** Verification-only execution against the ignored harness and GREEN-C private run evidence in `pipeline/mod.rs`/`pipeline/engine.rs`, the exact feature-gated `AotInpainting::device` accessor and equally gated AOT engine device classification/reporting, the independently prepared clean references, and `scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts`. Final acceptance parses the non-executable D0 JSON ledger in fresh Bash without evaluation and revalidates every recorded path/hash before use. It makes no production or test-scaffolding edit, no configuration change, and commits no image.

Before the model matrix, run the deterministic input-contract suite. It covers byte-sniffed PNG/JPEG/WebP, unsupported compiled formats, corrupt/truncated files, extension/MIME mismatch, JPEG orientations `1/3/6/8`, opaque and alpha-bearing inputs, hidden RGB under transparent pixels, checked dimension/product overflow, exact/over encoded and decoded byte boundaries, cache eviction/reservation release, and mixed valid/invalid replacement through both multipart and path routes. Format-equivalent visual scenes must produce the same oriented dimensions, target ordering, normalized OCR boxes, eligibility, layout ownership, warnings/status, and rendered/unsupported decisions within the frozen lossy-pixel tolerance; raw container or decoded hashes are not required to match. Every negative or over-limit case must prove byte-for-byte unchanged Scene serialization, History epoch/log, undo/redo result, page order, and zero new page references.

Before any matrix output is viewed, also freeze and validate `source-color-contract-v2`, the exact three product constants, the closed `HanOnlyColorMode`/`SourceColorReason` tables, the deterministic source stroke-width rule, and a deterministic test-only color/width oracle. Each manifest target declares exactly one expectation: `automatic_strict`, `manual_override`, `unsupported_source_color`, or `unsupported_rotation`. The nine independent pages collectively contain automatic solid light/dark/colored fill, no-stroke and uniquely provable colored-stroke cases, plus gradient, multicolor, texture/pattern, nonopaque text, shadow, and glow cases. Shadow and glow are unconditionally `unsupported_source_color` in this revision; every other richer style also fails closed unless the current one-fill/one-stroke contract proves it representable. At least one topology-unique stroked `automatic_strict` target and one `unsupported_source_color` target are in holdout. An alpha-bearing page remains valid input, but an automatic source-color sample with alpha other than `255` is unsupported in this revision.

**RED/GREEN.** Execute the deterministic suite, full workspace checks, and one machine-validated manifest containing one `regression`, four `calibration`, and four disjoint `holdout` full-page images with nine distinct decoded-source hashes. The nine entries collectively cover small/normal/largest-accepted page pixel counts, portrait/landscape/tall aspect ratios, tiny/normal/large source text, single/multiline and horizontal/vertical writing, thin/bold/stroke/shadow/glow text, dense/sparse/no-bubble layouts, protected Latin, page edges, and PNG/JPEG/WebP with at least one oriented JPEG and one alpha-bearing input; each axis has at least one holdout case and every retained numeric/model boundary has below/exact/above deterministic neighbors outside the 360-cell matrix. No entry may be a crop or re-encode of another decoded source, `c04` remains mandatory calibration evidence, and `test.jpeg` plus its decoded-equivalent WebP count as one regression identity. Every entry has an independently approved page-sized clean reference and target-local edit ROIs that cover complete nonempty source-ink masks even when ink extends outside the tight recognition anchor; the reference removes only targets expected as `automatic_strict` or `manual_override`, while retaining every `unsupported_rotation` and `unsupported_source_color` target plus all pixels outside rendered edit ROIs. Before any output is viewed, freeze the Revision 49 residual and source-color/width thresholds. For each target whose manifest expectation is `automatic_strict` or `manual_override`, require blind `M_erase == M_residual`, derive nonempty `M_delta={p in clean_reference_edit_roi | Source[p] != Clean[p]}`, require the agreed mask to be a subset of `M_delta`, and run the exact test-specification distance/source-closer/8-connected oracle independently over both masks before any cell pass. The existing pinned Source Gate OCR configuration may only add a rejection for a positive-area pre-composite Inpainted candidate intersecting `M_delta`; it cannot make either pixel oracle pass. The shell creates no per-cell output directory; the Rust harness validates every nested input from single-read bytes before descriptor-relative runtime/report/artifact creation, so a replacement race has zero cell-output side effects. For each `unsupported_rotation` target, do not apply discrimination/residual division: require Source, Clean, Inpainted, and Rendered pixels to be identical throughout its edit ROI, require zero final-erase-mask pixels there, no rendered block/sprite, exactly one rotation warning, and `CompletedWithErrors`. Every manifest entry runs on explicit CPU and actual Candle Metal for ten in-process repeats in each of two fresh feature-enabled processes. Every `(device, entry_id, process, repeat)` produces a structured result; B0 calibration and holdout share one selection-executable/model/library/fixture-manifest/image-input-contract/color-preimage fingerprint at clean `B0_SHA`, while GREEN-C final acceptance validates the immutable B0 artifact without reselection and captures a final-executable hash plus one trusted-target closure schema/common/generator-lock/target tuple that remains identical across all 360 cells. Both stages require the same default-off `hanonly-test-evidence` bridge, exact three-edge dependency inventory, five model/config hashes, loaded-library map, frozen fixture-manifest hash, frozen image-input contract, exact canonical color-contract preimages and digests, required anti-fixture check evidence, and frozen recall contract. Each required coverage axis, rendered-target residual check, rotation/color equality/zero-erase check, exact contract-RGBA/width/probe check, independent final RGBA recomposition and omission check, input budget, production closure, and machine oracle in T5 must pass; multiple crops from one source count once. Run the repository-wide production-policy, dependency, and release-feature inventory audits against manifest/runtime/build evidence. Use B0's same-instance Source Gate evidence and GREEN-C's exact AOT accessor/label writer/run-state evidence; repeated page writes must match the first AOT device label or fail. Flux is excluded from the paired gate. GREEN requires stable structural decisions, all safety invariants, non-vacuous validator-derived Source Gate CPU/Metal plus requested/actual AOT device equality, machine-verified rendered-target residual removal, exact automatic source-color/width rendering, exact final composite, explicit zero-erase/equality/warning/`CompletedWithErrors` evidence for rotated and unsupported-color cases, default/release feature absence, and human inpaint-texture acceptance.

For each `automatic_strict` target and cell, the oracle requires nonempty fill samples and nonempty stroke samples when stroke is present; source sample alpha `255`; every recorded p95/separation/edge value within the frozen contract; exactly one source-width candidate when stroke is present; independent recomputation of `F_s/W_s/F_t/W_t`; exact equality between the recorded source-derived contract RGBA/width and the values resolved by app Renderer; exact equality at every full-coverage pixel of test-only fill-only/stroke-only probe rasters using `W_t`; at most one channel value of integer-rounding residual for every antialiased probe edge; and no Planner/prediction/default/contrasting-color-or-width fallback trace. For each `unsupported_source_color` target, residual division is forbidden and Source, Clean, Inpainted, and Rendered must be byte-identical throughout its edit ROI, with zero erase-mask/sprite pixels, no target block, exactly one exact-prefix warning, and `CompletedWithErrors`. `manual_override` targets are reported and retain existing manual regression coverage but are excluded from source-color equality claims. Human review may reject inpaint texture only; it cannot waive, replace, or reinterpret any machine residue, geometry, style, width, unsupported-preservation, or composite verdict.

Every `automatic_strict` and `manual_override` target must also produce exactly one matching rendered block/sprite with a nonempty alpha bbox and a final Rendered image node after its pre-composite Inpainted residual oracle passes. The independent test-only compositor decodes the actual persisted Source, Inpainted base, optional Brush, ordered sprite, and final Rendered blobs. Before reading any runtime classification, mask, block, sprite, or final output, it independently recomputes protected support `P` from persisted Source Scene/page/node visibility, detector, transforms, and TextData using the exact Revision 46 oracle in the test specification: protected-detector whole-node precedence, ICU Han and protected-Latin classification, mixed-line polygon requirements, translation-count mismatch protection, zero-rotation eligibility, half-open floor/ceil rasterization, and OR union. A reported protected mask/hash is comparison evidence only, never oracle input. Starting from the same base for every replay, it executes exactly `Inpainted base -> replace P with Source RGBA -> optional Brush at (0,0) -> sprites in persisted page-node production order`, requiring that this order is a bijection with reported successful target NodeIds. Overlay uses the pinned `image 0.25.10` RGBA source-over semantics, independently reimplemented without calling production Renderer, `imageops::overlay`, or the production protected-support helper: top alpha `0` preserves, `255` replaces, otherwise use the same premultiplied `f32` source-over and `NumCast<u8>` truncation. Exact decoded RGBA must equal the final Rendered blob. For each successful target, a fresh replay omitting only that target sprite, rather than erasing it from final output, must differ from final Rendered in at least one pixel inside that target's safe region. A missing/unreadable blob, wrong protected restore, empty/duplicate/reordered sprite, transform mismatch, composite mismatch, omission equality, missing final Rendered node, or successful-mode source-residue failure is a hard acceptance failure.

**Runtime aggregation scope.** The 360-cell validator compares only final-executable, trusted production-closure schema/common/generator-lock/target fields, model/config, loaded-library, selected-candidate, visual-manifest, Source Gate fixture-manifest, image-input-contract, source-color-contract, color-constant-set, feature-set, and parser fingerprints globally. It validates requested/derived device against each cell's device axis. It groups target/protected/rotation/color-mode sets and counts, crop choice, ownership/locality, warning/status, canonical format/orientation/dimensions, peak reserved/cache/mask bytes, and safety verdict by `entry_id` across that entry's 40 cells. It groups resolved geometry, source-size and `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` outcomes, final size, Planner outcomes, color mode/reason, source sample/reference/threshold and `F_s/W_s/F_t/W_t` facts, resolved fill/stroke RGBA/width, probe verdict, exact-composite/omission verdict, rendered/unsupported outcome, and residual/equality verdict by `(entry_id,target_id)` across that target's 40 cells. Distinct entries or targets are never required to equal each other; CPU/Metal numeric residuals may differ while every value must satisfy its frozen threshold, and source-color/width mode/contract values must remain structurally identical for the same target across all 40 cells.

**Stop condition.** Missing models, Metal fallback, any missing manifest/device/process/repeat cell, a nondiscriminative/missing clean reference, missing required input/size/text/style/color-mode/format coverage, no topology-unique supported-stroke target, any residual, color/width-contract, exact-RGBA/width, independent-composite, unsupported-preservation, production-closure, or input-budget threshold failure, an incomplete machine oracle or selection artifact, a production-policy audit violation, or unresolved human texture defects remain explicit acceptance gaps. They cannot be reported as pass.

**Rollback.** Revert the last failing batch only; preserve earlier independently passing batches and regression tests.

**Risks.** Runtime cost, model variation, and subjective inpaint texture quality are recorded separately. Structural equality and every color contract verdict/value are required; inpainted pixel hashes need not match CPU vs Metal. Rejection rate for complex source styles may be high, but rendering a guessed color is not an allowed mitigation.

**Inherited Revision 46 acceptance contract.** Every emitted automatic-target record must prove `preflight_consumed<=preflight_reserved`, `evaluation_consumed<=evaluation_reserved`, `target_consumed<=target_reserved<=target_limit`, and `page_consumed<=page_reserved<=page_limit`; exact-limit cases must match the checked P0/P1/E requested/reserved calculator; every record must carry identical final page totals, including under reversed input order; and `p0_metadata_unbounded` must separately hard-fail before target traversal with no target record or mutation. P1 must request and reserve exactly its admitted conservative `4*A+19*X_i+81_920` bound, consume only independently counted real declared operations, allow `consumed<reserved` for unused capacity/early termination, perform no padding/no-op/synthetic counter inflation, and leave all direct-core/P95/order/`bg(q)`/candidate counters and allocations zero. E must use the complete same-bin P95 domain, the one shared `bg(q)` table, exact checked integer blend, fail-closed cross-pair ties, and exactly one complete candidate. The complete pre-erase preflight must capture `B_prepare` after upstream producer commits, freeze one final geometry/font/group/Planner-size/color/`F_t/W_t` result, prove expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry translation identity, complete cluster/control/glyph/fill traversal, nonempty alpha, checked canonical-set retained logical-sprite reservation, and independently checked actual-scale raster dimensions/bytes/construction, raster each successful target serially exactly once with at most one scratch surface live and released per target, and atomically publish one immutable sprite object before any inpainter run/apply. Selected Renderer must persist/composite that same frozen sprite once with zero layout/raster/recomputation or public render-input changes; all terminal paths release pixel payloads. Every negative in either builder failure class must prove zero publication, inpainter run/apply, erase, or downstream persistence, exact final Scene/History/reachable-blob/sprite equality to `B_prepare`, and retained upstream commits. A translation-identity mismatch, final-layout failure after local fallback, cluster/control-coverage failure, glyph-validity failure, fill-traversal failure, or nonempty-alpha validation failure is a deterministic hard pre-write `geometry_or_font` failure. Only checked logical/actual-raster arithmetic or `usize` conversion failure, retained owner-rectangle/page-cap reservation failure, or fallible raster-surface-construction failure is a recoverable `geometry_or_font` failure. Process-level allocator OOM/abort is outside the verdict. Generated adversarial cases must fail closed with the stable complexity reason before erase, no `U*M` width enumeration may occur, repeated runs must produce identical non-time diagnostics and pixels, and no wall-clock threshold may affect correctness. Both closed color-contract preimages must reproduce their frozen SHA-256 values byte-for-byte; sprite/raster reservation remains outside those preimages. Every Revision 39 through 45 geometry, source-style fidelity, width, composite, UI Auto, dependency, B0, sixteen-ID, C-first-generation, closure, governance, and dual-SHA contract remains mandatory as inherited contract, while the current operative artifact revision remains Revision 50.

### Governance input freeze and required static production-policy gate

**Boundary.** The local half of this gate is completed after GREEN-C but before `IMPL_SHA`: the eight B0 interpreter paths, including every checker-contained closure rule, remain byte-identical, while workflow, CODEOWNERS, and every other non-frozen tracked common/generator-lock input are committed before T6. The remote half runs only after the final nine-source/360-cell audit and C-only commit, before the implementation may be called complete or merged. Ordinary PR CI does not rerun models, Metal, images, or the 360-cell matrix. Evidence is acyclic and ordered: T6 first writes sanitized runtime validation `R`; the full audit writes sanitized `A` and binds `R`; the verifier then writes excluded `scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json` as `C`, a closed Revision 50 test-only snapshot that binds `R+A`; the PR check emits one native notice annotation with the canonical closure JSON; and final T7 writes external `G` binding `R+A+C`, both target records, that annotation, and all remote evidence. Neither `R` nor `A` contains the hash of `C` or `G`, and no earlier input contains the hash of a later artifact. Every final cell and `R/A/C` carries identical schema/common/generator-lock/trusted-target fields plus current `plan_revision:50`; `C` deliberately carries no commit SHA or Ubuntu target record. `R/A` expose only correlation-ID-keyed non-length text-completeness verdicts; `C`, the annotation, closure summaries, and `G` expose no target text proof fields or guarded mapping. Ordinary PR CI requires the common/generator-lock fields, independently reproduces its Ubuntu target twice, explicitly checks out `candidate_head_sha`, and emits exactly one annotation containing both `candidate_head_sha` and the test-merge `check_sha` plus its target digest. Any tracked non-`C` repository change after T6 invalidates `R/A/C/G` and requires a new clean `IMPL_SHA` plus fresh T6/A/C. `C` contains the audit-rule version, `plan_revision:50`, contract hashes, trusted closure fields, visual/fixture manifest hashes, `R` hash, `A` hash, and positive policy-fixture hit count plus sorted digest evidence for each exact category. It contains no commit SHA, source/translated text, source or target RGBA values, raw runtime path, raw source hash, crop name, NodeId, bbox/ROI tuple, runtime node/line/text count, text-derived digest, target-correlation mapping, or target proof field. The `line_count` category is populated only by checker-owned synthetic syntax-coupling sentinels, never runtime/manifest values; all category counts and digest lists equal the sanitized sets in `A`.

**Required job.** Reuse `.github/workflows/test.yml`; add prerequisite job id `hanonly-windows-history-contract` with display name `HanOnly Windows History Contract` and retain exactly one required-context job id `hanonly-production-policy` with unique display name `HanOnly Production Policy`. Both run only under the workflow's unconditional `pull_request` and existing `push.branches: [main]`. The Windows prerequisite uses `windows-2022`, read-only contents permission, the same exact candidate-head env expression and `actions/checkout@v7` `with.ref`, the pinned repository Rust toolchain, and direct default-feature command `cargo test -p koharu-app --lib history::tests::windows_durable_replace_generation_contract -- --exact --nocapture`; direct Cargo is intentional for this filesystem-only test so the repository's Windows development wrapper does not require CUDA. It contains no model, CUDA, Metal, Vulkan, external image, feature flag, matrix, conditional, secret, token, write permission, or `continue-on-error`. The required Ubuntu job has exact `needs: [hanonly-windows-history-contract]`, exact job-level `if: ${{ always() }}`, and a first step that exits nonzero unless `${{ needs.hanonly-windows-history-contract.result }}` is exactly `success`; no later step may run before that assertion. Thus Windows failure, cancellation, or skip still creates a failing `HanOnly Production Policy` context instead of skipping the required context. The checker owns this sole allowed `needs`/job-`if` exception, rejects any alternate dependency/condition/result coercion, and retains the prohibition on step-level `if`, other `needs`, `continue-on-error`, and strategy matrices.

The required job env defines `HANONLY_CANDIDATE_HEAD_SHA=${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' && github.event.pull_request.head.sha || github.sha }}` and `HANONLY_CHECK_SHA=${{ github.sha }}`; `actions/checkout@v7` must use `with.ref: ${{ env.HANONLY_CANDIDATE_HEAD_SHA }}`. The checker rejects every other event, requires PR `HEAD == HANONLY_CANDIDATE_HEAD_SHA` and `GITHUB_SHA == HANONLY_CHECK_SHA`, and requires main-push `HEAD == HANONLY_CANDIDATE_HEAD_SHA == HANONLY_CHECK_SHA == GITHUB_SHA`. It installs the frozen Bun lock, runs `bun test scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.test.ts`, derives exactly 128 random bits from runner `/dev/urandom` into printed lowercase-hex `HANONLY_METAMORPHIC_SEED`, resolves unchanged ordinary default-feature short ID `han_only_source_color_work_budget_adversarial_stress` through `--list` to exactly one module-qualified full libtest name, executes only that variable with `--exact --nocapture`, proves `running 1 test`, exactly one matching `... ok`, and summary `1 passed; 0 failed`, and then runs `bun scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts --ci-static --ci-corpus scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json`. Missing, renamed, deleted, duplicate, root-unqualified, ignored, short-filter, or zero-test behavior fails. The metamorphic test generates dimensions, geometry, node order, text length, color, transforms, and PNG/JPEG/WebP-equivalent in-memory payloads after build and prints the seed for exact replay; it uses no new dependency, external image, model, or Metal. This behavior-level check is the primary fixture-independence proof; the scanner is defense in depth. `--ci-static` validates the corpus closed schema, hash syntax, internal count/digest consistency, exact two-job workflow/CODEOWNERS shape including Windows prerequisite propagation, unique full-name discovery, `--exact`, and started/passed/failed assertions, independently recomputed whole-file common and generator-lock hashes equal to `C`, twice-reproduced Ubuntu target record, normalized candidate digest matches, frozen color/width/work contract/constants and both closed-preimage hashes, automatic-style/work-budget fail-closed syntax contracts, and release-feature inventory; it does not authenticate absent trusted `R/A`. With `GITHUB_ACTIONS=true`, it emits exactly one escaped workflow command `::notice file=scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json,line=1,title=HANONLY_PRODUCTION_CLOSURE_V1::<canonical-json>`. The annotation JSON carries `plan_revision:50`, exact `candidate_head_sha`, exact `check_sha`, schema/common/generator-lock hashes, both color-contract hashes, Ubuntu target/digest, and the non-authoritative reproducibility seed. Local runs instead atomically write a closed external summary that omits unavailable `check_sha` and contains the local `candidate_head_sha` plus the same closure/target fields. It fails on every local production hit, unknown exclusion, corpus/rule drift, fixed/missing seed, skipped or zero-test metamorphic execution, default/wrong checkout ref, missing/swapped/collapsed PR identities, unsupported event, missing/duplicate annotation, missing file, empty category, closure/color/width/work-contract/preimage drift, target nondeterminism, absent/failing/bypassable Windows prerequisite propagation, or forbidden automatic fallback. Neither job contains an evidence-feature command, model, Metal, external input path, write permission, token, secret, raw color value from an evidence image, or literal `hanonly-test-evidence`; full runtime evidence and `G` remain required by trusted final acceptance.

**Tamper boundary.** Retain the existing wildcard and add root-anchored exact entries for `/.github/CODEOWNERS`, `/.github/workflows/test.yml`, `/scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts`, `/scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.test.ts`, and `/scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json`. Every exact entry uses the current five-owner set `@mayocream @fffonion @Map1en @karrot0 @liksunrice` in that order. Authenticated collaborator evidence must prove at least two distinct exact owners currently have `write`, `maintain`, or `admin` permission and at least one eligible exact owner differs from the implementation PR author; otherwise governance activation stops. No separate workflow, package script, dependency, GitHub App, or policy repository is introduced.

**Remote Ruleset.** After the exact job has completed once for the implementation PR and its current test-merge commit, an authorized repository administrator creates or updates one active branch Ruleset targeting `refs/heads/main`. It has an empty bypass list; requires a pull request with `required_approving_review_count: 1`; requires code-owner review and dismisses stale approvals; requires branches to be up to date; requires exactly `HanOnly Production Policy` from the same GitHub Actions integration; and blocks force pushes and branch deletion. Ruleset mutation is an external repository action and must not be attempted without explicit administrator authority. Missing authority, eligible independent owner, or repository-plan support blocks completion; there is no silent fallback to an advisory check.

**Machine evidence.** Freeze GitHub REST API version `2022-11-28` and `Accept: application/vnd.github+json`. Capture mode-0600 closed envelopes below the D0 root for: direct `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/rulesets/{ruleset_id}`; exact-name latest `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/commits/{check_sha}/check-runs?check_name=HanOnly%20Production%20Policy&filter=latest&per_page=100`; `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/check-runs/{validated_check_run_id}/annotations?per_page=100`; direct implementation PR; and five direct collaborator-permission responses, one per exact owner. Each envelope records exact method/path/query, API version, HTTP status, and response body but no token or authorization header. The PR body must prove `head.sha == candidate_head_sha`, `mergeable == true`, and `merge_commit_sha == check_sha`. The Ruleset body must contain the field `bypass_actors` with exact value `[]`; omission/redaction is a failure. The check-run body must bind the same PR, have `head_sha == check_sha`, and satisfy `total_count == check_runs.length == 1`; because `per_page=100`, any unequal count, pagination/truncation marker, stale test-merge SHA, or unexpected page is a failure. The five collaborator responses are unpaginated direct permission endpoints and must match the exact owner logins.

`--verify-required-check-ruleset` accepts Ruleset, check-run, check-annotation, PR, and collaborator evidence envelopes plus `R`, `A`, `C`, the expected repository, `refs/heads/main`, `candidate_head_sha`, and `check_sha`. From a clean detached PR worktree it requires local `HEAD == candidate_head_sha == PR head.sha`, independently recomputes the closure twice, requires schema/common/generator-lock equality with `R/A/C`, and requires its same-target PR record to reproduce. Separately it requires `check_sha == PR merge_commit_sha == queried check_run.head_sha`. It parses exactly one notice annotation from that sole successful test-merge check run: path `scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json`, line `1`, title `HANONLY_PRODUCTION_CLOSURE_V1`, and canonical-JSON message. Annotation candidate/check/common/generator/PR-target fields must equal both identity chains, local recomputation, and authenticated PR evidence. It then requires active enforcement, exact target inclusion, present-empty bypass actors, every named pull-request/review/up-to-date/non-fast-forward/deletion rule, one required approval, the exact five CODEOWNERS entries, at least two write-capable exact owners with one non-author candidate, and the sole completed `success` check bound to the exact PR/test-merge/context/integration. It rejects `neutral`, `skipped`, stale, incomplete, duplicate-name, any-source, wrong PR/SHA, swapped/collapsed PR identities, missing/duplicate/malformed annotation, closure drift, same-target nondeterminism, missing owner permission, self-only ownership, or extra HanOnly policy contexts. On success it validates `C` against actual `R+A`, descriptor-hashes all evidence, and writes/fsyncs mode-0600 `G` beneath the external evidence root with `candidate_head_sha`, `check_sha`, trusted and PR target records, and verified repository/ref/context/integration/owner facts. `G` is never an input to `R`, `A`, or `C`. A UI screenshot is supplemental only and cannot replace API evidence.

**Stop/rollback.** Stop if the job can be skipped by path/event filters, if it does not explicitly checkout the candidate head, if either SHA chain is missing or contradictory, if workflow/checker/corpus changes do not require code-owner review, if the job embeds the evidence feature or trusted inputs, if the Ruleset has any bypass actor, if the test-merge check is not uniquely successful, or if API evidence cannot be obtained. Rollback removes the required context from the Ruleset before removing or renaming the job, preserves before/after Ruleset JSON, and never leaves `main` permanently blocked by a nonexistent context. This contract prevents bypass through normal merge paths; it does not claim to prevent the ultimate repository settings owner from editing governance.

## RED/GREEN verification matrix

| Failure | RED proof | GREEN proof | Primary tests |
|---|---|---|---|
| source ink residue | target outline/component pixels outside final erase mask or Inpainted output remains source-like inside independent ink masks | complete target ink support, zero protected/non-target overlap, and globally frozen clean-reference residual oracle passes before target rendering | T1, RED-A, T5 |
| too-small target text | larger raster fits safe surrounding geometry but tight box rejects it | largest cap-bounded raster fits dynamic owner region | T2, RED-B |
| Planner lines ignored | valid reversible one-region suggestion produces no runtime hint | transient candidate locally fits or exact captured original fallback runs; Scene unchanged | T3 |
| Planner style overrides source evidence | black/no-stroke Planner beats source/prediction evidence | `manual_override` is explicit; otherwise source-derived contract controls automatic fill/stroke and no HanOnly Planner Scene style exists | T3, T5 |
| stale persisted Planner style receives manual priority | marked black/no-stroke Scene style wins or survives as a HanOnly style source | HanOnly ignores marked stored style entirely; validated source color controls automatic fill/stroke; AllText unchanged | T3, T4 |
| wrong automatic fill/stroke or guessed complex style | predicted stroke is discarded, default/contrasting color renders, or gradient/multicolor/shadow/glow is approximated as a solid color | exact source-contract RGBA for representable solid styles; otherwise zero erase/sprite, byte-identical ROI, one warning, and `CompletedWithErrors` | T3, T4, T5, T7 |
| source stroke width is guessed | prediction/default width reaches Renderer, or source topology admits zero/multiple widths | one unique `W_s`, checked reduced ratio and round-half-up `W_t`; otherwise `UnprovableStrokeWidth` with zero erase/sprite | T3, T5, T6 |
| source translation is truncated, glyphs are silently skipped, or the failure is discovered after erase | frozen text differs from the expected translation, cluster coverage has gaps, a shaped glyph ID is unrepresentable/missing, fill visits fewer glyphs, alpha is empty, or any failure first appears in Renderer | the sole builder proves expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry identity, reversible breaks, complete cluster/control coverage, nonzero `u16` glyphs, zero missing glyphs, complete fill traversal, and nonempty alpha before any inpainter run/apply; Renderer proves same frozen sprite identity with zero raster calls | T3, T4, T5, T6 |
| sprite exists but is not in final Rendered | nonempty sprite/final node exist while final RGBA ignores or misplaces it | independently replay persisted Source/Inpainted-base/protected restore/Brush/ordered sprites; exact final RGBA plus per-target omission inequality | T5, T6 |
| unsafe expansion | synthetic region crosses page/protected/other/target | pre-write atomic failure with zero persisted blobs/ops | T1, T2, T4 |
| scale/order instability | normalized geometry/font differs by scale/order | proportional scale and identical order-independent decisions | T1, T2 |
| backend drift | engines receive different canonical target/forbidden supports, Source Gate load evidence cannot derive CPU/Metal, executable/model fingerprints drift, or AOT Metal falls back | identical canonical supports, backend-specific final masks are safe subsets, Source Gate metrics/device are recomputed from raw evidence, and actual loaded AOT device is recorded | T1, T2, T6 |
| absolute crop/size/OCR policy | C2, `-5px`, `12..28`, `72px`, source-box UI estimation, or source-page `160/1024` thresholds produce non-proportional or misleading behavior | geometry-relative backend crop/font ceiling, crop-local model-contract OCR scaling, no UI automatic-pixel estimate, preserved Auto/empty UI state, and UI/backend format-allowlist parity | T2, T5 |
| decode/import drift | equivalent containers/orientations produce different geometry, cache bytes exceed the budget, or failed replace clears pages | one canonical bounded PNG/JPEG/WebP decoder, byte-weighted eviction, and one post-validation Scene Batch | T0, T2, T4, T5 |
| false rotated success | rotated source is restored but only traced | private rotation reason, unchanged source ROI, no rendered block/sprite, one warning per node, `warning_count > 0`, RPC `CompletedWithErrors` | T2, T4, T5 |
| one-source corpus overfit | all real fixtures share one decoded-source hash | one regression plus four frozen calibration and four disjoint holdout full-page hashes; every coverage axis passes | T5 |
| production-policy audit is optional or self-disabled | policy command is absent/skipped, workflow/checker/corpus is altered without protected review, or the required context is missing from the current PR test-merge commit | unique static job succeeds on the current test-merge commit while checking out the exact candidate head; API-verified active empty-bypass Ruleset requires it and code-owner review | T7 |
| trusted runtime evidence is attached to different production code | T6 executable/`R/A/C` comes from one common/generator-lock input set while the PR candidate contains another, or the authenticated required check belongs to another test merge | trusted cells and `R/A/C` share one clean checkpoint; PR CI must match common/generator locks, reproduce its own target twice, and emit both candidate-head and check-commit identities that T7 revalidates into `G` | T6, T7 |

## Deliberate pre-mortem

| Likely failure | Earliest warning | Mitigation and rollback |
|---|---|---|
| GREEN-A covers decorative Han ink but leaks into protected Latin or nearby non-target source | T1 reports a nonzero forbidden overlap, or T5 protected ROI hashes differ before visual review | Block GREEN-A before inpaint persistence, inspect canonical target/forbidden support equality, and revert only the isolated GREEN-A mask batch against the D0 ledger; never broaden to bubble/card fill |
| GREEN-B/B1 is deterministic on small masks but becomes slow, over-conservative, spatially displaces a target, or leaves a guessed UI automatic pixel disconnected from private runtime geometry | T2 resolver timing or owner-mask bytes exceed the recorded bound, independent safe sizes collapse while free area remains, rectangle/sprite center leaves the source anchor, or UI tests expose any numeric automatic HanOnly value or require UI/backend `G/Csrc` equality | Keep the locality-aware exhaustive oracle and one-pass profile gate; retire the UI estimator chain and test only Auto/empty state plus format allowlist; keep resolved layout only in crate-private HanOnly records and never add or repurpose public render inputs, Scene/OpenAPI fields, or the UI algorithm; stop before GREEN-C and revert the complete B1 crop/layout/backend/UI/warning batch if any invariant fails |
| GREEN-C's internal-marker boundary rejects a real client flow or stale marked style still changes HanOnly output | HTTP/MCP marker-presence tests, AllText snapshots, import fixtures, or the focused marked-style HanOnly regression change unexpectedly | Gate the exact raw pointers at HTTP/MCP, preserve AllText byte-for-byte fixtures, require the D3 current-project provenance audit, and revert only GREEN-C task hunks if the public mutation boundary or style lifecycle cannot be proven without a shim |
| GREEN-C appears visually plausible but uses a predicted/default/contrasting color or erases an unrepresentable source style | exact RGBA/probe oracle differs, an automatic node lacks one closed color mode before inpaint, or an unsupported-color ROI changes | freeze source-color constants before images, require source-derived contract bytes and isolated pass probes, fail unsupported before erase, and revert the bounded GREEN-C color/run-state/renderer hunks rather than weakening thresholds |
| adversarial source colors make exhaustive core/candidate/reconstruction/path/width work production-unbounded | checked reservation fixtures, instrumented counters, and generated high-core/high-candidate/large-`U`/page-aggregate stress cases detect a missing or exceeded bound | freeze `source-color-work-budget-v1`; require exact `4*A+19*X_i+81_920` P1 and closed `Wbg/Wcore/Worder/Wfill/Wstroke` E charges; classify valid complexity rejection as `ClassifierWorkBudgetExceeded` before erase; make elapsed time diagnostic only; abort on accounting invariant drift |
| a huge page or target count consumes metadata/pixel work before accounting, or P1 silently performs E work | anything beyond O(1) `page.nodes.len()` occurs before `J` admission; page-node or target traversal starts before its `J`/`J+T` reservation; pair ranking starts before `checked(Wterm+choose2_checked(T))`; `T=0/1` underflows; pair multiplication or final addition wraps; owner-map allocation, `Q/B` access, or histogram read occurs before P1; or any direct-core/P95/order/`bg(q)`/candidate counter changes before E admission | enforce `J -> T -> Wterm -> Wmeta`: reserve `J` before the sole node traversal, reserve `J+T` before terminal record allocation, totalize `choose2_checked`, and reserve full pair work before ranking; assert zero/one/two/boundary/overflow cases, exact phase tuples, and all E-only counters/allocations remain zero after P1; preserve every rejected ROI and revert classifier accounting on any pre-reservation or cross-stage work |
| coincident `H/K` limits, an early stop, or semantic color ambiguity produces contradictory outcomes | the same input changes `classifier_limit_kind`, null fields become zero, page totals differ by target/input order, P95 is computed over an already filtered core, candidates rebuild `bg(q)`, or pair order resolves equal minimum residuals | enforce the fixed terminal precedence and exact null/zero table; use complete same-bin nearest-rank P95, one immutable candidate-independent `bg(q)`, checked `+127` integer blending, and fail-closed cross-pair ties; compare identical final page totals and classifications under reversed order |
| B0 authorizes crop policy against stale or non-canonical source-color/work semantics | either color/work hash is absent from the artifact/frozen projection, the canonical JSON bytes differ across Rust/TypeScript, or phase recomputation differs | bind `source_color_contract_sha256` and `color_constant_set_sha256` to their exact closed preimages before model load, reject old Revision 45/swapped/malformed/alternate-serialization hashes, and invalidate B0 before B1 |
| staged RED appears valid because a short substring ran the wrong or multiple tests | `--list --ignored` resolves zero or multiple full names, the execution command omits `--exact`, or the log does not prove one started and one failed test | treat the short ID as discovery-only, require one module-qualified full name whose final segment matches, execute only that name with `--exact --ignored`, and block B0 on any count or command drift |
| conservative P1 slack is fabricated as execution | an independent operation counter differs from reported consumed work, or `consumed==reserved` despite `|B|<8X_i`, `H<64`, `H>64`, or another admitted early stop | require real declared-operation counters only; unused slack remains unconsumed; padding/no-op work or synthetic increments are `accounting_invariant_failure` and roll back classifier accounting |
| Arbitrary engine-spec order lets inpaint precede a selected font/Typography producer, pre-inpaint raster validation is incomplete, private task-local state crosses run/scope boundaries, frozen sprite memory accumulates, or Renderer reconstructs/rerasterizes after erase | a permutation violates the total producer matrix; any static inpainter descriptor or AllText order changes; a zero-inpainter branch reserves/rasters/publishes anything; a one-inpainter branch lacks exactly one complete layout/cluster-run-alpha/sprite/transform/direction publication; any Renderer+Inpainter failure leaves state after `B_prepare`; public `EngineCtx`/`Engine::run` changes; `try_with` succeeds outside scope; concurrent/nested runs cross state; a spawned child inherits/accesses the carrier; Arc/object/sprite identity differs; pixel payload survives a terminal path; or Renderer has any nonzero layout/shape/glyph/raster/fit/group/Planner-size/rounding/width counter | keep static descriptors unchanged; add HanOnly-only conditional order in `pipeline::run`; reject ambiguous producers and Renderer-without-inpainter before engine load; scope one private Arc; reserve the complete peak-live set; publish one immutable preflight object; run Renderer+Inpainter in one `B_prepare`-scoped page transaction; commit sprite plus normalized transform/direction/final Rendered output once; release every payload/scratch on every terminal path |
| repair endpoint resolves an arbitrary engine ID | a non-Segment role or descriptor producing Renderer/Typography/other/multiple artifacts reaches body decode, blob persistence, Scene access, engine load/run, or apply | before every side effect require URL role `MaskRole::Segment`, successful registry lookup, and exact descriptor output `[Artifact::Inpainted]`; all negative classes return one stable HTTP 400 while the accepted repair keeps its direct atomic path | T3, T4 |
| AOT test evidence leaks into a default/release public API or behavior path | default Cargo JSON exposes the accessor, an app reference is ungated, or the device label changes execution | permit only the exact feature-gated hidden borrowed-device accessor and equally gated observational app path; fail default/release inventory and roll back the evidence bridge on any leak |
| A geometry-relative crop ratio improves calibration but regresses holdout | a frozen holdout loses Source Gate recall or protected/rotation exclusions | keep the known image outside selection, freeze candidates/selection before holdout, end the revision on any holdout failure, and revert B0 without starting B1 or same-revision retuning |
| a large or adversarial image exhausts memory, changes orientation, or destroys existing pages during replacement | decoded/cache/mask reservations exceed the frozen budget, canonical dimensions differ after reload, or a mixed invalid request changes Scene/History | use one checked decoder and memory owner, serial request decode, byte-weighted eviction, and one final remove+add Batch; revert the complete B1 input hunk if any reservation or atomicity test fails |
| the policy scanner passes locally but is bypassed or weakened in GitHub | required context is absent/skipped/renamed, a stale test-merge check is accepted, or workflow/checker/corpus changes merge without code-owner review | block completion until the candidate/test-merge identity chains and active empty-bypass Ruleset are API-validated; revert remote required context before any job rename/removal |
| T6 passes, then production code changes before C/G | PR `--ci-static` or T7 recomputation yields a different production-closure hash | invalidate old `R/A/C`, rerun T6 through corpus generation, and never regenerate only `C` to hide drift; C/governance-only changes remain outside the closure |
| first corpus generation is treated as a tracked modification or an interrupted generator leaves output behind | the clean implementation worktree produces `?? scripts/fixtures/hanonly-production-policy-ci-corpus.json` while the command expects ` M`, or a failed write/fsync/rename leaves a temp/output | require the corpus to be absent before first generation, require exactly one `??` afterward, then stage only C; fault tests require prior bytes unchanged when C existed and output absent when it did not, with no temp in either case |
| a supported stroke has no unique source width | the bounded topology oracle returns zero or multiple `W_s` candidates | classify `UnprovableStrokeWidth`, preserve the source ROI, and block release if the required topology-unique supported-stroke holdout is absent |
| a nonempty sprite hides truncated text or skipped glyphs, or missing glyph detection occurs only after source erase | expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry digests differ, insertion-only break reconstruction fails, cluster/control coverage has a gap/conflict, any shaped glyph is unrepresentable/missing, fill visits fewer glyphs than shaped, alpha is empty, retained logical bytes or actual-scale raster arithmetic/construction fails, scratch overlaps targets, or any failure appears after inpainter run/apply | run the shared layout/glyph/fill raster path exactly once in the sole builder, capture `B_prepare`, independently check retained logical and transient raster facts, keep one scratch surface live, freeze the transcript/sprite before inpaint, return an error for out-of-range glyph IDs, and require full digest/coverage/fill/alpha plus exact `B_prepare` equality/upstream-retention proof |
| a nonempty sprite is dropped or transformed incorrectly in final output | independent recomposition differs from final RGBA or per-target omission leaves final RGBA unchanged | hard-fail T6 and revert the renderer/composite GREEN-C hunk; do not permit human waiver or a second renderer |
