## Linked-worktree acceptance

- The original dirty worktree is preservation-only. Before implementation, capture its `HEAD`, NUL status, tracked/staged binary diffs, every untracked path/blob hash, and the exact dirty `typography.rs` patch/blob outside every worktree.
- Create a unique implementation linked worktree from the captured `HEAD`; replay and commit only the exact `typography.rs` baseline before task-owned edits. The other dirty paths remain solely in the original worktree.
- After GREEN-A and B0-only implementation, finalize every B0-sensitive harness/parser/validator/feature/model-contract input, all sixteen normative test IDs, all eight `B0_FROZEN_INTERPRETER_PATHS`, and the Revision 50 required anti-fixture check; commit a globally clean `B0_SHA` containing the allowed Source Gate / PP-OCR recall enhancement but no B1 target layout/font/render/UI/color behavior. Create a detached globally clean `B0_WORKTREE` at that SHA; create/rehydrate D0 there, uniquely resolve every remaining staged short ID, execute only its full libtest name with `--exact --ignored`, prove the remaining five T2 plus nine T3 staged-RED IDs fail while the two B0-owned IDs are unignored and pass and the default workspace suite passes, then run `bun scripts/check-hanonly-production-policy.ts --b0-source-gate-anti-fixture`, calibration-freeze, the same required check again before holdout model load, and fresh disjoint holdout. Validate the sole external `crop-policy-selection.json` including both exact preimage-derived hashes, frozen recall-parameter evidence, B0 source-removal preflight / pre-render coverage evidence, both required-check executions, mandatory `c04` pass evidence, and all fresh holdout pass evidence, then freeze its descriptor-derived SHA-256. The artifact binds exact `B0_SHA` and is never rewritten, recalibrated, or reselected in Revision 50. If the fresh disjoint holdout clean/mask/oracle/hash assets are unavailable, G004 remains blocked and G005 cannot start.
- Return to the implementation branch, remove only the remaining five B1 marker attributes and prove their exact tests RED before implementing B1 to GREEN; consume only the frozen recall contract and accepted source-coordinate boxes/anchors. Remove only the nine GREEN-C marker attributes and prove their exact tests RED before implementing GREEN-C to GREEN. Finish every remaining local governance/closure input and commit a globally clean `IMPL_SHA`; create a detached globally clean acceptance worktree at that SHA. Before ledger rehydration or artifact access, prove all eight frozen paths exist as mode-`100644` regular blobs with identical endpoint OIDs and zero endpoint-tree diff, then run the final RED-state validator and full default workspace suite. Only then rehydrate and validate the immutable B0 artifact, frozen recall contract, and accepted source-coordinate boxes/anchors without invoking the selection filter, followed by T5, T6, the final production format/build/post-build-clean gate in that detached acceptance worktree, full audit, and visual review with the same owned mode-0700 evidence base outside all worktrees.
- Generate `C` in the clean implementation worktree at the same `IMPL_SHA`, allow only `C` dirty for immediate local `--ci-static`, commit only `C`, and create a detached globally clean PR worktree at `C_SHA` for local PR-static and authenticated T7.
- Completion requires byte-for-byte equality between the original before/after snapshots. Remove linked worktrees only without `--force` after evidence is checkpointed. `git reset`, `git clean`, `git checkout --`, forced worktree removal, and any original-worktree mutation are prohibited.

## Verification commands

The sole normative shell surface is `## Required command matrix` in `.omx/plans/test-spec-hanonly-visual-rendering-remediation.md`. It owns linked-worktree preparation assertions, D0 rehydration, B0/T5/T6, the final `IMPL_SHA` production format/build/post-build-clean gate in the detached acceptance worktree, trusted closure and corpus generation, the C-only commit, clean PR-worktree static verification, authenticated T7, and original dirty-worktree equality. This main plan must not duplicate, abbreviate, or provide an alternative executable T6/T7 block.

The document-consistency gate requires exactly one canonical T6 marker and one runtime-matrix validation invocation in the test specification, exactly two B0 selection-filter invocations in the pre-B1 B0 stage and zero at final `IMPL_SHA`, exactly two `--b0-source-gate-anti-fixture` invocations before B0 model load points, exactly one executable B0-to-IMPL frozen-interpreter byte gate, exactly one focused `han_only_source_color_work_budget` command in the final stage, and zero executable T6/T7 shell blocks in this main plan. It also rejects contradictory singular run-wide frozen-object wording, defines the normal-pipeline matrix only for `region.is_none()`, requires both pre-job HTTP and first-line direct-run region rejection with unchanged public field/type/serialization shapes plus truth-synced generated/localized behavior documentation, permits `pages.rs` as the sole region-bearing repair ingress only behind the Segment/exact-`[Inpainted]` pre-side-effect guard, requires exact `J -> T -> Wterm -> Wmeta` phase accounting, requires complete pre-inpaint translation/cluster/glyph/fill/nonempty-alpha evidence with glyph-zero `.notdef` rejection, requires checked whole-page sprite reservation and zero Renderer layout/raster calls, requires guarded detailed evidence and sanitized exported evidence to satisfy their distinct closed schemas, and requires the existing run-state staged test to cover two-page `Scope::WholeProject`, wrong/prior/post-run access, failure transition, payload release, no cross-page pixel accumulation, and complete drop without adding an ID. Execution must use the current Revision 50 test specification; Revision 46 text is inherited contract only where not explicitly superseded.

## Critical stop conditions

No staging guard may cross permit acquisition. The coherent pre-permit History-then-Scene staging snapshot is read-only and releases Scene then History before the permit-held publication boundary begins.

1. Protected Latin or non-target source pixels enter the final erase mask or any target sprite.
2. A dynamic layout region or sprite leaves page bounds, overlaps another owner, or depends on input order.
3. A resolved rectangle does not contain the full source anchor, its center leaves that anchor, or the final sprite center leaves that anchor.
4. Complete ink requires broad PP-DocLayout/bubble/card erasure rather than local component evidence.
5. Any blind source-ink mask is empty, nonbinary, wrong-sized, outside its edit ROI, or disagrees with its independent peer; `M_delta` is empty, fails to contain the agreed mask, or is derived after runtime output opens; either mask reaches residual math before its own `|X|>0` and `Dsrc_X>=0.08` are proven; the pinned OCR supplement is allowed to pass a failed pixel oracle; or any unsupported-rotation target reaches residual division instead of its equality/zero-erase oracle.
6. A transient HanOnly hint changes Scene, outlives one pipeline run, cannot restore its captured exact original after a rejected reflow, fails to bound `Csrc` by `G`, violates `S0<=Smax`/`G1>=G0`, or applies a font size outside the exact outcome table.
7. Planner provenance is ambiguous: raw external marker presence is checked only after deserialization, HTTP/MCP can write either value, generated/API docs imply external marker writes are accepted without an explicit runtime-rejection contract, direct apply bypasses the marker guard, Renderer reasserts trust, `true` exists without style, persisted style invalidation is absent from inverse/undo/redo state, untrusted import promotes marked Planner style, or current data contains irreducibly ambiguous false provenance.
8. Any failure from either builder class reaches erase, inpainter `engine.run`/`app.apply`, Inpainted/Rendered/blob/sprite persistence, published frozen state, or post-`B_prepare` Scene/History mutation; the deterministic/recoverable taxonomies drift; allocator OOM/abort is represented as recoverable; History publishes from anything except observed `Committed`, misclassifies `Unchanged`, treats `Indeterminate` as recoverable, accepts partial/mixed/unexpected bytes, task-temp recovery, guessed generation, or double apply; Blob/CAS bypasses `durable_put_exact`, claims Windows parent-directory fsync, makes unverified bytes reachable, or poisons before a History `Indeterminate`; any library-mediated canonical or success-publication path bypasses the one ProjectSession control, acquires the gate while holding History/Scene/Blob-cache/autosave/Registry/job/event locks, awaits under a permit, releases the permit before canonical/in-memory/response/Dirty/autosave/job publication, omits the under-gate poison recheck, fails to capture authoritative `expected_epoch` in one coherent pre-permit History-then-Scene staging snapshot, carries either staging guard across permit acquisition. Stop also when the permit-held boundary acquires History or reads/validates epoch before Blob promotion, performs anything other than exactly one permit-held post-Blob History acquisition and exactly one `current_epoch == expected_epoch` comparison, uses an atomic epoch mirror or an additional gate, or accepts a permit/store control-identity mismatch; a stale epoch after verified Blob promotion poisons, publishes Scene/History/success, or leaves anything reachable beyond exact verified unreachable CAS; the History adapter reacquires the gate or `Indeterminate` releases it before storing poison; a race test uses sleep/time instead of barriers/channels or fails either total ordering; `ProjectSession.scene/history/blobs` remains publicly write-capable, any of the exact 13 caller files lacks a gated/read-only migration, an unlisted live caller remains, or `ProjectSession::open_untrusted` can bypass the same control; `persistence_poisoned` can be cleared in process, any apply/undo/redo/snapshot/compact/autosave/page-import/mask-repair/pipeline write succeeds after poison, a success/Dirty/autosave/job event follows poison, close performs a write or fails to release autosave/lock, reopen skips strict recovery, read-only access exposes a write handle, or the fatal text differs; or `B_prepare` equality includes physical unreachable CAS instead of exact `reachable_blob_state`.

## Rollback strategy

- Keep GREEN-A, GREEN-B/B0, GREEN-B/B1, and GREEN-C as separately reviewable/revertible batches.
- B1 requires a passing B0 frozen-selection artifact; GREEN-C requires B1. Revert only the failing batch with a normal revert or inverse patch when no downstream dependency remains. If B1 fails after GREEN-C, revert GREEN-C first and then B1. If the defect invalidates B0 evidence, then also revert B0. Do not reset/clean the worktree.
- B0 rollback removes the three feature declarations/propagation entries, every gated observational item, the conditional llama-ext binding, and the app harness hunks together; default Cargo behavior is then byte-for-byte restored. Cargo ownership remains split between the model owner (`koharu-llm`/`koharu-ml`) and integration owner (`koharu-app`), with no shared Cargo file.
- B0 rollback owns Source Gate / PP-OCR recall-forward changes, including crop-local preprocessing, inverse mapping, coverage acceptance, and source-removal preflight. B1 rollback treats target layout, automatic font policy, shared decoder/cache, both import routes, UI allowlist/preview, and warning behavior as one compatibility batch; it excludes OCR scale, recall thresholds, crop-local preprocessing, inverse mapping, and Source Gate acceptance. Revert only the failing task-owned batch; content-addressed blobs written before a rejected final Scene Batch may remain unreachable, but no rollback deletes blobs or user pages.
- Before implementation, record `git diff` plus file hashes for every pre-existing dirty file. Roll back only hunks attributable to this plan, never whole dirty files.
- `support.rs` has the main GREEN-A mask hunk, one narrow GREEN-B/B1 unsupported-reason hunk, and one later bounded GREEN-C source-color extractor/reason hunk. The three inpaint engine files likewise have earlier mask hunks and later supported-NodeId handoffs. Ownership transfers only after each prior checkpoint; record per-phase diff/hash evidence and reverse only the failing hunk. GREEN-C preserves protected-source behavior through the original-translation path.
- GREEN-C rollback removes the HTTP pre-job and direct-run first-line region guards, the `pages.rs` Segment/exact-`[Inpainted]` pre-side-effect repair guard, page-keyed run store, current-page scope/accessor checks, sole-builder Arc/`PageId` handoff, translation/cluster/glyph transcript, glyph-zero/nonzero-out-of-range low-level errors, correlation-ID mapping, sanitized export projection, run-state drop checks, and repair-aware inpainter bypass together. It also reverts the matching route comments, four localized HTTP API references, and normally regenerated OpenAPI/Orval artifacts so documentation cannot describe behavior that is no longer enforced. It restores the prior pipeline/inpainter/renderer internals while retaining unchanged public region/engine/render/request type and serialization field shapes. The strengthened repair, complete-target-text, public-behavior-doc, and evidence-confidentiality regressions remain mandatory and therefore block acceptance until the whole GREEN-C batch is restored or the plan is revised.
- No Scene/OpenAPI persistence or generated-code migration is planned. Dependency rollback removes exactly the two app dev edges, one llm build-only edge, and their three lock-list additions; it requires no user-data migration.
- Preserve regression tests that demonstrate the original failure unless they assert behavior intentionally rolled back.
- Any change to a common or generator-lock input, trusted target record, workflow, CODEOWNERS, checker, checker test, ledger helper/test, build/config/toolchain input, or any other tracked non-`C` repository file after T6 invalidates old `R/A/C/G`; create a new clean `IMPL_SHA` and rerun closure, T6, full audit, and corpus generation. Only the explicitly excluded `C`, external evidence, remote Ruleset state, and external `G` may change after T6. A C-only regeneration may retain T6 only when generator/checker/tests/`R`/`A` are unchanged and the accepted sets reproduce exactly; regenerating only `C` after any closure-input change is forbidden.
- Governance rollback is ordered: first capture the active Ruleset, remove only `HanOnly Production Policy` from its required checks through an explicitly authorized remote change, capture the resulting Ruleset, and verify `main` is no longer waiting on that context. Only then may the workflow job be renamed/removed or its checker/corpus be deleted. CODEOWNERS protections remain unless a separately reviewed governance change removes them.

## ADR

### Decision

Revision 46 makes `source-color-contract-v2` admissible only through deterministic `source-color-work-budget-v1` with explicit P-1 node admission, closed P0 target terminalization/ranking, P1 pixel-preflight, E evaluation admission, and exact page aggregation. Read only `J=page.nodes.len()` before admission; reserve `J` before the sole node traversal derives `T`; extend to checked `J+T` before allocating target terminal records; and extend to checked `Wmeta=checked(J+T+choose2_checked(T))` before exact unordered canonical ranking. `choose2_checked` is total at `T=0/1`, divides the even operand before checked multiplication, and is tested through zero/one/two plus multiplication/addition boundaries. The source-contract preimage binds that evaluator, all six phase-specific terminal requested/reserved/consumed/target-record tuples, admitted accounting, and checked page evaluation/preflight/total sums. P1 is limited to ownership/domain construction, fixed histograms, and representatives under the exact conservative requested/reserved upper bound `4*A+19*X_i+81_920`; actual consumption is only independently counted real declared operations, may be lower than reserved, and cannot be padded or synthetically inflated. Direct-core/P95/order/background-witness/candidate work is E-only. E uses one shared immutable `bg(q)` table, complete same-bin nearest-rank P95, checked integer half-up alpha reconstruction, fail-closed cross-pair semantic ties, and one-complete-candidate semantics. The revised source-contract canonical preimage is exactly 5506 bytes with SHA-256 `13d2256fed7b8189e67db7222ce6ce7964f2745c977c42e7693679ffb2a341f8`; the constant-set preimage remains exactly 433 bytes with SHA-256 `ea277ff2674aae711b62a39b6a0b930e7d9c863bd518521c59ff44be56c4c6e9`. High-core, high-candidate, large-`U`, arithmetic-overflow, and page-aggregate inputs fail before candidate evaluation or erase with `ClassifierWorkBudgetExceeded`; every traversal/allocation is table-charged before execution, width uses one rectangular distance/interval proof rather than enumeration, charged execution cannot exceed its checked reservation, and wall-clock time is observability only.

Derive erase, layout, and strict source-color/width contracts privately at runtime in the existing HanOnly pipeline, preserve recognition/source-anchor geometry unchanged, and keep Planner output subordinate to local component/layout/raster/color validation. The authoritative layout contract is one private axis-aligned obstacle-free rectangle per supported target whose rectangle and final alpha-bbox centers remain inside the source anchor; page-level ownership masks are intermediate allocation data only. Rotation terminates before Source Gate and is not part of color-mode completeness. The authoritative automatic-style contract is one source-derived opaque solid fill plus zero or one source-derived opaque solid stroke with unique Chebyshev-topology `W_s` and checked round-half-up target `W_t`, validated before erase; an unrepresentable, ambiguous, shadow, or glow style is preserved and reported unsupported rather than approximated. Replace absolute C2, backend `-5px`, automatic font caps, and source-page OCR thresholds with geometry-relative backend crop/font rules plus crop-local model-input preprocessing. The backend exclusively computes `G/Csrc/S0/Smax/G0/G1` from crate-private HanOnly resolved-layout records; no automatic pixel value is exported to or reconstructed by the UI. Public `RenderBlockInput`, public `PageRenderOptions`, and public `Renderer::render_page(...)` remain unchanged. The sole builder completes HanOnly layout, shaping, glyph validation, fill rasterization, nonempty-alpha validation, and checked whole-page sprite reservation before inpaint, then freezes immutable sprite bytes plus transcript/placement in the private `PageId` object. The crate-private entry in existing `renderer.rs` only persists/composites those bytes once after `Inpainted`; AllText continues through the existing public path. HanOnly automatic UI without a manual size preserves the existing Auto/empty-numeric state, while manual sizing, AllText, and UI/backend PNG/JPEG/WebP allowlist parity remain unchanged. Canonicalize byte-sniffed PNG/JPEG/WebP once through bounded oriented RGBA8 decoding, byte-account decoded/cache/mask work, and apply page replacement only after all inputs validate in one remove+add Batch. The complete HanOnly Planner suggestion and source-color contract are fresh private run data, not Scene state. Every successful final image is independently replayed from actual persisted decoded Source/Inpainted-base/brush/ordered-sprite inputs through an independently classified protected restore and exact source-over composition. Full rotated rendering and richer gradient/multicolor/texture/nonopaque/shadow/glow style reproduction are deferred, using typed private reasons and the existing warning/`CompletedWithErrors` result boundary now. Keep trusted nine-source/Metal/360 acceptance outside ordinary PR CI. Bind trusted cells and `R/A/C` to one clean implementation checkpoint's whole-file common/generator-lock/trusted-target closure; require PR CI to match common/generator-lock inputs and reproduce its own Ubuntu target twice; explicitly checkout `candidate_head_sha`; authenticate the required check under the distinct test-merge `check_sha`; and make `G` bind both target records plus both identities and the notice annotation. `C` omits commit SHA and Ubuntu target data to avoid self-reference. The digest-only production-policy corpus remains enforced by one portable required static job and an active empty-bypass `main` Ruleset.

History durability is one private platform adapter, not a Unix-only invariant. Blob/CAS promotion has a separate Blob-local platform helper because an unreferenced immutable object must not share History poison semantics. Unix uses parent-directory sync; Windows uses handle release, canonical-file sync, and exact verification with no directory-fsync claim. One ProjectSession-owned `PersistenceControl` gives poison, every library-mediated canonical publication, and synchronous success publication a total order: an atomic fast reject is advisory, the mutex-backed permit and under-gate recheck are authoritative, Blob promotion precedes History then Scene locks, no `.await` occurs under the permit, and History `Indeterminate` stores poison before permit release. The session remains read-only for diagnosis/export until explicit no-write close, and only strict-recovery reopen clears poison by constructing a new session. Public ProjectSession write-capable fields become private and all direct callers migrate to read-only/gated methods; unchanged BlobStore public put signatures auto-gate, with crate-private permit-aware variants for an outer transaction. This deliberate downstream-breaking ProjectSession field removal is chosen over changing the frozen public Engine/Renderer/request/Scene contracts. HanOnly backend acceptance is equally closed at both RPC and direct ingress: explicit CPU is portable, requested Metal is macOS-only with actual-Metal evidence, and Windows/Linux/other non-CPU skips Registry preflight but creates one job whose direct run emits the stable unsupported result. The doc-hidden admission helper is explicitly additive downstream-public because `pipeline` is public. The sole required context transitively requires the real Windows History/Blob contract.

The final-size and complete-target-text chronology are part of that decision. Existing artifact edges conditionally order the selected font producer before Typography and inpaint, Typography before inpaint, and the selected inpainter before Renderer; absent optional producers create no edge, while ambiguous multiple producers fail before engine load. Normal HanOnly `pipeline::run` is exactly `spec.options.region.is_none()`; the total matrix is authoritative per page only in that domain: no Renderer/no inpainter is valid with zero builder/reservation/raster/publication; no Renderer/one inpainter is valid with exactly one complete `PageId` frozen-sprite publication immediately before that inpainter; Renderer/no inpainter rejects before engine load with zero publication; Renderer/one inpainter requires that same object and sprite identity to be consumed by both engines. HTTP `/pipelines` rejects a region before job creation, and direct `pipeline::run` rejects it before scheduling/run-state creation; public field/type/serialization/conversion shapes remain unchanged, while response comments, generated OpenAPI/Orval behavior documentation, and all four localized HTTP API references state the stable 400 and point to the direct repair route. The sole allowed region-bearing repair ingress is the explicit direct `pages.rs` route only when a pre-side-effect guard proves URL role `MaskRole::Segment`, successful registry resolution, and exact descriptor output `[Artifact::Inpainted]`; every other ID/role/output class returns the stable HTTP 400 before body/blob/Scene/engine/apply, while accepted repair stays outside the matrix with zero builder/publication/accessor. The exact public `EngineCtx` field set, crate-root re-export, and by-value `Engine::run(&self, EngineCtx<'_>)` signature remain unchanged. For accepted runs, `pipeline/mod.rs` owns one crate-private `tokio::task_local!` key, creates one `Arc<PipelineRunState>` per pipeline invocation, stores immutable objects by `PageId`, and scopes each related awaited engine future with that Arc plus the current page; the sole crate-private accessor uses non-panicking `try_with` and rejects outside/wrong/prior/post-run scope.

On each qualifying one-inpainter page, crate-private `prepare_and_freeze_hanonly_render_inputs(...)` receives that same Arc and `PageId` directly. Immediately after all selected upstream producer commits and before the call, capture `B_prepare`; against that exact page snapshot the builder completes final layout/font/effects, per-target fit, Planner outcome, group-min result, checked integer page-pixel `F_t`, checked `W_t`, source-color mode, expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry translation identity, cluster/control coverage, glyph-zero/nonzero-range validation, exactly one fill raster traversal per successful target, nonempty alpha, final safety, and the immutable sprite bytes/placement. It checked-reserves the complete retained logical sprite set under the page RGBA cap, then independently checked-computes actual 2-4x raster dimensions/bytes per target, constructs the existing fallible surface, rasterizes targets serially, and releases each target's supersampled/copy/downsample scratch before the next begins. It atomically publishes only after every target and both retained/transient checks pass. A translation-identity mismatch, final-layout failure after local fallback, cluster/control-coverage failure, glyph-validity failure, fill-traversal failure, or nonempty-alpha validation failure is a deterministic hard pre-write `geometry_or_font` failure. Only checked logical/actual-raster arithmetic or `usize` conversion failure, retained owner-rectangle/page-cap reservation failure, or fallible raster-surface-construction failure is a recoverable `geometry_or_font` failure. Either class produces the existing `geometry_or_font` builder error with zero inpainter run/apply, publication, erase, or downstream persistence and exact final equality to `B_prepare`, without rollback of prior producer commits. Process allocator OOM/abort is outside this recovery promise. The selected inpainter consumes its supported/unsupported NodeIds. A selected Renderer retrieves the same run-state Arc/current-page object after `Inpainted` and persists/composites those exact frozen sprite bytes once. It performs zero layout, shaping, glyph validation, fill/stroke raster, fit, regroup, reselect, round, clamp, or width derivation. Low-level `render_line` still returns an error instead of silently skipping an unrepresentable glyph as defense in depth in the shared builder raster path. Pixel payloads release immediately after successful composite, after no-Renderer completion, or after inpainter failure with zero sprite persistence; transient raster scratch releases per target on success or either builder failure class and never accumulates. A failed page cannot expose its record to the next page; concurrent invocations use distinct Arcs, nested same-task scopes restore the outer Arc, spawned tasks do not inherit the carrier, no state-derived mutable handle may escape its awaited scope, and all remaining records drop with the run Arc. Before any target text is read or hashed, each validated manifest target receives a run-random 128-bit correlation ID; guarded evidence retains the mapping and complete digests/counts, while exported evidence is a closed sanitized projection of correlation-ID-keyed non-length verdicts.

Execution chronology is part of the decision: clean pre-B1 `B0_SHA` produces the sole immutable selection artifact; B1 consumes only its frozen recall contract and accepted source-coordinate boxes/anchors; final clean `IMPL_SHA` contains all production and local-governance closure inputs and validates that artifact without reselection before T5/T6. After T6, only excluded `C`, external evidence, remote Ruleset state, and external `G` may change.

### Drivers

- Preserve causal, production-equivalent evidence from pre-B1 B0 through final `R/A/C/G`, the candidate head, and the required-check test-merge commit without self-reference.
- Make source removal, target geometry, automatic style/width, and final composition deterministic and machine-verifiable across independent production inputs.
- Deliver the smallest shared-boundary correction while preserving dirty-worktree state, manual sizing, AllText, format parity, and the explicit dependency/schema/renderer exceptions.

### Alternatives considered

- backend-local mask growth and renderer retry loops;
- retaining absolute C2/`-5px` and adding fixture-specific exceptions;
- retaining `12..28`/`72px` font caps, page-dimension OCR thresholds, broad backend decoding, count-only cache capacity, or clear-before-decode replacement;
- persisted recognition/erase/layout Scene fields;
- persisting HanOnly Planner reflow/style behind the existing single boolean marker;
- trusting predicted fill/stroke as final color, retaining default black/contrasting stroke, or approximating gradients/multicolor/textures/shadows/glows as a solid style;
- trusting predicted/default stroke width or human visual judgment as width authority;
- supporting fill-only automatic while classifying every automatic stroke/shadow/glow as unsupported; this is the strongest lower-risk alternative because it deletes the width oracle, but it would evade the required independent topology-unique supported-stroke holdout rather than satisfy source-style fidelity;
- storing the final commit SHA in `C`, which would create a post-T6 generation/self-reference cycle;
- rerunning B0 selection at final `IMPL_SHA`, which would let already-implemented B1 authorize itself;
- allowing checker/ledger or their package-resolution bytes to change after B0, which would let final code reinterpret an old authorization artifact;
- delaying all T2/T3 RED tests until their GREEN phases, which would leave B0 without a frozen behavior contract;
- removing the pre-B0 workspace suite or broadly excluding a crate/module, which would trade away baseline health instead of using exact temporary test-ID markers;
- staging workflow, CODEOWNERS, checker, or other `production_common` inputs after T6 while treating them as excluded;
- relying on default merge-commit checkout only, which would not prove the candidate-head closure;
- querying the required check by PR head, which conflicts with `pull_request` test-merge check ownership;
- treating nonempty sprite plus final node existence as proof of either complete target-text rasterization or final composition without digest/cluster/glyph-fill and exact-RGBA replay evidence;
- full polygon-aware rotated rendering in the same correction;
- full renderer rewrite or new visual scoring/model layer;
- running the model-, image-, and Metal-dependent 360-cell acceptance directly in ordinary Ubuntu PR CI;
- adding a second workflow, package script, dependency, external service, or separate policy repository for this bounded gate.

### Why chosen

The selected option reuses the current mask expansion, per-node source ink, crop helper, bubble/occupancy masks, `ResolvedLayoutBox`, local fitter, separate stroke/fill raster passes, group reducer, persisted blob references, BlobStore, `Op::Batch`, unsupported collection, `EngineCtx::warn`, RPC status mapping, `fontPrediction`, existing persisted-style marker, existing `FontPredictions`/`TypographyStyles`/`Inpainted` artifacts, and production-closure inventory. Two entry-boundary guards reserve region-bearing execution for the existing atomic page repair route: route-only validation would leave non-RPC callers unsafe, while run-only validation would create observable job state before rejection. The repair route itself additionally validates Segment role plus exact Inpainted-only descriptor before any side effect, closing the otherwise arbitrary pipeline-string dispatch without changing its request schema. For accepted `region.is_none()` runs, one private Tokio task-local `Arc<PipelineRunState>` per run, immutable objects keyed by `PageId`, and one sole builder per qualifying one-inpainter page give Typography, inpaint, and an optional Renderer the same run-local HanOnly typography, complete target translation, source-authoritative color/width data, checked sprite budget, and exact final sprite bytes without overloading Scene provenance or adding a public carrier; no-inpainter pages allocate no frozen object and publish none. The guarded direct repair branch does not need this data and stays on its direct inpainter path without touching the accessor. The existing public region field/type/serialization/conversion shapes, `EngineCtx`, `Engine::run`, render inputs, and public `render_page` remain unchanged; generated and localized documentation changes because behavior changed. The sole builder invokes the shared layout/raster path once per successful target before inpaint, and a crate-private HanOnly Renderer entry only persists/composites those frozen bytes once. The same frozen object/sprite identity, zero Renderer raster count, and payload release on success/failure/no-Renderer paths close both the post-erase missing-glyph hole and cross-page memory accumulation risk. AllText remains on the public path. Guarded digest/cluster/glyph/fill/alpha transcripts close the gap between a nonempty sprite and proof that the complete translation was rasterized; glyph-zero `.notdef` and nonzero conversion failures become hard pre-write errors instead of silent success. A guarded-detail/sanitized-export split preserves strict proof without leaking dictionary-testable text evidence into committed or remote artifacts. Keeping automatic pixel authority backend-only avoids a second geometry implementation or exposing private runtime layout; the UI needs no new contract because its existing Auto/empty state already represents an unknown runtime-resolved size. A test-only independent compositor reuses persisted Source/base/brush/sprites and independently derives protected support rather than adding a renderer. The fill-only alternative is simpler, but it is not selected because the strict target requires at least one independent topology-unique supported stroke to render rather than be relabeled unsupported. Conservative whole-file common hashing plus target-independent generator locks and same-target repeated generation proves shared-source equivalence without pretending macOS and Ubuntu binding bytes are identical or putting commit SHA into `C`. It fixes each responsibility once, preserves spatial correspondence and failed-replace state, removes the audited absolute/guessed production policies, uses only conditional edges over existing artifacts, and avoids a new `Artifact` variant, public contract, runtime dependency, learned scorer, or second rendering path.

### Consequences

- Region remains present and type/serialization-compatible on public pipeline request/options types but is operationally reserved for the existing atomic page repair route. HTTP `/pipelines` returns one pre-job 400 and direct non-RPC `pipeline::run` returns one pre-scheduling error; neither creates job/warning/completion/run-state/mutation evidence. The repair route accepts only a URL role of `MaskRole::Segment` whose resolved descriptor produces exactly `[Artifact::Inpainted]`; rejected IDs return one stable pre-side-effect HTTP 400. `PutMaskParams.pipeline` shape remains unchanged, while source comments, normal generated OpenAPI/Orval behavior docs, and all four localized HTTP API references are updated and tested.
- Crate-private HanOnly records/frozen state carry transient geometry and fallback context through one task-local Arc per accepted `region.is_none()` run and immutable objects keyed by `PageId`, but public `EngineCtx`, public `Engine::run`, public `RenderBlockInput`, public `PageRenderOptions`, public `Renderer::render_page(...)`, Scene, and API field shapes remain unchanged. The accessor is intentionally unavailable outside each awaited current-page engine scope, rejects wrong/prior/post-run pages, is distinct across concurrent runs, is shadowed/restored for nested same-task runs, and is not inherited by `tokio::spawn`; the sole builder's direct Arc/`PageId` handoff is the only private exception. Carrier-dependent work therefore stays directly awaited, cannot export a mutable state handle, and all records drop at run completion. Direct direct repair remains outside the matrix and accessor. Existing generated schema shapes may remain permissive for full Scene/patch serialization while HTTP/MCP external apply is runtime-restrictive for marker presence. The resolved layout stored in the private record is the single authoritative fit/validation rectangle; existing public `transform` and `source_transform` retain their current semantics.
- Dynamic region resolution adds one bounded page-level geometry pass.
- Source Gate crop context and backend automatic font ceilings scale from runtime geometry. Candidate ratios are predeclared, selection uses four calibration hashes, the chosen ratio is frozen before four disjoint holdout hashes, and any holdout failure forces a new plan revision. Backend removes blanket deductions and fixed automatic pixel caps; UI retires HanOnly automatic pixel estimation and shows Auto/empty numeric state instead.
- PP-OCR preprocessing becomes crop-local and model-contract-bounded; resizing a page may change the integer model upscale only at declared crop/model boundaries, while inverse-mapped normalized boxes and recall must remain stable across below/exact/above neighbors.
- UI and backend expose one PNG/JPEG/WebP contract. Import/reload share oriented RGBA8 decoding, model alpha composition is explicit, decoded memory/cache/mask allocation is bounded by checked bytes, and failed replacement is Scene/History atomic. Unsupported compiled formats now fail explicitly.
- HanOnly Planner acceptance remains intentionally narrow: insertion-only breaks on originally single-line text, exact reconstruction before local fit, bounded per-field outcomes, and a style/font preference that lives only for the current run. `G` bounds both paths, `S0<=Smax` is total, and `G1>=G0` proves group-min cannot fall below its no-Planner baseline.
- Successful HanOnly targets now require the sole builder to finish guarded complete-text and sprite proof before inpaint: exact expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry digests or audited-newline reconstruction, complete cluster/control byte coverage, glyph-zero `.notdef` rejection, nonzero `u16` representability, zero missing glyphs, one fill visit per shaped glyph, nonempty alpha, checked complete-page retained logical-sprite reservation, independently checked actual-scale raster dimensions/bytes, serial one-target scratch lifetime, and immutable sprite identity. Builder failure atomicity is relative to `B_prepare`, captured after upstream producer commits and immediately before the builder; those commits remain intact. Renderer must receive the same frozen identity and perform zero layout/raster calls. Process allocator OOM/abort is not claimed as a recoverable verdict. Only guarded mode-0600 reports retain text-derived digests/counts. Exported `R/A` carry random correlation-ID-keyed non-length verdicts; `C`, workflow annotation, closure summaries, and `G` carry no target text proof fields, and all exported artifacts pass the forbidden-field/value scan.
- Rotated text is visibly and measurably unsupported in this revision. Typed classification runs before Source Gate removal, warning dedupe is separate from tracing dedupe, one exact-prefix warning reaches the existing sink per rotated node, RPC reports `CompletedWithErrors`, and no rendered block/sprite is produced.
- Automatic source color is also fail closed before inpainting. Manual overrides remain explicit and exempt; representable solid styles render with exact source-contract RGBA; every ambiguous or richer style keeps a byte-identical ROI, has zero erase/block/sprite pixels, emits one typed warning, and reports `CompletedWithErrors`.
- Automatic source-color/stroke classification has deterministic checked target and page reservations. Valid complexity rejection preserves the ROI and returns the same unsupported boundary; accounting drift aborts the page as an invariant failure before mutation.
- Supported stroke width is source-topology-derived and deterministic; prediction width remains diagnostic. Zero/multiple source-width candidates fail closed, and the required topology-unique stroked holdout prevents “all strokes unsupported” from satisfying the plan.
- Independent page-sized source-ink masks, deterministic ROI matching, protected pixel equality, alpha/locality/size/color/width checks, exact final RGBA recomposition, and the frozen selection schema make calibration/holdout machine-decidable. Human review remains responsible only for inpaint texture quality.
- Existing persisted Planner style invalidation becomes inverse-complete and untrusted imports remove marked Planner style rather than promoting it to false/manual.
- Human visual review remains necessary for inpaint texture only. Color/style support is machine-decided by the frozen source-color contract and unsupported-style table; human review cannot waive a failed color oracle.
- Ordinary PR CI remains portable and deterministic: it independently recomputes whole-file common/generator-lock inputs, reproduces its Ubuntu target twice, verifies digest-only `C`, explicitly checks out `candidate_head_sha`, and emits one native notice annotation containing both `candidate_head_sha` and `check_sha` without a write token. The trusted implementation environment remains responsible for generating `R`, `A`, then `C`; final `G` binds both identities, both target records, trusted artifacts, and remote evidence without a reverse hash edge. Main-push execution collapses candidate, check, `GITHUB_SHA`, and checked-out `HEAD` to the pushed commit.
- Enabling the required context is an explicit authenticated repository-administration action. Completion requires API snapshots proving the PR candidate head, current test-merge commit, at least two write-capable exact owners with one non-author candidate, the test-merge check, and the active Ruleset; without that authority, the plan remains blocked. The empty bypass list and one required approval prevent normal merge bypass, but the ultimate repository settings owner can still alter governance.

### Follow-ups

- Run one regression, four calibration, and four disjoint holdout full-page images with every T5 coverage axis on CPU and actual Metal before production sign-off.
- Create a separate reviewed revision before claiming HanOnly success on CUDA, Vulkan, or any non-CPU/non-Metal backend; add same-instance device evidence and a production runtime matrix rather than weakening the explicit-CPU fallback.
- Create a separate RALPLAN for polygon-aware rotated erase/layout/render support if product requirements require rotated-text translation rather than explicit unsupported reporting.
- Create a separate RALPLAN for richer gradient, multicolor, texture, nonopaque, shadow, or glow extraction/rendering if product requirements require translating those styles rather than explicit unsupported reporting.
- If profiling later shows the page-level ownership pass is material, use `$performance-goal` with captured benchmarks; do not optimize speculatively in this correctness fix.
- If future production coverage requires a larger classifier budget, open a new reviewed plan revision with new constants, accounting proof, stress evidence, and contract hashes; do not tune limits from accepted images or elapsed acceptance time.
- If protection against repository-admin policy edits becomes a requirement, create a separate governance plan for organization-owned reusable workflows/rulesets; do not smuggle that broader authority model into this remediation.

## Available agent types and execution guidance

Durable consensus state: `.omx/state/ralplan-hanonly-visual-rendering-remediation.json`. It records amended Revision 46 as complete historical consensus. Revision 51 is the current operative G004/B0 amendment; its exact machine contract and test specification govern encrypted one-shot custody, while Revision 50 is immutable historical evidence. Every Review 50-54 accepted/frozen boundary, all four frozen canonical JSON byte lengths/hashes, all sixteen staged IDs, all three work-budget IDs, the exact 13-file closure and `open_untrusted` ownership, dependencies, renderer count, 360 matrix, regression-only `test.jpeg`, and required-check topology remain unchanged except where the Revision 51 authority statement explicitly supersedes R50 B0 execution/custody fields. Runtime, visual, real-PR, Ruleset, owner, and authenticated remote evidence remain implementation completion gates.

Available installed roles: `analyst`, `architect`, `code-reviewer`, `code-simplifier`, `critic`, `debugger`, `dependency-expert`, `designer`, `executor`, `explore`, `git-master`, `planner`, `prometheus-strict-metis`, `prometheus-strict-momus`, `prometheus-strict-oracle`, `researcher`, `scholastic`, `team-executor`, `test-engineer`, `verifier`, `vision`, `writer`.

Recommended durable path: `$ultragoal` owns the sequential ledger and gates the user's exact order: D0 -> D1 -> D2 -> D3 -> RED-A -> RED-B -> GREEN-A -> GREEN-B/B0 -> GREEN-B/B1 -> GREEN-C -> final acceptance -> required-check governance gate. Suggested leader reasoning: high.

Recommended Team + Ultragoal staffing:

- `executor` medium: erase/model-evidence lane owns `ctd_segment.rs`, `support.rs`, three inpaint engine files, `koharu-ml/src/aot_inpainting/mod.rs`, conditional `mask.rs`, every model-side B0 hunk in `koharu-llm/Cargo.toml`, `koharu-llm/src/paddleocr_vl.rs`, `koharu-llm/build.rs`, `koharu-llm/src/safe/model.rs`, `koharu-ml/Cargo.toml`, and the evidence plus B0 production crop-local preprocessing/inverse-mapping hunks in `koharu-ml/src/pp_ocr_v5.rs`; it also owns the exact llm build-only `sha2`, `rust-toolchain.toml`, `.cargo/config.toml` archive digest, and build-script verification as one checkpoint. It applies the main GREEN-A support hunk and the narrow GREEN-B/B1 unsupported-reason hunk, then checkpoints and transfers `support.rs` ownership before GREEN-C. B0 is applied before any B1 production behavior; the integration owner owns app propagation/harness consumption, the B0 Source Gate detection/coverage acceptance hunks in `source_language_gate.rs`, and the coordinated `Cargo.lock` result before `B0_SHA`. After the integration owner exposes the private current-page task-local accessor and sole builder in GREEN-C, this same erase/AOT owner adds only the direct `pages.rs` repair-path `region.is_some()` bypass before accessor use, narrow supported-NodeId handoffs in all three inpaint files, and the AOT accessor/device-label evidence hunks. No region-bearing `pipeline::run` may reach this bypass.
- `executor` medium: Planner/provenance lane owns only the non-persistence typography/provenance surfaces `typography.rs`, `pipeline/engines/typography.rs`, `koharu-rpc/src/routes/history.rs`, and `koharu-rpc/src/mcp/mod.rs`; it may not edit, co-own, review-sign, or checkpoint `session.rs`, `app.rs`, `autosave.rs`, `blobs.rs`, any persistence-control/permit/direct-caller migration, `koharu-core/src/op.rs`, `koharu-app/src/history.rs`, `support.rs`, `pipeline/mod.rs` task-local/transaction state, or `pipeline/engine.rs` public contracts.
- `team-executor` medium: integration/source-policy lane exclusively owns every production and test hunk in `koharu-app/Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `source_language_gate.rs`, `pipeline/engine.rs`, `pipeline/mod.rs`, `blobs.rs`, whole-file task ownership for `session.rs`, `app.rs`, and `autosave.rs`, every `PersistenceControl`/permit/accessor/field-encapsulation/direct-caller migration and race test, both app renderer files, cluster/run alpha and fallible buffer ownership in `koharu-renderer/src/renderer.rs`, GREEN-B Batch staging in `koharu-core/src/op.rs`, whole-log copy-on-write History commit in `koharu-app/src/history.rs`, apply/undo/redo/snapshot/compact/autosave/page-import/mask/pipeline fault tests, replacement/import integration, GREEN-C reuse, `koharu-rpc/src/routes/pipelines.rs`, `koharu-rpc/src/routes/pages.rs`, the four localized `docs/*/reference/http-api.md` files, normal `ui/openapi.json`/`ui/lib/api/generated.ts`/`ui/lib/api/schemas/` regeneration, `ui/lib/io/openFiles.ts`, `RenderControlsPanel.tsx`, focused decoder/cache/import/UI tests, OpenAPI snapshot/generated tests, and focused RPC status tests. 1. Ultragoal opens only the current phase and records its focused evidence. No worker may start D1/D2/D3, RED-A/RED-B, GREEN-A, GREEN-B/B0, GREEN-B/B1, GREEN-C, or final acceptance until the preceding named checkpoint passes; B1 requires the validated B0 frozen artifact.
2. Each owner runs its focused RED/GREEN filters and records exact exit codes before requesting the next gate.
3. Integration owner runs the combined HanOnly pipeline plus decoder/cache/import/UI contract tests, the existing `http_options_inherits_source_text_policy` route test, the focused `hanonly_pre_greenc_red_t3_run_state_lifetime_contract`, and the strengthened existing repair-route/source-color tests. 4. Verifier runs workspace check/test/clippy/fmt/UI/generated checks and `git diff --check` after GREEN-C.
5. Verifier validates one regression plus four calibration and four disjoint holdout hashes, source/mask/clean-reference provenance, frozen image-input/source-color/stroke-width contracts, exact color constants, format/orientation/alpha and byte-budget/atomic-replace evidence, raw Source Gate load/node/build evidence and recomputed metrics, `crop-policy-selection.json`, pre-render residual/color thresholds, every page/text/style/color-mode/format coverage axis, locality, exact source-contract RGBA/width/probes, expected/frozen/pre-inpaint-raster-entry translation identity or audited-newline reconstruction, complete cluster/control byte coverage, glyph-zero/nonzero-range/missing/fill-visited/nonempty-alpha verdicts, retained logical sprite bytes/page cap, actual raster scale/dimensions/surface bytes, one-live-scratch and per-target release verdicts, `B_prepare` equality plus retained upstream commits on injected failures from both builder classes, same frozen sprite at Renderer entry, zero Renderer raster, payload release, actual persisted-input final RGBA recomposition and per-target omission, explicit rotation and source-color warnings/`CompletedWithErrors`, CPU/actual-Metal execution, all 360 cells, repeat/restart stability, mode-0600/mode-0700 guarded containment, correlation-ID uniqueness/stability/nondeterminism, sanitized-export forbidden-field/value scans, and absence of any process-level allocator OOM/abort recovery claim, or records exact unavailable prerequisites.
6. Verifier runs the all-production-root policy audit and requires zero violations, zero forbidden automatic-color fallbacks, exact color-contract/constants, plus only the explicit D0 test allowlist.
7. Before `B0_SHA`, test engineer finishes the checker, checker tests, ledger, ledger tests, exact sixteen-ID staged-RED inventory, and every B0-sensitive validator input; verifier proves the eight-path endpoint set is complete and the B0 default suite skips only those exact marked IDs. Git-master finishes workflow and CODEOWNERS before final `IMPL_SHA/T6`. At final, verifier runs the frozen-interpreter byte gate and final RED-state gate before any ledger rehydration or B0 artifact read, then proves all remaining tracked inputs are present in the clean T6 closure. Only after accepted `R` and `A` may test engineer generate `C` in the clean implementation worktree; verifier proves neither prior artifact references `C`, proves the trusted closure fields agree, confirms only `C` is dirty, reruns immediate static checks, and hands a C-only commit to git-master.
8. The clean PR worktree and required job independently reproduce the Ubuntu target twice, explicitly checkout the candidate head, and emit one notice annotation binding `candidate_head_sha` plus the distinct test-merge `check_sha`. Only after that job succeeds may an explicitly authorized repository administrator activate the empty-bypass `main` Ruleset. Verifier proves `local HEAD == PR head.sha == annotation.candidate_head_sha` and separately `PR merge_commit_sha == check_run.head_sha == annotation.check_sha`, validates closure/target fields, captures authenticated Ruleset/check-run/check-annotation/PR/collaborator API JSON, proves independent owner eligibility, and creates `G`; unavailable authority, closure drift, stale test merge, target nondeterminism, or evidence blocks completion.
9. Team returns changed-file ownership, original-worktree equality, command evidence, manifest/schema/hash/role/coverage/residual/color/width evidence, exact P-1/P0 phase tuples, repair role/descriptor negative-class results, guarded complete translation/cluster/glyph-fill digest-count-verdict evidence, sanitized `R/A` correlation-ID verdict evidence, `C/G` no-target-proof scan results, exact composite/omission evidence, exact color modes/contracts/probes/unsupported results, common/generator-lock/trusted-target/PR-target closure hashes, `R/A/C/G` hashes, persistent external evidence-root paths, candidate-head/test-merge check/PR/collaborator/Ruleset snapshots, texture-review gaps, and rollback DAG. Ultragoal checkpoints that evidence as durable completion state.

`$ralph` is an explicit fallback only if the user intentionally selects one persistent single-owner edit/test/fix loop. It is not the default durable path.

## Goal-Mode Follow-up Suggestions

- `$ultragoal`: recommended default for durable implementation and acceptance tracking.
- `$team` + `$ultragoal`: recommended for the three disjoint production lanes plus one durable ledger.
- `$ralph`: explicit single-owner persistence fallback only.
- `$performance-goal`: only after correctness if the new page-level geometry pass shows measurable regression.
- `$autoresearch-goal`: not applicable; this is an implementation/verification task, not a research deliverable.
